Still refused as for a taker, by `walletPaymentFor` and the records: any warning of the state machine (`BondAmountOutsideOrder` from `MakerTerms.bondAgrees`, `InvoiceAmountMismatch`, `TradeTermsChanged`, `InvoiceExpiringSoon`…), an invoice past `payableUntil` less `TradeTolerances.walletPaymentMargin`, an invoice other than the one quoted, and a payment hash another row of `trade_payments` holds (`trade_payments_hash_once`, any trade, step or contract: `BondPayNotRecorded`, the wallet never asked; `maker_bond_from_wallet_test.dart`). **The owner's payment rule** (§ 3.3): once the quote is given and before the confirmation shows, `rateOfBond(record, quote)` compares the quote's amount with the bond the order implies at the wallet's own price of a bitcoin read then (`WalletRateCheck.ofMakerBond`, `domain/trade/wallet_rate_check.dart`; `MakerTerms.bondAtPrice`: the order's amount, a range's maximum, at that price with the order's premium, times the bond size, exactly); `null` for a take and for the escrow's step, so the taker's confirmation is unchanged and no price is asked at a take. One threshold, `TradeTolerances.walletRateGap` (10 %), on the side that costs only: more than 10 % above the exact bond expected, the confirmation shows a red line with both amounts (`p2pPayRateWarning`; since #250 the red alert's two lines, `p2pPayoutBreakdownAgainst` and `p2pPayAgainstFigures`), and the user may still pay; within it, or a favourable gap of any size, the line of the price, the bond expected and the gap (hundredths of a percent rounded away from zero, so a gap shown above 10.00 % always warns). Never the coordinator's price of the creation for a currency the wallet prices (a price announced ten times too low makes a bond ten times too large agree with `bondAgrees`: the rule warns of it, security review of #179). `WalletRateCheck` is sealed in three cases: `WalletRateAtWallet` (the wallet's price), `WalletRateNotPriced` (a currency the wallet does not price, outside `SupportedCurrency`: no figure at all, one line in the warning colour, `p2pPayRateNotPriced`, « The Bull wallet has no price for {currency}: rate not checked. You are trusting {coordinator}'s price: check it yourself before paying. » (since #250 the grey « not quoted » line alone, the owner's decision of 2026-11-24, the key removed), no « Retry », « Pay » still offered; the owner's decision of 2026-11-17 in the evening, #181, which replaced the comparison with the creation's price, `WalletRateAtCreation`, removed) and `WalletRateUnverified` (the wallet prices the currency but its price could not be read, or is outside the bounds of `MakerTerms.create`'s: nothing compared, no price cited, a line in the warning colour, « rate not checked » (since #250 « Amount not checked » in orange), and « Retry », `BondPaymentCubit.retryRate`, which reads the price again for the same quote, never a new quote; the owner's decision of 2026-11-17, after the functional review of #180, C1). The rule never refuses: `payBond` never reads the price. The wallet's price comes through the port, `P2pWalletPort.bitcoinRate(network, currency)` (`P2pWalletBitcoinRate`: `P2pWalletRateRead`, `P2pWalletRateNotPriced`, `P2pWalletRateUnavailable`, no text), which only `pay_bond_from_wallet_usecase.dart` and, since #181, the create form's `check_make_price_usecase.dart` name (`wallet_port_architecture_test.dart`); the shell implements it in `lib/features/p2p_wallet` with `ReadWalletRateUsecase` over `Pricer.getIndexPrice` of `packages/bull_pricer` (the price the wallet's home screen and Bitcoin Price feature read, its one-minute cache shared), the trade's network choosing the price service's host, a currency outside `SupportedCurrency` (USD, CAD, MXN, CRC, EUR, ARS, COP) not priced without a request, every failure unavailable, nothing logged; nothing new in `lib/core`. Privacy, accepted by the owner on 2026-11-17: unless cached, one HTTPS POST over clearnet, outside Tor, to Bull Bitcoin's public price API with the currency's code and the user's IP address, nothing of the trade; a currency other than the display currency tells which one the user is about to pay; the wait is the pricer's own bound (10 s, `packages/bull_pricer/lib/src/data/datasources/pricer_api_datasource.dart:16`) and the shell's above it (`ReadWalletRateUsecase.readBound`, 15 s, then « rate not checked »), and only an `Exception` of it is caught (rule 11); the request leaves just after the swap quote (which gives the swap service the bond's amount), before the confirmation, once more at each « Retry », so an operator seeing both services could tie the IP address, the amount and the currency together (an assumption, not established in the code). The comparison with the creation's price that a currency the wallet does not price had until #181 could in practice never warn at 0 (the bond payable is within 2 % of the same coordinator's estimate, `bondAgrees`, and a price ten times too low read « −0.01 % »): it compared the coordinator with itself, and the owner replaced it on 2026-11-17 in the evening with the line above, which cites no figure (security review of #180). ARS is priced as the others: the API's `indexPrice` is a market index, for ARS the market rate, within 0.5 % of yadio.io's (checked on 2026-11-17). The price in the line is written as the reference's « Order current rate », 5 significant digits, at most 3 decimals (`P2pFormats.orderRate`, `MakerForm.tsx:168`). The cubit (`BondPaymentCubit`, `rate:` given by the locator for the bond's cubit only) asks the comparison after the quote and gives it with `BondPayConfirming.rate`; the confirmation shows it (`PayConfirmationCard` of `pay_confirmation_card.dart` since #250, `_RateLines` before). The panel of 0 (`TradeContract`): « Lock {{amountSats}} Sats to PUBLISH order » with `bond_satoshis` and « (1/5) » (`TradeBox/index.tsx:379-389`, `stepXofY.ts`), the bond panel of a taker's 3 (`BondPanel`, now typed on the status and its bond invoice: « You are BUYING BTC », « Pay with this wallet » over « Pay with another wallet » with « See Compatible Wallets », the QR code, the copy and the reference's hold-invoice sentence word for word, #135), « Copy URL » and « Cancel » with the reference's `Dialogs/CancelOrder.tsx` (`CancelOrderDialog`, a free cancel, the project's line when a payment of the bond is on its way), the « Order » tab's countdown of the 300 s; the screen judges a cancel by `seen.status` (an order answered unchanged keeps the panel), never by the outcome's type, and says nothing of a bond returned. Texts of the project, marked so in the ARB files: the four lines of the rule (`p2pPayRateWallet`, `p2pPayRateNotPriced`, `p2pPayRateUnverified`, `p2pPayRateWarning`; since #250 only `p2pPayRateWallet` stays, in a bond's detail, the others replaced by the validated words), which name « the Bull wallet » (« le portefeuille Bull ») since the owner's decision of 2026-11-17 (#181), « Pay with this wallet » and the reference's texts unchanged; the French of the title, « Bloquez {amountSats} sats pour PUBLIER l’ordre ».
- **The create form (#181): the reference's basic form, reachable since #182 (below).** Design note `docs/design/phase-3-making-an-order.md` § 1.11, § 2.1, § 3.9. `lib/src/ui/create_order_screen.dart` (`CreateOrderScreen`), over `MakeOrderCubit` (`lib/src/presentation/make_order_cubit.dart`), built only by `P2pRouter.createPage` (the route `create` since #182, below; the testing seam's `createOrderPageForTesting` builds it for the app's captures; `trade_screen_architecture_test.dart` and `create_route_test.dart` pin both). The fields of `MakerForm.tsx` in its order and words: « Clear form » (no side, USD, the amount, the methods and the premium emptied, the coordinator kept), « Enable advanced options » (disabled with « Coming in a later version. » until #183, below; its defaults `MakerFormCheck.publicDurationDefault`, `escrowDurationDefault`, `bondSizeDefault`), « Buy or Sell Bitcoin? * » (« Sell » disabled with « Selling as maker is coming in a later version. » until #188, which made it a choice as « Buy »), « Amount * » and the currency (`FilterPickerSheet`, every currency but bitcoin), « Fiat Payment Method(s) * » (the book's `FilterSheet` over the reference's list, with no « any » row, and chips) with « Add custom payment method » (`AddPaymentMethodDialog`, without the reference's GitHub link), « Premium over Market (%) * », the coordinator (`SelectHostSheet` in the lottery's order, with the fee row of `OrderFeeRow`), the summary and « Estimated Bond: … Sats », « Create order » with its reason (a line under it and a tooltip), « Order current rate: … » (with a placeholder while the rate is not known); « Buy » is a choice, never a toggle; the methods' bound is the reference's 50 characters, said « Must be at most 50 characters ». When no coordinator takes a new robot: « No coordinator can be chosen right now. » with « Retry » (`MakeOrderCubit.retryHosts`). At « Create order », limits and price older than `MakeOrderCubit.staleAfter` (3 minutes) are read again first (`refreshIfStale`), and the press goes no further when the request or the price's warning changed. The rules are pure (`lib/src/domain/trade/maker_form.dart`): `FormNumber` reads a field once (a point or a comma, one separator, the Arabic-Indic, Persian, Devanagari and full-width digits and the full-width full stop and comma, nothing else: no `NaN`, exponent, inner space or sign `+`; at most 20 characters and the field's decimals, which an input formatter also keeps); `MakerFormCheck.of` gives the reference's limits (`MakerAmountLimits`, the make use case's own), the rate to 5 significant digits, the reference's estimate (`bondCalculator.ts`, at the rounded rate, at most about 0.005 % from `MakerTerms.expectedBond`), the reasons in the reference's order (`MakerFormBlock`; « The coordinator limits are not available. » said at once when they could not be read) and the request built from the values the summary shows (the amount shown with all its decimals, not the reference's 4 significant digits). `CustomPaymentMethod` keeps a typed method as others see it (the ideographic space made ordinary, ends trimmed, spaces single, `isShownAsWritten`). The default coordinator: every coordinator's `/api/info/` read anonymously, then `CoordinatorLottery` (draws `Random().nextDouble`, the middle in the testing seam), the first that takes first contacts; its `/api/limits/` read anonymously (`FetchCoordinatorLimitsUsecase`), again at each choice; an answer for a coordinator no longer chosen is dropped. The confirmation as the take's: « You do not have a Garage Key » without a key, else « Store your garage key », whose « Done » calls `MakeOrderCubit.make` with the request taken at the press, the fields locked from the press; `make` runs once at a time and never after `MakeMade` (the trade opened, `pushReplacementNamed` of the trade) or `MakeUncertain` (`MakePending`, `MakeNoLongerPending`: the form locked, « Bull Market does not know yet whether {coordinator} made your order. It keeps checking. », resolved by #182's line); a refusal or a make not made is said with a text per type (`make_order_l10n.dart`), never the coordinator's. **The price check (the owner's decision of 2026-11-17):** while an amount is typed and the chosen coordinator's limits give a price for the currency shown, `CheckMakePriceUsecase` (the only other reader of `P2pWalletPort.bitcoinRate`) compares that price, without the premium, with the Bull wallet's (`MakePriceCheck`): « For comparison, the Bull wallet's indicative price: … ({coordinator}: …, gap …). » under « Order current rate », and beyond `TradeTolerances.walletRateGap` either way (both ways is the coordinator of the work's choice) the line in red and a red warning (a price too low: a bond larger than it should be; too high: fewer sats at the take, both written for a buyer); a currency the Bull wallet does not price: one line in the warning colour, no figure, no « Retry »; a price that could not be read: « rate not checked » with « Retry »; « Create order » never blocked. The read leaves outside Tor before any robot, only while an amount is typed, at most once per currency every 3 minutes but at « Retry », never when the form opens nor for « Clear form », never in a loop (`P2pWalletPort.bitcoinRate` lists the moments and what the service learns); without the wallet's port, no line. **The price the order keeps is the one checked** (security review of #181, C1): the press hands `MakeOrderUsecase.execute` the line seen (`MakePriceSeen`), and the use case, after its own `/api/limits/` read and its terms, before any robot, refuses with `MakePriceCheckChanged` when the user would see another line at the price it read (always a price within 10 % of the one shown, a red line included, C3 of the second review; and the same verdict against the Bull wallet's price already compared; and the same currency); the form then shows the new limits and line, and a second press makes the order. At a press after 3 minutes the form stops when the request or the line's verdict changed, not only the red (C2); a press while the Bull wallet's price is being read waits for that read, then stops the same way (R8 of the second review). The form keeps the last price read of each currency for 3 minutes (R10). « Estimated Bond » is the reference's expected bond in sats; nothing more is shown of the bond. `MakeOrderRequest.tryCreate` of the package refuses since #181 a payment method or description that shows as empty or holds what others would not see as written (`isShownAsWritten`, `packages/robosats/lib/src/coordinator/visible_text.dart`: invisible and format characters, the tag block, spaces but U+0020, private use, U+FFFC, U+16FE4, the unassigned planes 4 to 13, combining marks of the scripts counted at the start or more than 6 after a character (pointed and cantillated Hebrew needs 5), a joiner between two Latin, Greek or Cyrillic letters or digits, joiners (a joiner may follow a character seen or its variation selector, as in emoji sequences, or come before the virama of its Indic cluster, as in the Bengali ra-phala), variation selectors and combining marks out of place), the form saying it before.
- **The maker's panels, the pending makes, and « Create » enabled (#182).** Design note `docs/design/phase-3-making-an-order.md` § 1.11, § 2.1, § 3.3, § 3.4, § 3.7. The create form is reached by the route `create` of `P2pRouter` (`P2pCreateRouteArgs.fromBook`: the side and first currency of the book's filter, the coordinators it could not reach, the orders it received), opened by « Create » of the tab while the network has no active trade nor pending make, and by « Create order » at the top of « Active trades » otherwise (design note § 7, proposal 9: « Order » then always opens that list, even for one trade); `create_route_test.dart` and `trade_screen_architecture_test.dart` pin who builds and opens it. The trade screen of an order made here (`TradeContract.maker`, from `TradeTrail.made`): « Your order is public » (`TradeTitle.orderPublic`, `Prompts/PublicWait.tsx` word for word, its deposit time from the order's escrow duration, then « Public orders for {{currencyCode}} » and « Premium rank » computed by `PublicWaitStanding` from the book the tab received when the trade was opened, `TradeScreen.book`, with the reference's quirks: every order of the currency counted, 0.5 when at most one order of the side; the pause button beside « Public orders for … », #184), « Your order is paused » with `Prompts/Paused.tsx`'s text and « Unpause Order » (#184), « (x/5) » for a maker (`TradeContract.stepOf`, `stepXofY.ts`), « Your maker bond is locked / unlocked / settled » (`BondStatus.tsx:39`), « Cancel » at once with no dialog at 1 and 2 (`TradeCancelOffer.immediate`, `CancelButton.tsx:30`, `:79-80`), the reference's `CancelOrder` dialog at 0 and `ConfirmCancel` with `BondLossAcknowledged` at 6 and 7 as a taker's; the « Order » tab gives the maker's side and the maker fee (`OrderDetails/index.tsx:193-196`); 17 and 18 follow the statuses' classes (`DisputeLost`, `DisputeWon`), so by side; an order back in the book is the public panel again with its bond line; a next contract has its own statuses and gap. The gap of § 3.3 (`RateGapLine`, the project's text) is said before the payout panel at 6 and 8, before the chat at 9 and in the dialog of « Confirm … sent » (`TradeDialogBody.lines`), in the warning colour when it is unfavourable to the maker's side, information only, and only when the owner's rule below shows no comparison (one figure for one question, decided by the coordinator of the work after the functional review); since #234 only to a maker buyer and only where that rule does not run on the page (no wallet's port): never beside « rate not checked », a currency the Bull wallet does not price, a price being read or a read with no sats, nor before the rule's first answer (one mechanism: no gap drawn while the screen is given the rule's `PayoutRateCubit`, `TradeScreen._gapLine` and `_dialogLines`; `PayoutRateLines.hidesGap` removed; the owner's decisions of 2026-11-17; security review of #188, C1), and never to a maker seller, at 9 included (`TradeScreen._gapOf` keeps a buyer's statuses only; functional review of #234, C1, R4). The reference's texts false for a maker get a project line under them: « This box will ring » (`p2pTradeNoRingYet`), the bond « back automatically » at 7 (`p2pTradeEscrowWaitMaker`: the order goes back to the book, its bond locked); « unpause it at any time » at 2 is true since #184, with no line. `ContractSeenLate` has its own words, in the text colour (`p2pTradeWarnContractSeenLate`). The screen judges a cancel by the status read, never by `CancelDone` nor `makerBondReturned`, and says nothing of a bond returned. **The pending makes**: `PendingMakesCubit` (one per screen that shows them: the tab, the create page) is the only caller of `MakeOrderUsecase.pendingMakes`, `resolve` and `dismiss`, and only `PendingMakeLines` asks it to resolve or set aside; each make is resolved once when « Active trades » or the form opens, when the app comes back while the list is shown, and on « Retry », never in a loop; « Set aside » appears once a resolution left a make pending and, after a dialog that says an order may still exist at the coordinator, unpaid, expiring on its own, closes it in the records (`MakeOrderUsecase.dismiss`, under the makes' lock, nothing sent, never made again, its robot never used again); a resolution that confirms the make opens its trade. The form makes nothing while a pending make of the network is listed or the records cannot tell (`PendingMakesState.blocksMaking`), the makes shown above it with their « Retry » and « Set aside »; while the make is sent, the page cannot be left (`PopScope`, the top bar's back), a line under « Create order » saying to wait once going back was tried, so that the user is always taken to the trade or to its pending make; once the network's pending makes are all set aside or found not made, the form becomes usable again without leaving (`MakeOrderCubit.pendingCleared`). A press during the Bull wallet's price read stops only when the line read warns, is not priced or could not be read (third functional pass of #181). The book opens an order this device made as its trade, never « Take Order » (`ActiveTradesListed.madeHere`). `isShownAsWritten` counts the viramas a joiner lets through with the marks of their character (third security pass of #181). **The owner's rule on the buyer's side** (design note § 2.1, § 3.3): `WalletRateCheck.ofMakerPayout` (the side `WalletRateSide.receives`: the warning when the sats received are fewer than 90 % of the contract's amount at the Bull wallet's price with the premium, `MakerTerms.satsAtPrice`, exactly). What is compared is `CheckPayoutRateUsecase.subjectAt` (pure): at 6 and 8 the invoice the payout panel asks (`PayoutAsk.buyerSats`), compared again at once when the coordinator changes it; nothing while the state machine raises an amount warning (`amountsDisagree`: its warning alone, no comparison, no gap); at 9, where the coordinator gives no `trade_satoshis`, the order's `satoshis_now` less the maker fee (`MakerTerms.payoutAt`), checked against the smallest invoice's sats asked at 6 or 8 for the contract, kept by `PayoutRateMemory` (a singleton of the locator, in memory: it outlives the trade's page, **not a restart**; #204, schema 9, will keep the invoice given): the smaller compared, both said when they differ (`p2pPayoutRateToldMoved`), and with nothing kept the line says it compares the coordinator's figure of now and asks the user to compare it with the invoice (`p2pPayoutRateToldNow`, in the text colour since #207: a restart between 8 and 9 is a normal case), or, for a contract seen late, that its invoice was never seen here (`p2pPayoutRateNotSeen`, alert, said too beside « rate not checked » or the amounts' warning when nothing is kept, `PayoutRateNoSats.notSeen`, `PayoutRateMismatch.notSeen`, third security pass of #182, R13, #207); the kept invoice is compared even beside an amount warning (`PayoutRateAskedOnly`, its line naming « the {amount} asked here » and « the payout invoice this app saw asked », #211), and « Confirm … sent » repeats that warning (`PayoutRateLines.dialogLinesOf`); a read with no sats says « rate not checked » (`PayoutRateSatsMissing`). `PayoutRateCubit` (only with the wallet's port, one per trade page) is the only caller of `CheckPayoutRateUsecase` (`termsOf`, `priceOf`, the third and last reader of `P2pWalletPort.bitcoinRate`, `wallet_port_architecture_test.dart`). The price is read only while the trade screen is shown (never hidden, never in the background), only for a step with something to compare, and only: with no price of the currency read in this run; when a step is shown for the first time, appears anew or moves to another status (8 after 6) with a read three minutes old (`PayoutRateCubit.freshFor`); at a press of « Submit » or « Confirm … sent » with a read three minutes old (`beforeAct`, the button busy, at most `readBound`, 15 s: a line that changes stops the press and is shown, a second press goes on, but a line that now says within the margin never stops it, #207); on « Retry » after a price not read. The watch's next read of the same status never reads it. `PayoutRateLines` says it before the payout panel and before the chat step, and the dialog of « Confirm … sent » builds its lines from the rule's state when it opens, after `beforeAct`, and again at each change while open (`LiveDialogLines`, never the lines of a build drawn during the read, « Reading… »: security review of #208, C1), its lines from the status the screen shows now, at each change of the rule or of `TradeCubit` (#209; security review of #219, C1, C2): first the alerts the screen shows (the state machine's warnings of that status, or of the status a wait waits on, with the advice for an order made here, or the message shown alone; `LiveDialogLines.alerts`), then the rule's lines for that status's step only and the gap line, never the rule's own « do not add up » in place of the cause; `PayoutRateCubit.show` clears its check when the step shown goes (it kept it before, `_step` being cleared first); the payout panel, `PayoutCubit`, `ChatStepPanel` and `TradeCubit` never name it (`trade_screen_architecture_test.dart`): they are given a check to await (`PayoutPanelRequest.beforeSubmit`, `ChatStepPanel.beforeConfirmFiatSent`), so it never blocks « Submit » nor « Confirm ». The request leaves outside Tor, as for the maker bond (accepted by the owner on 2026-11-17). Under every text that says a bond is returned (at 8 for a seller, the collateral too), a line says how a bond or collateral paid with this wallet comes back through the swap service, less its fees, after a delay, only as the trade's payment records tell (`WalletBondState.bondPaid` and `escrowPaid`, from `BondPaymentPanel.bondOf` and `paidHereOf`, functional review of #182, U2, #207): named when paid with this wallet (`p2pTradeBondPaidHereBack`, `p2pTradeEscrowPaidHereBack`, `p2pTradeBothPaidHereBack`), said as a condition when the records cannot tell (`p2pTradeBondBackThroughSwap`, `p2pTradeBondOrEscrowBackThroughSwap`), nothing when neither was paid with this wallet or without the wallet's port. The book opens an order made here as its trade once the active trades are read (a tap while they are being read awaits them, at most 10 s, a bar under the tab's entries meanwhile, `P2pTabScreen.waitingForTradesKey`, #207, another tap meanwhile doing nothing; records that cannot be read open the order's page without « Take Order », `P2pOrderRouteArgs.takeable`, one line saying why in its place, `OrderDetailsScreen.recordsUnreadable`, `p2pOrderRecordsUnreadable`, #207). An order made here whose coordinator contradicted the trade's history says what to do (`p2pTradeBreachWhatToDo`). `ScriptedMakerCoordinator` plays a maker buyer's steps after the take (`update_invoice` with its clear signature, the last invoice taken refused again as the reference refuses it, `api/logics.py:877-879`, `confirm`, `lockEscrow`, `sellerConfirms`, `buyerPaid`), each field after the take as `api/views.py` gives it to a participant (no `trade_satoshis` at 9 nor once the bonds are returned or settled, the hold invoices' locks, `is_fiat_sent` kept after 10; the trade summary not played), as `test/support/maker_orders.dart` does, and `maker_journey_test.dart` runs an order from the form's cubit to a successful end over the fakes. Not in #182: the check that a contract's payout invoice was given by this wallet, which would need what was submitted stored (a schema change).
- **The advanced options of the create form (#183): a range, a description, the two durations and the bond size.** Design note `docs/design/phase-3-making-an-order.md` § 1.11, § 2.2 (« As #183 wrote it »). « Enable advanced options » (`MakerForm.tsx:700-722`) can be opened once the coordinators are drawn and the chosen coordinator's limits read, and closed at any time; opening it sets the range as `resetRange` (`:394-411`: half the amount typed and the amount, else a quarter and three quarters of the coordinator's `max_amount`, each to 2 significant digits, `MakerRange.opening`); a change of currency sets it again when a bound is out of the new currency's orders (`:184-199`, `MakerRange.afterCurrency`, a bound left empty read as 0). With it open: « Amount Range » / « Exact Amount » (`amountRangeEnabled`, on by default, never reset, `:90`), « From » … « to » with the currency and the reference's slider, continuous (`AmountRange.tsx`, `MakerRange.slid`: the other thumb follows to keep 1.6 to 14.8, each bound to 2 or 3 significant digits; the thumb moved told by `MakerRange.movedThumb`, the one that moved the most, since Flutter's `RangeSlider` can shift the other; where the reference can write a range its own check refuses (20-302), the slider writes the accepted range nearest to it (20-296): the other bound computed again from the one moved, held within what the check takes, rounded at the slider's precision or at more digits), « Description » (`OrderDescription`: ends trimmed, an ideographic space made ordinary, at most 240 UTF-16 units as the reference counts, « Must be equal to or shorter than 240 characters », and seen as written, `isShownAsWritten`, the app's own, with the custom method's line), « Password » (#189, below), « Public Duration (HH:mm) » (00:10 to 23:59) and « Escrow/Invoice Timer (HH:mm) » (01:00 to 10:00) through a 24-hour `showTimePicker` titled with the reference's picker titles, a time outside its bounds moved to the nearest (`MakerFormCheck.publicDurationOf`, `escrowDurationOf`), and « Fidelity Bond Size » (2 to 15 % by 0.25, marks 2, 5, 10, 15 %). « Face to Face Location » is shown where the reference puts it, under the payment methods, options open or not (#189, below). The rules are pure, in `MakerFormCheck.of` (`maker_form.dart`): a range only while the options are open and « Amount Range » chosen (`MakerFormInput.hasRange`), its bounds within 0.99 and 1.01 of the form's limits (`MakerAmountLimits.rangeMinMargin`, `rangeMaxMargin`, the make use case's own check), its ratio checked as the reference checks typed bounds (`MakerFormCheck.rangeErrorsOf`, `typedMinRangeRatio` 1.6 − 0.1 and `typedMaxRangeRatio` 14.8 + 0.15 in floating point, `AmountRange.tsx:57-77`), a bound left empty or no number read as 0 as the reference's number field reads it; « The amount range is not valid. » after « Please enter an amount. » (which a range never says) and « The description is not valid. » last, in the reference's order (`:517-565`); « Estimated Bond » on the range's maximum at the size chosen (`bondCalculator.ts`); a duration or bond size no picker gives is never sent (« You must fill the form correctly »). The values of the options are kept and sent while the options are closed, the range aside, as the reference's `buildOrderAttributes` sends them (`:257-275`), and the summary names those that differ from the defaults (`MakerFormCheck.optionsSent`: that a description is attached, never its text, the durations, the bond size), as information while the options are open and in the warning colour once closed (security review of #183, C1); « Clear form » puts them back to the defaults, « Amount Range » kept. The request at the press is compared whole (`MakeOrderRequest ==`, description, durations and bond size included) by the press's checks of #181. Nothing changes in the package, the records (schema 8 already keeps the range, the description and the durations in `maker_terms`), the make use case or the state machine, which compared them since #178 and #179 (`TradeTermsChanged` for the bounds, the description, the bond size, the escrow duration and the public duration where the read gives it; the maker bond on the range's maximum, `MakerTerms.bondAgrees`; a range's amount taken kept with the contract and refused outside its bounds). **What #183 changes in the payment rule**: `CheckPayoutRateUsecase.subjectAt` takes the contract's amount from the trail (`TradeTrail.takenAmount`, kept from the contract's first read), never from the read alone, and the gap line at 6 and 8 (`TradeScreen`) and the rule's line (`PayoutRateLines.amountOf`) name that amount; while an order read gives the contract another amount than the one asked here (a range's amount kept, or none; the terms' fixed amount, which `TradeSeen.asked` carries from the trade reader, a defect of #182 closed here) (`TradeSeen.contractAmountMoved`, through the state machine's own comparison, `TradeTolerances.sameFiat`, the state machine then saying `TradeTermsChanged` or refusing the read), nothing is compared at 6, 8 and 9 (`PayoutRateMismatch`, the kept invoice not compared either) no gap line is shown and « Before letting you send … » writes no figure (`p2pTradePayoutPromptNoAmount`; with one kept, it names it, never the read's): its alert speaks alone, as for amounts that do not agree (C1 bis of #182; functional review of #183, C5), « Submit » and « Confirm … sent » still possible; a coordinator's refusal of a range (1006 to 1009) is said with its message, as 1004 and 1005 (`make_order_l10n.dart`), where it was said as a robot refused; the maker bond's comparison (`WalletRateCheck.ofMakerBond`, `MakerTerms.bondAtPrice`) was on the range's maximum since #180. Deviations from the reference, each in the pull request of #183: the slider continuous (the reference's step is a 5,000th of the span) and never writing a range its check refuses; the bounds typed kept with all their decimals (the reference rounds each to 2 or 3 significant digits as it is typed, `:154-161`); the description checked as seen and trimmed; a time outside a picker's bounds moved to the bound (the reference's picker greys it out); « Swap? » not drawn (design note § 7, proposal 4). Tests: `maker_form_advanced_test.dart` (the rules at their bounds, the range the form sets, every range the slider writes taken by the form), `maker_range_test.dart` (a range made with every option through the make use case and `ScriptedMakerCoordinator`, its bond on the maximum, the amount taken kept, another amount raised or lowered within the contract compared at none of 6, 8 and 9, an amount out of the range refused), the group « the advanced options (#183) » of `create_order_screen_test.dart` (real drags on the slider), and a range's contract on the trade screen in `maker_panels_test.dart`.
- **A range of the create form is never refused without its reason (#225).** Design note `docs/design/phase-3-making-an-order.md` § 2.2 (« As #225 wrote it »). The range the form fills in, or sets again at a change of currency (`MakerRange.opening`, `MakerRange.afterCurrency`, given the form's own limits), is one its check takes, where the reference's ignores the premium and a young coordinator's size limit; the range's box says its rules (the amounts accepted with the coordinator, the ratio of the bounds); and a range refused says which rule it breaks with the bound the form takes (`MakerRangeProblem`, in the range's box and under « Create order »). Keep three things so: the form never changes a bound the user typed (a change of currency sets a range again only when its amounts are outside the new currency's); the amounts named in the reasons are the ones shown under the slider's ends; and the reasons, followed one after the other, lead to a range the form takes (a new reason must not name a bound another reason then refuses).
- **One breakdown of the buyer's payout (#228), lean since #250.** Design note `docs/design/phase-3-making-an-order.md` § 3.3 (« As #228 wrote it », « As #250 made it lean »); the words are the owner's, validated on 2026-11-24 (`p2pPayoutBreakdown*`, `p2pDetailShow`, `p2pDetailHide`, `p2pPayoutRateAgainst`), English composed on the model of the French. The payout panel draws above its tabs, for a maker buyer and a taker buyer alike, the sats paid by the coordinator, the fees (« Routing fees », « Routing and swap fees » once this wallet's invoice is in the field) and in bold « You will receive » (`PayoutBreakdown.fees`, `.received`: their difference, to the sat, in `lib/src/domain/trade/payout_breakdown.dart`, pure), then the rule's check of those very sats (`PayoutCheck`, built only by the trade screen from the rule, `PayoutRateLines.checkOf`; drawn by `PayoutCheckLines`): « Gap to the Bull price (indicative) » within the threshold; beyond it the red alert in two lines, its bold line « Gap against you » with the gap in the gap line's place (never « (indicative) ») and « Bull Wallet expected … sats, the coordinator pays … » under it, the figure paid in red; « Amount not checked » in orange with « Retry » and its line; the grey « not quoted » line for a currency the Bull wallet does not price (the orange line « since the order was made » of #237 is not written). The detail (`PayoutBreakdownDetail`: what Bull Wallet expected and its price's line, the coordinator's fee, the routing fees, « Your invoice » with the reference's copy, the swap fee, one explanation) opens with « Show details », both inside the breakdown's card under the gap's line (or the alert's two lines, or « not quoted »), the tabs and buttons moving down as it opens: the validated mock-up of this screen, the owner's decision of 2026-11-24 (« under the buttons, which never move » is for the bond's and collateral's confirmations, whose buttons are in their card); « A small gap is normal… » only beside a gap within the threshold both ways (`PriceCheckLines.smallGap`, `PayoutCheckCompared.small`, functional review of #268, C1), never beside an alert. Keep it so: an alert is always drawn without a tap, in the breakdown or, where no breakdown can draw it (no form yet, a breakdown of other sats than those the rule compared), alone above the form with its explanation under it (`PayoutPanel.looseCheck`; while the amounts do not agree the rule compares nothing, `PayoutRateAmountsDisagree`, and the state machine's alert speaks alone), and no banner of the rule above the panel where the panel is drawn (`PayoutRateLines.comparisonInBreakdown`, set from the panel actually built); a comparison within the threshold beside an alert of the screen is not drawn at all (`PayoutRateAlertAlone`, #249); no breakdown while `PayoutBreakdown.amountsDisagree` or `TradeSeen.contractAmountMoved`, the reference's « Submit invoice for … sats » then said instead (« sats » in lower case, the owner's vocabulary), so that one figure of the invoice is never beside « You will receive »; nothing new read from the coordinator; the panel and the wallet's section never name the rule's types (`trade_screen_architecture_test.dart`). At 9 and in « Confirm … sent » the rule's red alert is the same two lines (`p2pPayoutRateAgainst`, then `p2pPayoutBreakdownAgainstFigures` in `TradeDialogTone.alertFigures`, not bold), then its explanation (`p2pPayoutBreakdownAgainstDetail`, same tone: there is no detail there, the last moment before the money is sent), for the sats kept at 6 or 8 compared alone too (`PayoutRateAskedOnly`, beside the state machine's alert, whose neutral line is not said, #228's comment); the comparison's sentence, « rate not checked » and the « not priced » line keep their earlier words there, the page validated on 2026-11-24 covering the breakdown only.
- **The lean confirmations of « Pay with this wallet » (#250, second slice): one card for the bond and the collateral.** Design notes `docs/design/phase-3-making-an-order.md` § 3.3 and `docs/design/phase-3-taken-order-price-check.md` § 5 (« As #250 made it lean »); the words and the mock-up are the owner's, validated on 2026-11-24, English composed on the model of the French. `PayConfirmationCard` (`lib/src/ui/trade/pay_confirmation_card.dart`) draws the four confirmations (a maker's bond, a taker's bond, a taker seller's collateral, a maker seller's collateral with its contract), in place of `_Confirmation`, `_EscrowConfirmation`, `_RateLines`, `_AmountRows`, `_AlertLine` and `_InfoLine`. On arrival: « Bond » or « Collateral » (`p2pPayRowBond`, `p2pPayRowCollateral`), « Swap and network fees » (the swap fee and the network fee added, the detail's two rows), a rule, « Total to pay » in bold (what « Pay » takes, read out as the card opens: the card has no title, the step's title above says what is paid), the rule's check drawn by `PriceCheckLines` (`lib/src/ui/widgets/price_check_lines.dart`, shared with the payout breakdown's `PayoutCheckLines`: « Gap to the Bull price (indicative) », beyond the threshold « ⚠ Gap against you » and « Bull Wallet expected … sats, the coordinator asks … » (`p2pPayAgainstFigures`), « ⚠ Amount not checked » with « Retry » and its line, the grey « not quoted » line), then under a rule the confirmation's own blocks (stale, in red, or asked again, in orange, never both; one earlier collateral not back of a known amount in two lines, `p2pPayEarlierNotBack`, `p2pPayEarlierOutTotal`; several, an amount not known, a collateral kept and the bond out too keep their #187 and #188 words, one bold red line each; « New contract », neutral), « Go back », « Pay {total} sats », and « Show details » (`DetailToggle`, `lib/src/ui/widgets/detail_toggle.dart`) under the buttons, in the card. The detail opens under the link, so the buttons never move: the swap fees, the network fee, « Balance after payment », then the comparison's paragraph and « A small gap is normal » (a gap within the threshold both ways only, `PriceCheckLines.smallGap`), or the red alert's explanation (`p2pPayAgainstDetail`), or « Amount not checked »'s (`p2pPayUnverifiedDetail`); the earlier collateral's explanation; for a collateral the swap service's facts (`p2pPayEscrowHeld`, reworded); the reference's hold-invoice sentence, word for word, in grey. The comparison's paragraph: for a collateral asked above the sats expected the validated one (`p2pPayRateDetail`, the gap without its sign, `P2pFormats.gapSize`); for a collateral at or below them `p2pPayRateWalletEscrow`, for a bond `p2pPayRateWallet`, unchanged (the validated sentence says « more » and « its fees included », which a bond does not carry). Rows and toggle are `AmountRow`, `AmountCard` (`lib/src/ui/widgets/amount_row.dart`, the payout breakdown's `PayoutRow` and card moved there). « Pay » is wrapped in `MovedTapGuard` (`lib/src/ui/widgets/moved_tap_guard.dart`): when the layout moves it (an alert of the screen that comes or goes, « Retry » that brings its answer), a tap on it is ignored for `MovedTapGuard.afterMove` (700 ms), since it was aimed at what stood there (« Show details », « Go back »; security review of #268, C1 and C2); its place is measured after each frame on the screen with the scroll offsets of every `Scrollable` around it added back, so that no scroll (the page, between tabs, a nested list, the screen bringing it into view) sets it off and a move of the layout does, whatever the structure (one move is not seen: content under the button that shrinks while the page is scrolled to its end, the scroll offset following by as much, #267); its first frame is only recorded (`moved_tap_guard_test.dart`); no lock, the button stays enabled and pays at the next tap; the cubit's own guard against a second payment is unchanged; a small gap separates « Pay » from « Show details ». Keep it so: every alert visible without a tap, with its figures, above « Go back », only its explanation in the detail; beside an alert (the screen's, `ShownAlerts`, or the card's own blocks) no comparison within the threshold, nor « A small gap is normal »; an alert never silences another (red, « Amount not checked » and « not quoted » stay beside any other); « (indicative) » never on an alert; « Pay » pays exactly the quote shown and is offered with every alert but a stale confirmation. Nothing changed in the cubits, the use cases, the domain, the computations or what is sent. A negative gap or amount of sats is written with the minus sign, U+2212, on every P2P screen (`P2pFormats.gap`, `.bigSats`, display only: no figure written by them is read back, compared with a figure or sent; the copy of « Your invoice » copies the raw number), as the validated page writes « −320 », « −0.03 % ». Tests: `taken_price_route_test.dart` and `maker_escrow_route_test.dart` (« the lean confirmation … », in English and in French, `support/lean_confirmation.dart`: the card on arrival, the detail opened and closed with « Go back » and « Pay » at the same place, the red alert, « Amount not checked » and « Retry », « not quoted », « Pay » paying the quote shown once, 320 points at ×1, ×2, ×3 with no word cut), `maker_sell_route_test.dart` (the earlier collateral, « New contract », the confirmation asked again or stale, two alerts at once), `maker_bond_panel_test.dart`, `bond_payment_panel_test.dart`, `escrow_payment_panel_test.dart`, `escrow_contract_lines_test.dart`.
- **The payment rule's three gaps (#237).** Design note `docs/design/phase-3-making-an-order.md` § 3.3 bis, written before the code. (1) A buyer's trade whose records cannot be opened now (the database, the vault or the robot's binding refused the read: local only, no coordinator can cause it): `CheckPayoutRateUsecase.termsOf` answers `PayoutRateTermsUnreadable` (a `PayoutRateTermsRead`, with the terms themselves), `PayoutRateCubit` emits `PayoutRateTermsUnread` instead of nothing (the silence of the rule says « checked, nothing wrong »), does not keep that answer for the page's life, opens the records again only at « Retry » and when the step is shown anew (never at the watch's cadence), and the screens say « Amount not checked » with « Retry » and a second line of their own (`p2pPayoutRateTermsUnreadLine`, `p2pPayoutRateTermsUnreadDetail`, proposed to the owner, not validated: the validated second line speaks of the Bull wallet's price, never asked there); in the breakdown `PayoutCheckTermsUnread`, at 9 and in « Confirm … sent » three lines (`TradeDialogTone.warningLine`, the orange not bold). (2) For an order made here in a currency the Bull wallet does not price, under the grey « not quoted » line, the owner's orange alert (decided on 2026-11-20, words validated on 2026-11-24: `p2pRateAgainstSinceMade`, `p2pRateAgainstSinceMadeDetail`) when the contract's sats fixed at the take (`last_satoshis`) are more than `TradeTolerances.walletRateGap` against the maker from its amount's sats at the rate of the creation (`TradeRateGap.againstFor`, exact, in sats, rounded away from zero, `null` within the threshold or in the maker's favour, so that no percentage is ever said there): in the payout breakdown at 6, 8 and 15 (`CheckPayoutRateUsecase.againstOf`, `PayoutRateChecked.against`, `PayoutCheckNotPriced.against`, explanation in the detail), at 9 and in « Confirm … sent » (explanation under it, no detail there), and in a maker seller's collateral confirmation (`PayConfirmationCard`, from the status's `rateGap`, explanation in the detail); never at the maker bond (no take yet), never for an order taken here (the owner's decision of 2026-11-24), beside amounts that do not agree only at 9 with the invoice of 6 or 8 kept (`PayoutRateAskedOnly`: an alert beside the state machine's, functional review R5), nothing compared otherwise then; it silences nothing; « the rate of the creation » is the price read again when the make was sent, which the make accepts up to 10 % from the price the form showed for a currency not priced (`MakePriceSeen.holdsAt`), the margins adding up to about 18 % (#282); at 15 the new invoice was compared with the Bull wallet's price only, not with the invoice of 6 or 8, until #281 (below); later batch #283. (3) The rule runs at 15 once the failed invoice expired (`PayoutRateStep.of`, `subjectAt`, `subjectOfTake`: `trade_satoshis` is `Logics.payout_amount` there as at 6 and 8), for an order made or taken here; `PayoutPanelRequest.checkedByRule` keeps « No Bull Wallet check at this step » for a page without the rule only; the trade screen draws the rule's lines above the panel at 15 where the panel does not. One more read of the Bull wallet's price outside Tor at most when 15 appears (none within 3 minutes of the last), one at « Submit » when 3 minutes old, one per « Retry ». `ScriptedMakerCoordinator.failPayout` plays 15 for a maker buyer. `MakerTerms.rateGap` is called by the state machine and, since #237, by the rule, from the order's bounded `satoshis_now` (`robot_bindings_architecture_test.dart`). Tests: `rate_against_since_made_test.dart`, `payout_rate_test.dart` (group « the three gaps of #237 »), `taken_price_usecases_test.dart` (« #237, for an order taken here »), `unquoted_rate_route_test.dart` on the real composition.
- **At 15, the new invoice against the one asked at 6 or 8 (#281).** From the security review of #237 (R1), not a decision of the owner. Design note `docs/design/phase-3-making-an-order.md` § 3.3 bis, point 4 (written before the code) and « As #281 wrote it ». In the reference the sum asked at 15 is `Logics.payout_amount`, the very sum of 6 and 8 for the same contract, to the sat. `CheckPayoutRateUsecase._atPayout` (a maker buyer and a taker buyer alike: a taker's state machine leaves about 4 % and the fee unseen between two reads) keeps nothing at 15 (in `main` before, 15 fed `PayoutRateMemory.keepAsked`, so that a comparison would have met 15's own sum), and compares the sats asked at 15 with the **largest** the payout panel asked at 6 or 8 for the contract (`PayoutRateMemory.firstOf` / `keepFirst`, in memory only, beside the smallest kept for 9: the largest cannot be lowered by an earlier read of a small sum), **with no tolerance** (two figures of the coordinator from the same function): fewer is `PayoutRateBelowFirst`, a red alert in two lines (`p2pPayoutFirstFewer`, `p2pPayoutFirstFewerFigures`, explanation `p2pPayoutFirstFewerDetail` in the detail), « Paid by the coordinator » in red, and beside it neither the gap « (indicative) » nor « A small gap is normal »; the Bull wallet's red alert, « Amount not checked » and the « not quoted » line are drawn beside it, never silenced; nothing kept (a restart, the trade screen not shown at 6 or 8, or a next contract the coordinator opens at 6 or 7 with a later deadline, `TradeStatus._contractOf`, with no restart: security review of #281, C1, the substance in #285) is `PayoutRateFirstUnknown`: the comparison with the Bull wallet's price, the discreet `p2pPayoutFirstUnknown` and in the detail `p2pPayoutFirstUnknownDetail` instead of « A small gap is normal »; as many sats or more is as before. Amounts that do not agree at 15: the state machine's alert alone, as at 6 and 8. The five texts are proposed to the owner, not validated; the red alert's « Check the order before going on » at 15 is unchanged (security review of #237, R4), to be shown to him. Keep it so: no sum of 15 ever kept as a reference, no comparison without a sum kept, nothing on disk (schema 9 unchanged), nothing new read. Tests: `first_invoice_route_test.dart` (real composition, English and French, a maker buyer in EUR and GBP and a taker buyer), groups « #281 » of `payout_rate_test.dart` and `payout_breakdown_test.dart` (320 points, text ×1 to ×3).
- **Follow-ups of the payment rule (#283).** From the reviews of #237 and #281, not decisions of the owner; design note `docs/design/phase-3-making-an-order.md` § 3.3 bis (« As #283 wrote it »). `MovedTapGuard`, reused as it is (its place after every frame less the scrolls around it, 700 ms, `MovedTapGuard.afterMove`), guards the « Confirm » of `ConfirmFiatSentDialog` (the buyer's declaration that the fiat was sent; since #293 the seller's « Confirm » of `ConfirmFiatReceivedDialog`, which releases the escrow, too) (`TradeDialogBody.guardsGo`, for a dialog whose lines follow a state while open): a tap just after the rule's lines moved it is ignored, said nowhere, the button enabled, the next tap goes through. « Submit », « Generate Invoice » and the chat step's « Confirm … sent » have no guard, deliberately: the guard cannot tell a move the user caused, and their ordinary gestures move them just before the natural tap (review of #283, C2; a guard on « Submit » armed by the rule's changes only is a variant left to a decision). `PayoutRateCubit` keeps the steps its page has shown, each with the kind of status it was shown at (`(PayoutRateStep, Type)`, since the follow-ups of #270, #273 and #283: the first 15 after 6 or 8, of the same step, 13 between them, is shown for the first time; verification of the review of #283, V-R1): after a read of the Bull wallet's price that failed, a step shown for the first time at its status reads once (as before), a step already shown at the same status that comes back (15 ↔ 13, 9 ↔ 10, 6 or 8 ↔ 7) reads again only once the last read is `PayoutRateCubit.freshFor` (3 minutes) old; 8 directly after 6, and 15 directly after 6 or 8, the same step at another status and no step shown anew, read after a failure only once the last read is 3 minutes old, as before #283, while the first status of each kind (6, 8 or 15) shown after a status outside the rule (7, 10 or 13) is a step shown anew and reads once (6, 13, 8 with the price service failing: two reads; review of #287); « Retry » always reads; `beforeAct` unchanged; nothing changes when the service answers. `TradeRateGap`'s `==` and `hashCode` include its two sats. At 15 with nothing kept of 6 or 8, « Not compared with the first invoice » is said alone above the form when the breakdown shows other sats than those compared (`PayoutCheckLines.firstOnly`; in the app, a payout given at 15 awaiting its answer), never the comparison of other sats; its words and colour stay (#285). The app's capture test has « captures of status 15 with the payment rule » (`reception-15-regle`, `reception-15-moins-de-sats`), and no longer the capture of 15 without the wallet's port. Tests: `rule_follow_ups_route_test.dart` (real composition, English and French, 320 points, text ×3: « Submit » taking a tap at once after a move, the dialog's « Confirm » ignoring one, the reads at 15 ↔ 13), groups « #283 » of `payout_rate_test.dart` (9 ↔ 10, a step shown for the first time after a failure), `rate_against_since_made_test.dart`, `payout_breakdown_test.dart` (the lines without a panel, unused by the app: the rule's and the panel's cubits are registered together), `payout_panel_test.dart`, and a maker seller's collateral card at 320 points in `unquoted_rate_route_test.dart`.
- **Follow-ups with a large text (#270, #273, #283).** From the reviews of #276, #277 and #286, not decisions of the owner; no text, request, computation or schema changed, the rendering at the normal text size unchanged (measured on `main` 0e8bb8a84 at 360 points and pinned by tests). `DetailToggle` (`lib/src/ui/widgets/detail_toggle.dart`, the order page before the take, the payout breakdown, the payment confirmations, « See history » of the Garage): with a text larger than normal, or when either of its two words takes more than one line, its label holds the room of the larger of « Show details » and « Hide details » (or of its own two words), in height and in width, measured by `WordFitText.measure` (the same shrink as `WordFitText`, `lib/src/ui/widgets/word_fit_text.dart`), so that the link keeps its place and size as it opens or folds and a second quick tap at the same place folds it again (at three times the text size on a phone of 320 points « Show details » took two lines and « Hide details » one); at the normal size it is the word shown alone, as before. The title of the robot's panel (`RobotCoordinatorPanel`, « Coordinator that knows your robot: ») and the robot's nickname above its avatar (`RobotNickname`, `lib/src/ui/garage/garage_wizard.dart`, shared by the Garage's profile and its wizard) are `WordFitText`: a word is never broken when it fits a line, a word wider than the line shrinks the text to fit, never under the normal size, nothing is elided, a screen reader says the same words (a nickname wider than the line at the normal size still breaks, as any `WordFitText`). The first 15 after 6 or 8, 13 between them, reads the Bull wallet's price once after a failed read (`PayoutRateCubit`, entry #283). « The order changed. » for its two causes at once (the premium read moved, and the confirmation that found the order changed) is said once, pinned by a test. Left as it was then, described in #270 and #267: with the page scrolled within the detail's height of its end, folding the detail makes the page scroll back, so that everything above the detail moves down on the screen and nothing moves in the content; on the payment confirmation a second tap at once at the place of « Hide details » could land on « Pay » and pay, or on « Go back » (closed by #288, entry below). Tests: `taken_price_page_route_test.dart` (group « the order page with a large text »: the link at 360 points and the normal size as on `main`, at 320 and 360 points ×1 to ×3 two quick taps at the same place, the two causes of « The order changed. »), `rule_follow_ups_route_test.dart` (the breakdown's link, 320 points, ×1 to ×3), `order_history_route_test.dart` (group « the robot's nickname and its panel's title »: 320 points ×1 to ×3, 360 points at the normal size as on `main`), `payout_rate_test.dart` and `rule_follow_ups_route_test.dart` (the first 15).
- **The second tap of a fold that paid (#288), and a button just shown (#291).** From the independent review of #287 (and #267, first point) and the two reviews of #291, not decisions of the owner; no text, request, computation, schema or rendering changed. `MovedTapGuard` (`lib/src/ui/widgets/moved_tap_guard.dart`, on « Pay » of `PayConfirmationCard`, « Continue » of `TakeOrderSection`, the « Confirm » of the buyer's `ConfirmFiatSentDialog` and, since #293, of the seller's `ConfirmFiatReceivedDialog`) keeps its measure (its place after every frame with the scroll offsets around it added back, more than half a point of move starting `MovedTapGuard.afterMove`, 700 ms) and measures too its place on the screen alone: a move on the screen sets it off when no `ScrollPosition` around it told its listeners of a scroll since the last measure made and none is scrolling. A scroll by a finger, a fling, an animation or a jump (`jumpTo`, `ensureVisible`) tells the position's listeners; the page brought back to its new end by the layout (`ScrollPosition.correctPixels`, the default physics keeping the offset in range: content under the button that shrinks with the page at its end, as « Hide details » folding the detail under « Pay », or a viewport that grows, as a system bar that goes or a rotation) tells nobody, and everything above moves down on the screen while nothing moves in the content: that move, which the first measure could not see, now ignores a tap for 700 ms. A scroll made after a frame's layout and before the guard's measure of that frame (another post-frame callback, as `BondPaymentPanel`'s `ensureVisible` of « Pay » when the confirmation opens) is shown only at the next frame: that measure is skipped (`_stale`), the guard listening from its first frame (`didChangeDependencies`). A guarded button that appears starts the delay at its first measure (`MovedTapGuard.armsWhenShown`, true by default): a tap on « Pay » or on the dialog's « Confirm » less than 0.7 s after it was shown is ignored, since a tap meant for a button just shown, whose amounts are still to read, is no ordinary gesture, while a second tap at the place of « Pay with this wallet », where `ensureVisible` brings « Pay », is (security review of #291, B1: with a text ×2 or ×3 and « Pay with this wallet » at the page's bottom edge, `main` 165b2bd18 ignored that second tap by the accident of a measure that mixed the place before the jump with the offset after it, the first head of #291 paid it). « Continue » has `armsWhenShown: false`: it is shown once the robot's read the user waited for has come, where « Take Order » stood busy, and its card is brought into view by a 300 ms animation during which no tap goes through. A card built anew (the tab « Order » then « Contract ») is a button shown and starts the delay; the screen built again in place (back from the background, a read of the coordinator that changes nothing, the balance) does not. Measured on the real composition (`fold_tap_route_test.dart`): at 390 × 490, the normal size, English, a maker's bond, the fold brings « Pay » under the finger between 140 and 188 points before the page's end, and a second tap there after 16, 100, 300 or 700 ms paid on `main` and pays nothing now, the next tap after the delay paying once; the same over 320 and 390 points, ×1 and ×3, English and French, for a maker's bond, a maker seller's collateral and a taker's bond; the second tap on « Pay with this wallet » at the bottom edge (the review's four sizes, the quote at once or the price read held 1 or 2 s) pays nothing at 100, 300 and 600 ms after the confirmation's first frame and pays at 750 ms. « Go back » under the finger is not guarded and closes the confirmation, as before (no money). The cost: a tap on « Pay » or the dialog's « Confirm » within 0.7 s of its showing, and a tap on « Pay », « Continue » or « Confirm » within 0.7 s after a fold at the page's end, a view that grows (a system bar or a keyboard that goes) or a rotation, is ignored once, with no sign (#283 keeps the question of a sign); the next tap always goes through. A coordinator moves the layout only through its answers, read at most every 5 s at the bond and 10 s at the collateral and in the chat (`TradeCadence`, the next read that long after the last one ends): a tap is ignored only within 0.7 s of the last move an answer caused, so that it can never hold « Pay » off for long; the user opens the confirmation and the dialog, but a coordinator can make the confirmation's card go and come back without a tap (corrected by the second security pass of #291: a read refused for changed terms shows the wait and takes the card away, the next read brings it back, its delay started again), at most the guard's delay per read, the button never dead; in the reads measured, no line under the confirmation's card changes while it is open (the alert of other terms and its trace come above the card, which the first measure sees; the bond locked or the order expired replace the card). Not seen: a move in the same frame as any scroll (a user's or one the app makes); a drift of less than half a point per frame; on iOS a fold during the bounce at the page's end (the spring tells the listeners; not played). Tests: `moved_tap_guard_test.dart` (group « #288 »: content under the button that shrinks at the page's end, a line under it that goes at a read with the one tap aimed at it, the viewport that grows or shrinks, a drag, a fling to its last frame, an animation, a jump, a jump from a post-frame callback, a button brought into view by `ensureVisible` after its first frame and its taps within the delay, a skipped measure followed by a move, a button that appears and one built again in place), `fold_tap_route_test.dart` (the review's case with its four delays and the tap after the delay, the sweep of the three confirmations, the second tap on « Pay with this wallet » at the bottom edge, an opening that scrolls followed by the fold, the ordinary gestures with the taps each ignores, « Continue »), `maker_confirm_dialog_test.dart` (the dialog just opened); the tests that tapped « Pay » or the dialog's « Confirm » as soon as shown now wait `MovedTapGuard.afterMove` first, as a user's tap comes later (their `tap` helpers wait when the button is guarded).
- **The pause and « Unpause Order » of a public order made here (#184).** Design note `docs/design/phase-3-making-an-order.md` § 1.6, § 2.2, § 3.4 (« As #184 wrote it »). `PauseOrderUsecase` (`lib/src/domain/usecases/trade/pause_order_usecase.dart`), its only caller `TradeCubit.pause` (the locator gives it `execute`, as the cancel's, and `running`; a cubit given none offers no pause), its types in `lib/src/domain/trade/trade_pause.dart` (`PauseWanted`, `PauseRefusal`, `PauseOutcome`). The request is the reference's `{"action":"pause"}` for both buttons (`TradeBox/index.tsx:310-313`), a toggle with no direction (`api/logics.py:1758-1783`), so: what the user asked (`PauseWanted.of`) comes from the status shown at the tap, which must be a maker's public or paused order read at that very status, marked stored, this robot shown as the maker, the trade made here; the order is read again under the order's lock (`TradeTakeLocks`, shared with the take, the cancel, the payout, the bond's payment and the chat step) while the watch sends nothing (`TradeReader.duringAction`), and the request leaves only when that stored read still shows the status the user saw (`PauseStatusMoved` otherwise, nothing sent: paused or unpaused from another app, taken, expired, cancelled); it is sent once through the robot's places and never again by the app, whatever the answer (a busy transport, which sends nothing, is tried again by the limiter). **The answer is not kept as the status shown** (an outcome unknown carries when its request left, `PauseOutcomeUnknown.requestLeftAt`): only the read sent after the request is, so that an answer lost on the way back is resolved at once by that read and « paused » is only ever shown from a read (it does not catch a coordinator that lies: the read comes from the same coordinator); the cubit then asks the watch for no read more after a done, a not reached or a status moved, whose read was just kept (three requests a tap: the read before, the pause, the read after); `PauseDone` only when that read after is stored and shows the order where the user asked (`PauseWanted.reachedAt`), `PauseNotReached` when the coordinator accepted it and the read after shows it elsewhere (toggled twice or the other way, taken, expired, a coordinator that answers falsely), `PauseNotDone` for a refusal (1032, 1033, 1047, the empty `400`, the authentication's) or a request that never left, `PauseOutcomeUnknown` otherwise (an answer lost, an accepted answer with no read after stored). The screen (`TradeScreen`): the pause icon with the reference's tooltip « Pause the public order » beside « Public orders for … » (`Prompts/PublicWait.tsx:101-133`), alone under the lines when no book was received; « Unpause Order » under `Prompts/Paused.tsx`'s text; one tap, no dialog; spinning and off while it runs; off while a cancel runs, with « The order is being cancelled: wait until it ends. »; « Still waiting for {coordinator}. Tor can take a minute. » after 30 s. « Cancel » stays on during a pause, as the reference's (`TradeBox/index.tsx:246-251`): `TradeCubit.cancel` is not stopped by a running pause, the order's lock makes the cancel wait for the pause's end, and the coordinator then refuses it (1020) when the pause moved the status it guards, which the cancel's notice says (functional review of #184, B1). `PausesInFlight` (one for the feature, in memory) knows the pause of each order running now: a trade screen opened again meanwhile shows it running and says what it gives (`TradeCubit.start`, `PauseOrderUsecase.running`), rather than offer a tap that would wait and then say « The order changed before your request left ». A pause asked here whose outcome was unknown and that a later read shows paused counts as paused here, so that a later return to public is said. **After an outcome unknown, of a pause or an unpause** (security review of #184, C1 and C2), `PausesInFlight` also keeps a `PauseUncertainty` for the order (`trade_pause.dart`), which every screen of the order shows (`TradeCubit` gives it to `PauseOrderUsecase.uncertainty` with each status read): until a read sent after the request (its `TradeSeen.requestedAt` later than the request's) shows the order paused, « Until a read made after your request shows it paused, count on your order being public » under any public or paused status, and the reference's « it cannot be seen or taken by other robots » not shown; once one does, « A request whose answer was lost may still arrive and make your order public again… » until a second read, at least three minutes after the first (`PauseUncertainty.settleAfter`, a choice: the transport gives no sure bound past which a request sent can no longer arrive) on the device's clock, and three minutes plus the transport's deadline for a request (`coordinatorRequestDeadline`, 132 s) on a time that only moves forward (`PausesInFlight(elapsed:, requestDeadline:)`, a `Stopwatch` the locator starts, as the limiter's, so that a clock set forward does not lift it sooner, #214, security review of #219, R1), still shows it paused, the line saying that two such reads lower the risk without ruling it out, and the reference's « it cannot be seen or taken by other robots » not shown beside it either (#214, security review of #184, R-a, R-b); a read public restarts the wait, another status ends it; a later request's done does not end it. The unknown outcome's text says to wait before tapping again (a second request would undo the first if the first arrives late). A tap while another screen's pause of the order runs follows that pause and sends nothing. A cancel waiting behind a pause says « Your cancel will leave once the pause or unpause ends. »; a maker's cancel of a public or paused order refused with 1020 says « The order changed state before the cancel: nothing was cancelled. Tap "Cancel" again if you still want to cancel it. », with « Your order is public… » when it is read public. What it gave is said under the prompt in the app's words (`TradePauseNoticeL10n`; the reference says nothing, its button only stops spinning), never the coordinator's text; when the user asked to pause and it is not done, « Your order is public: robots can see it and take it. » while a stored read shows it public, and « Until a read shows it paused, count on your order being public: robots can take it. » when the outcome is unknown; an order this screen saw paused by its own pause and later read public with no tap here is said « public again » (`PausePublicAgain`, in memory, for the screen's life), whatever statuses that are no end were read between (taken, then back in the book, #214). Deviations, declared in the pull request: those lines; the answer not kept as a status (where the cancel and the chat step keep theirs); no « Unpause Order » nor pause button from a cubit given no pause. `ScriptedMakerCoordinator` answers the `pause` as the reference (`api/views.py:617-619`, `:686-695`; 1047, 1032, 1033; the toggle; the maker's read), with hostile switches named as such (`pauseAnswerChanges`, `pauseNotToggled`, `pauseRefusals`). Tests: `pause_order_usecase_test.dart`, `pause_cubit_test.dart`, `maker_pause_screen_test.dart` (real taps, two quick taps, a tap while it runs, the app hidden and back, the real use cases over the scripted coordinator counting the requests, a pause held on its way by `test/support/holding_transport.dart` with a cancel, a watch and a screen opened again).
- **The renewal of an order made here (#185).** Design note `docs/design/phase-3-making-an-order.md` § 1.8, § 2.2 (« As #185 wrote it »). « Renew Order » under the expiry's reason at 5 (`Prompts/Expired.tsx:40-97`) and « Renew » in `SuccessfulPanel` above the trade summary (`Prompts/Successful.tsx:276-287`), for a record `madeHere` at a status marked stored where the reference offers its maker a renewal (`OrderRenewal.offeredAt`, `lib/src/domain/trade/trade_renewal.dart`: 5 and 14 for both sides, 13 and 15 for a seller only, `TradeBox/index.tsx:602-690`), given by the router (`TradeScreen.onRenew`) only where the feature can make an order; a tap opens one page, none while it is open. A renewal is a new order: `MakeOrderUsecase.renewal(record)` reads the records only (the stored trail and `maker_terms`, restored by `MakerTerms.restore`), sends and writes nothing, and gives `OrderRenewal` (the order renewed, its coordinator as the federation list gives it now at the address the order was made on, the kept request) or a `RenewalRefusal` (`RenewalNotMadeHere`, `RenewalNotEnded`, `RenewalTermsUnreadable`, `RenewalCoordinatorGone`); never what the coordinator reads back (the reference takes every field from the order read, `TradeBox/index.tsx:162-181`). Its page is the create page in a renewal mode (`P2pCreateRouteArgs.renewal`, `MakeOrderCubit.renewal`, registered under `MakeOrderCubit.renewalInstance`, `MakeRenewalState`): « Renew Order » as title, `p2pRenewIntro`, the fields filled with the kept request and locked (the options open for a range or an option that differs from the defaults; the methods' text one chip), the order's coordinator only (no lottery, no « Select Host »; its `/api/info/` and limits read, then the Bull wallet's price, an amount being shown from the start), and everything else the creation's: the summary, « Estimated Bond », the rate, the comparison, the press's checks, the pending makes, `PopScope`, `MakeOrderUsecase.execute` (a fresh robot, `MakePriceCheckChanged`), the made order opened; no « Store your garage key », as the reference's renewal sends at once. « Create order » sends only when `MakerFormCheck` judges the locked fields into exactly the kept request (`p2pRenewNotAsKept` otherwise), and `MakeOrderCubit.make` refuses any other request or coordinator in this mode. The order renewed stays as it was in the records; the new one has its own record, terms and trail. **Navigation** (functional review of #185, C1): `P2pRouter.openRenewal` pushes the renewal's page; an order made there closes it with the new record (`P2pRouter.openMade`, which still replaces a creation's page by the new trade), and the renewed order's page is then replaced by the new trade's, as the reference navigates to the new order (`TradeBox/index.tsx:186-188`), so no page of the renewed order stays behind with its button spinning (go_router never completes the wait of a page replaced); the page left otherwise (back, a refusal, an outcome unknown now a pending make) gives the renewed order's page its button back, and a new page then says why it cannot send. **Stuck** (R3, the project's line, the reference leaving the user with the coordinator's refusal): a reason of the form the user could correct in a creation (an amount outside the limits now, a currency no longer priced, a method no longer accepted) or a make refused for the kept terms (`MakeAmountOutsideLimits`, `MakePriceUnknown`, `MakeTermsRefused`) adds `p2pRenewNoLongerAsKept` (« This order can no longer be renewed as it was: create a new order. »); limits not read add « Retry » (`MakeOrderCubit.retryLimits`), « Select Host » not being offered. Deviations, declared in the pull request: the kept terms, a fresh robot (so « Renew » after a trade works, where the reference in Garage Key mode refuses it, `:150-157`), a page before the make, no « Store your garage key », « Create order » as the page's button. Reached again after its screen was left through the Garage's « Last order #N » (#216, next item). For a password, since #189: « Password » is the only field open of the renewal of a protected order, its SHA-256 sent (« The order password and the meeting point (#189) », below). Tests: `renew_order_test.dart` (the use case and the cubit over the real records and `ScriptedMakerCoordinator`, a hostile read of the expired order, the price), `renew_order_screen_test.dart` (real taps, from the trade screen to the makes counted at the coordinator).
- **The last finished order opened again, and an order renewed twice (#216, #217).** Design note `docs/design/phase-3-making-an-order.md` § 2.2 (« As #216 and #217 wrote it »). « Last order #N » (`p2pRobotLastOrder`, the reference's `GarageKeyProfile.tsx:226`) under the robot navigator of the Garage profile (`LastOrderLines`, rows of `TradeRecordRow`, shared with « Active trades »), where the reference shows its robot's last order (`GarageKeyProfile.tsx:214-230`), which opens the order again: here each trade has a robot of its own, so the lines come from the records, the last finished order made here and the last finished trade taken here, each a record closed after its order was confirmed, the newest end first (`ListLastOrdersUsecase`, its only caller `LastOrdersCubit`, provided above the Garage by the tab; `TradeRecordRepository.watchTrades`, every record of the network with an order id, closed ones included, read only); a tap opens the trade as « Active trades » does. Said once: the robot panel says the coordinator's « Last order #N » of the robot shown only when no line above opens that order (`RobotCoordinatorPanel.lastOrdersShown`; a robot of a pasted key, whose trade this device does not hold, keeps it: functional review of #220, R4, and its verification). One tap opens one screen: another does nothing until the Garage is shown again (security review of #220, R4). Not the « Order » list, which opens only while a trade runs or a make is pending. As the reference, a renewal cancelled or expired since becomes the « last order » and hides the order it renewed (functional review of #220, R5). The trade screen of a closed record: its status is the trail restored (`StatusUnknown` at the end, « This trade is over for this robot. »), so the renewal is offered for a record closed at an end where the reference offers it (a record is closed only once its end is on disk), not only on a status stored by a read, and the use case reads the records again; an expired order is read once like a successful end (`TradeReader.readsEndOnce`, `endRead`). What that read gave, when it did not show the end as kept, is `TradeWatchStatus.endReread`, each case in its own words (reviews of #220, C1): `TradeEndReading` while it runs (« Reading this order again from … », never « over »); `TradeEndNotRead` (no answer, a timeout, a transport error, an answer that does not parse, which includes an order of another number or of another maker, refused by the package as malformed, or a read held back: `p2pTradeEndNotRead`); `TradeEndUnknownToCoordinator` (1042, or a `404`: `p2pTradeEndUnknown`); `TradeEndShownAlive` (an order at a status that is no end, 0 to 3, 6 to 11, 13, 15, 16: the alert `p2pTradeEndAlive`, in the colour of an error, that it may still be taken or need an action and that sats may still be locked in it, to check in the official app before creating another; the record stays closed: no lasting change on the word of an answer); under no answer, an order unknown and a status still running, the message says where the end comes from, `p2pTradeEndKeptEnded`, never « over »; `TradeEndShownOtherwise` (another end, 1043, 1044: the state machine's alerts, the advice given an order made here, then `p2pTradeEndOtherwise`; that very end with other terms, `asRead`, shown as read, its panel's alert first, the advice and the line under it). The screen keeps the records' end, never a status it would act on; « Renew Order » or « Renew » then offered under the message (R7). Where the renewal is offered, the records give (`MakeOrderUsecase.renewalsOf`, its only caller `RenewalsCubit`, one per trade screen of an order made here, read only where the renewal is offered, R6): the terms kept, said with the day it was made (`p2pTradeKeptTerms`, the create form's summary, `MakeOrderRequestL10n.summary`); the orders made here after it (a fresh robot of the same Garage Key always takes a higher index, `robotForNewTrade`, so « after » is a higher index within one key, never its clock, and the time written only between two keys: security review of #220, C2, N2), on its coordinator, with that very request (`OrderRenewals`, `OrderMadeSince`, `MadeSinceStands` from the record and its stored trail; terms of such an order that cannot be read make the answer unknown, never « none », C3), said as only what is established (R1): « An order with the same terms was made here after this one: order N, {still running here | expired | cancelled | completed | ended}. » (`p2pSameTermsMade`), the newest running one named first, else the newest, with « Open order N », which opens it in place of the page; records that cannot tell, said (`p2pSameTermsUnknown`), never « none ». Renewing stays possible, one tap more, never locked: « Renew this order? » (`RenewCheckDialog`, « Go back » emphasized, « Renew anyway ») with its reasons whenever something should be known first: the end not confirmed by the read (running, no answer, unknown to the coordinator), shown otherwise or still running; an order with the same terms made since; a make of the same terms still pending (`OrderRenewals.pendingSameTerms`, said on the screen too, `p2pSameTermsPending`, security review of #220, R2); records that could not tell, or did not answer within 10 seconds of the tap (R3), whose first answer is awaited before deciding; the reference, which keeps the robot, has a second order refused while the first is active (`api/logics.py:75-100`, error 1000). The renewal's page closes for its own make only: a pending make a resolution confirms while the page is open closes it only when that order is made with the renewal's very request on its coordinator (`MakeOrderCubit.pendingStarted`, `MakeOrderUsecase.madeAsRenewal`; an earlier try of the same renewal whose answer was lost), a make unrelated to it leaving the page and the order to renew as they are (security review of #185, R3); a creation's page opens such a trade at once, as before.
