A renewal's page knows its own make sent with an outcome unknown by its robot (security review of #220, R1, N1): confirmed by a resolution, the page closes on it with no read to fail; closed as not made or set aside (`PendingMakesState.ended`, `MakeOrderCubit.pendingEnded`), the page is free again; the pending list emptying alone never frees it (`pendingCleared`); an earlier make confirmed whose terms cannot be read (`madeAsRenewal` gives `Err`, never « unrelated ») locks it, saying to go back and open the order again (`MakeUncertain.unverified`, `p2pRenewUnverified`); while that check runs, « Create order » sends nothing. No change of schema: `p2p.sqlite` stays 8. Tests: `reopen_order_route_test.dart` (the real composition, from « Last order #N » to the makes counted, each case of the end read again, 1, 6, 1043, a slow read then the honest answer, another coordinator's terms, a pending make, two quick taps, the page left open by an unrelated make), the group « the records of an ended order » of `renew_order_test.dart` (the same coordinator, the clock set back, unreadable terms after a restart, a pending make, the network, where each order made since stands), the passing refusals, the limits being read, a description alone, the screen's own guard, a quick tap, the records unknown or late, a renewal's own make confirmed unreadably in `renew_order_screen_test.dart`, `trade_end_read_test.dart`, `garage_screens_test.dart`.
- **« Last order #N » shows its order's robot, and the Garage the robot of an order made here once its bond is locked (#227).** Design note `docs/design/phase-3-making-an-order.md` § 2.2 (« As #227 wrote it »). A tap on a « Last order #N » line calls `GarageCubit.showOrderRobot` before its trade opens (`GarageProfile`, the guard of `LastOrderLines` keeping it to one switch per tap): the robot of the record when `NetworkGarageKey.robotOf` gives it back as it is (the same network, key fingerprint and index), nothing for an order of another Garage Key or network. One rule for a creation and a renewal (the owner's decisions of 2026-11-19): `FollowMadeOrdersUsecase.execute(key)` (over `ListActiveTradesUsecase.execute`, the records not closed with their restored position, and the lines' own `ListLastOrdersUsecase.execute`, the device only, registered with the trades and given to the cubit as `madeRobots`) gives first the robot to open on, which the cubit awaits when it reads the key, before any robot is shown: the newest order made here with the key, in progress (its record not closed, the complement of what the line may show, review of #229, C3), whose stored trail shows its bond locked once (`TradePosition.makerBondLocked`: public, paused or further, never at 0, 5 or 1043; the newest by index), else the first « Last order #N » line's when it is of the key (review of #229, C1, its private `_lastOrderRobot`), else the first usable robot; then the robot of each order seen locked afterwards, which the cubit shows. Since #251 (below) the rule is `GarageOpening` over `FollowTradesUsecase` (which replaced `FollowMadeOrdersUsecase`, given to the cubit as `tradesOf`), widened to trades taken here, and the arrows and the tap leave the Garage by hand in `GarageNavigation` for the run of the app, until a trade of the key ends at 14, 17 or 18 or a trade's bond is seen locked after it. One following at a time (review R3), stopped by a read of the vault, by a delete of the key (R4) and when the Garage is left. Tests: the group « « Last order #N » shows its order's robot in the Garage (#227) » of `reopen_order_route_test.dart` (the real composition, Tor heard ready by each new listener: two robots, another key, a renewal before and after its bond, two quick taps, a creation from « Create » by the form before and after its bond, the arrows, two orders, an order of another key, an order public then expired, or cancelled (since #251 the fresh robot), an order locked against a later one expired, an order succeeded against a later one expired, an order at 15 against a later one expired, the last order of another key, the requests compared with the arrows'), the group « the robot shown by the records, « Last order #N » and the hand (#227, #251) » of `garage_cubit_test.dart`, `network_garage_key_test.dart`.
- **The history of finished orders in the Garage (#253).** Design note `docs/design/phase-3-order-history.md`. An addition of Bull Market, declared as such: the reference (commit babf62e) shows one « Last order #N » per robot and no history. Decided by the owner on 2026-11-25 on the mock-up of the Garage: in the Garage, under the « Last order #N » lines, folded by default; no cap, ten lines then « See more », ten more at each tap; each line names its robot; the words accepted, what « End unknown » covers settled in writing first. `TradeRecordRepository.watchClosedTrades` (one statement over `trades` and `trade_trails`, read only, every closed record of the network with an order id and its trail's last position, `null` for no trail or one not read back as that order's: another version, network, coordinator, order or role); `OrderHistory.of` (pure: finished means closed after its order was confirmed, made or taken here, of the Garage Key shown, the « Last order #N » lines' rule; the newest `closed_at` first, then the higher order number, then the higher robot index) and `OrderEnd.of` (14 → Traded, 5 → Expired, 1043 → Cancelled, 17 and 18 → Dispute, everything else End unknown: `publicAgain` and `notAvailable`, where only this robot's own part is known to have ended, are never given an affirmative word); `ListOrderHistoryUsecase`, its only caller `OrderHistoryCubit` (one per Garage Key, made with the profile by `GarageProfile` through the factory the router gives `GarageView`, `orderHistory`; registered with the trades; it keeps the key's fingerprint, never the key; its state prints counts only), read from the profile's arrival so that the link shows only when the history holds an order the two lines do not show, drawn only once opened. `LastOrderLines` draws it under the lines (`historyKey`), with the lines' own guard (one tap opens one screen, the order's robot shown first, #227): `DetailToggle` with « See history » / « Hide history » (`p2pHistoryShow`, `p2pHistoryHide`), each line « #N · coordinator » (`p2pHistoryTitle`), side, made or taken here and the day of its end (`p2pHistoryFacts`, `P2pFormats.day` of `closed_at`, the day this device read the end), its end on the right (`p2pHistoryTraded`, `Expired`, `Cancelled` and `EndUnknown` in grey, `Dispute` in the warning colour), then « Robot #N · nickname » on a line of its own (`p2pHistoryRobotNamed`, the nickname from `NetworkGarageKey.robotOf`, derived once per index and visit; « Robot #N » alone when the key does not give it back), « See more » (`p2pHistorySeeMore`). Folded at each arrival: the Garage left, a page over it (a line tapped included) and the app hidden or paused; it lives inside the Garage's `PrivacyScreen`, so it follows the wallet's « Screen Privacy » as the Garage Key does. Records that cannot be read: `p2pRobotRecordsUnreadable`, never « no order » (no link while the lines say it). Nothing asked of a coordinator, nothing written, nothing logged; schema 9 unchanged. Tests: `order_history_test.dart` (the table of the ends, what counts as finished, the order of the lines, the statement over a database in memory with a trail missing, of another version or of another order, a new list after a write, a database that cannot be read, the cubit's pages), `order_history_route_test.dart` (the real composition, in English and French: folded on arrival, each kind of line with its robot, « See more » to the last line, no request at the opening, a line tapped twice opening its order once, folded after the order, after the app hidden and after the Garage left, no link with two lines or none, records that cannot be read, 320 points at text sizes 1, 2 and 3). Since #273: the history's values print their type only (`ClosedTrade`, `FinishedOrder`), and the state of its cubit its counts; a line says « Dispute » only when one side lost it (17, 18), a dispute the coordinator ends by giving everything back reading « Cancelled » and one it settles as a trade « Traded » (design note § 3); the robot navigator's label (`AccountNavigator`) stays on one line within the room the arrows leave, scaled down just enough when wider (`FittedBox` with `BoxFit.scaleDown` in a `Flexible`, so that a label that fits is laid out unbounded, as before, and drawn to the pixel as before), and the texts of `TradeRecordRow` (the « Last order #N » lines and the rows of « Active trades ») break between words only (`WordFitText`): at 320 points with a large text nothing overflows and no number or name is cut, the normal size unchanged. Tests added by #273: the requests at the Garage's opening equal to those of a Garage without the history (its cubit unregistered), the day shown being the end's when the take was on another day, the navigator and the « Last order #N » lines in the 320-point test (no error, no word cut, targets of 44 points at least), and their places at 360 points and the normal size pinned to those `main` drew before.
- **A fresh robot after a trade (#251).** Design note `docs/design/phase-3-fresh-robot.md`; the owner's decisions of 2026-11-25 (the reference's behaviour, no line of our own in the Garage, the reference's `p2pRobotUsedInTrade` kept; the five questions of its § 6, each as recommended). The robot of a take, a make and a renewal does not change: each reserves a robot never sent to a coordinator (`TradeRobots.robotForNewTrade`), whose computation is `NextTradeRobot.of` (the same code the Garage shows as the fresh robot, without reserving); schema 9 unchanged; no use case takes its robot from the Garage. The Garage: `GarageOpening.of` (pure, over `GarageRecords` from `FollowTradesUsecase`, the device's records only): the newest trade of the key in progress here whose bond was seen locked, made or taken here; else the first « Last order #N » line's robot when its end has not served (`OrderEnd.served`: an expiry, or `publicAgain` / `notAvailable`, a take that never started), the fresh robot when it has (14, 17, 18, 1043, an end unknown); else the first usable robot; a key pasted or acknowledged never moves (only orders made here, the line's robot whatever its end). `GarageCubit` opens on it and applies it again after each write of the records while shown; `GarageNavigation` (a lazy singleton, memory only) keeps where the arrows or a « Last order #N » tap left it for the run of the app, until a trade of the key ends at 14, 17 or 18 after it (the reference's `manualNavigationActive` gives the hand back only when the active order of the robot shown ends so, `GarageContext.tsx:128-160`: here any trade of the key, a declared deviation) or a trade of the key has its bond seen locked after it, for any move by hand, the arrows as well as the tap (for the tap #227's rule, a renewal being opened from a « Last order #N » line; for the arrows a change from #227 while the Garage is shown, where `main` kept the hand until the Garage was left; the reference does not move by itself after the arrows, `Garage.model.ts:473-521`, but is already on the trade's robot since its take and make use the robot shown; not named by the note's step 4, to confirm with the owner). Nothing is sent when the Garage moves. The panel says `p2pRobotUsedInTrade` for a last order and no active one, except a last order this device holds for that robot with an end that has not served (`GarageWithKey.ordersNotServed`). Step 0 (decision 5): when the panel of the robot the Garage opened on reads it with no active order and the device holds an open record of it, made or taken here with a key drawn here, `GarageCubit.robotWithoutActiveOrder` reads that order once per visit through `WatchTradeUsecase.readOnce` (one subscription of the watch that reads once and ends, `TradeWatchSession`'s `once`; nothing when another subscription reads the trade), whose answer is kept as the watch keeps it, the record closed at an end. The order page: `TakeOrderCubit` and `UnlistedOrderCubit` read `GarageRobotUsecase` (the Garage's robot by the same rule and navigation) before the preparation, and hand it to the use case (`garageRobot`), which keeps only whether a robot it reserves differs (`PreparedTakes.keepRobot`, `changedRobotFor`), so that every preparation of the order says the same; « A fresh robot for this order: {nickname}. » (`p2pTakeFreshRobot`, `PreparedTake.freshRobot`) under « Read by your robot at … », read out when it appears. No new dependency; nothing new logged. Tests: `garage_opening_test.dart` (§ 3 row by row, made and taken; the hand; the fresh robot equal to the next reservation over a database in memory, with « Select Host » above and below the last trade and a reservation never sent), `fresh_robot_route_test.dart` (the real composition, in English and French: after a trade, « Previous account », an expiry, a cancellation, a restart, step 0's single read), `fresh_robot_take_test.dart` (the line over the real use cases: the ordinary case, after an expiry, a trade in progress, a move by hand, « Select Host », the same verdict at a second preparation, an order by its URL), `fresh_robot_read_once_test.dart`, the group « « A fresh robot for this order » (#251) » of `take_order_section_test.dart` (320 points at text sizes 1, 2 and 3), the group of #227 and #251 of `garage_cubit_test.dart`.
- **« Existing orders match yours! » (#186).** Design note `docs/design/phase-3-making-an-order.md` § 1.11, § 2.2 (« As #186 wrote it »). `MatchingOrders` (`lib/src/domain/matching_orders.dart`, pure) is the reference's search (`basic/MakerPage/index.tsx:33-59` calling `utils/filterOrders.ts`) field by field: the type shown is the other side of the side chosen (`fav.type`, the type of the orders shown, as the book's « I want to buy » sets it), both while none is chosen; never an order whose currency is bitcoin; the form's currency; the premium typed, when a number is, keeping a sell order at it or lower and a buy order at it or higher (`filterByPremium`); the methods chosen, an order's text containing one of their names, case included (`filterByPayment`, as `BookFilter.matchesPaymentMethods`); the amount typed in « Amount », from `amount × (1 − 0.7)` to `amount × (1 + 0.7)` in floating point (so 30 is out for 100), or with « Amount Range » the range typed, from `minAmount × (1 − 0.7)` to `maxAmount × (1 + 0.7)` (`MatchingOrders.rangeThreshold`, so 15 to 340 for 50 to 200), overlapping the order's amount or range (`filterByAmount`); sorted as the book's table (the lowest premium first for sell orders, the highest for buy orders, the book's order for both, equal premiums in the book's order). A deviation from the reference, its defect corrected (#230, the owner's decision of 2026-11-19, declared in its pull request): the reference never searches a range's bounds (`filter.amount !== ''` always holds: the exact amount typed is, even hidden by « Amount Range », and none typed keeps nothing), where this search takes the range as that line is written, the hidden « Amount » not searched; bounds the form does not take (empty, no number, 0 or below, or in error by `MakerFormCheck.rangeMinError` and `rangeMaxError`) search nothing, as an exact amount none typed (`MatchingOrders.rangeOf`). The threshold around a range is the reference's 0.7, kept apart in `MatchingOrders.rangeThreshold` so that 0 makes the search the range alone, the owner's choice still open. Every order that matches is listed (review of #186, C1: the reference's table shows as many rows as its height counts and lets the others be reached by sorting a column; this list has no header), in a box at most 68 % of the screen high that scrolls by itself (`_Matches.heightShare`, the reference's `windowSize.height * 0.68`), « Create order » and « Edit order » under it, reached by dragging the page outside the box. `ActiveTradesListed.holds` leaves out every order of an open record of the network (made here, taken here, recovered), and nothing is searched while the device's trades are not listed. The create page (`P2pRouter.createPage`) gives `CreateOrderScreen` the book the tab received (`P2pCreateRouteArgs.book`, nothing read again) and an `ActiveTradesCubit` of its own (records only), for a creation only: a renewal searches nothing, as the reference renews without its create page. The screen: « Submit » (`p2pMakeSubmit`) while orders match and are not shown, which only shows them (nothing sent, nothing read, the Bull wallet's price neither), the page brought to its top; « Existing orders match yours! » (`p2pMakeMatchesFound`) and their rows (`OfferRow`, a tap opening the order's page through `P2pRouter.openMatch`, « Take Order » offered as from the book, nothing opened while the form is locked) above the form, whose fields are folded away (`collapseAll`), the coordinator, the summary with the options' line, the button and the rate with the Bull wallet's comparison staying as they are; « Create order » then goes on as before; « Edit order » (`p2pMakeEditOrder`, a pencil beside the button) unfolds the fields. Tests: `matching_orders_test.dart` (each field and bound against `filterOrders.ts`, a range against amounts and ranges at the threshold's bounds, the bounds the form refuses), the group « Existing orders match yours! » of `create_order_screen_test.dart` (real taps: « Submit », « Edit order », « Create order » after it, an order opened, nothing asked of anyone; 300 orders on a phone, the last reached by dragging the rows, the buttons by dragging the page), `matching_orders_route_test.dart` (the real composition and navigation, every request counted, an order held never offered, a range of 50 to 200 shown the order of 120 and not the one of 400), and a renewal that searches nothing in `renew_order_screen_test.dart`.
- **A maker seller's escrow from this wallet (#187): the escrow's instance of `PayBondFromWalletUsecase` at a maker seller's 6 and 7, earlier escrows not back an alert and never a lock (the owner's decision of 2026-11-19), which no screen pays before #188.** Design note `docs/design/phase-3-making-an-order.md` § 3.3, § 4.3, § 6 row 13 (« As #187 wrote it »); the study is the archive's `phase3/vente-sequestre/note-de-travail.md`. No schema change: `p2p.sqlite` stays at 8.
  No ceiling: `_offered` accepts the escrow invoice's own amount for a stored `WaitingForBoth` or `WaitingForEscrow` of a maker seller (`MakerTerms.escrowCeilingFor` and `TradeTolerances.makerEscrowFactor` removed, the owner's rule of 2026-11-17 replacing them); what still refuses is the state machine's exact agreements (`EscrowAmountMismatch`, `MakerFeeAboveAnnounced`, the invoice's checks), any warning of which forbids the wallet (`walletPaymentFor`). The rule: `rateOfBond` gives, for the escrow, `WalletRateCheck.ofMakerEscrow`, the quote's amount against the contract's amount kept in the trail (the fixed amount, or a range's amount its take chose) at the Bull wallet's price with the premium, exactly, a warning above 10 % only, nothing compared for a currency the wallet does not price or a price not read; the maker fee is not added to the sats expected (it shows in the gap, +0.025 % with the reference's default). A quote of another invoice than the escrow the trail pins is `BondPayInvoiceChanged`.
  The earlier escrows (the owner's decision of 2026-11-19, after the security review of #187: no lock, an alert): `checkBond`, `escrowContractOf` and, under the order's lock just before the payment, `payBond` read every payment recorded for the trade (`TradePaymentRepository.paymentsOfTrade`, a read, whatever the step and the contract) and, for each escrow of another contract (the bond's row left aside), the wallet's records of its hash (`P2pWalletPort.findPayment`, with or without a recorded wallet id, the id written when the wallet's differs). `EarlierEscrow.of` puts each in a case (`EarlierEscrowCase`): `back` (refunded, failed or expired with nothing sent, or no order of this feature in the wallet's records for a row without a wallet id: nothing of this feature is signed or broadcast without that order, whose record the wallet writes before its request leaves), `notBack` (a refund pending, which Bull's own code does not take as final, an order being prepared or « creating » with no limit of time, sent, pending, under review, failed or expired with funds that may have left, a recorded id the wallet does not know, records it cannot read), `kept` (paid). Its sats are what it took out of the wallet, the total of the quote this run confirmed, fees included, as the confirmation's « Pay » says, kept since schema 9 in the payment's record (`trade_payments.confirmed_sats`, written by the statement that records its start, before the wallet is asked; « Schema 9 » below), unknown only for a payment recorded before schema 9 (`EscrowAmounts`, in memory, was removed by #233). `EscrowContract` gives the sats not back yet (`notBackSats`, `notBackUnknown`), what will be out with this one (`outIfPaid`), the kept ones; no refusal comes of them. Under the lock they are read again with the quote about to be paid and more out than confirmed (`needsNewConfirmation`: a dearer quote for this escrow, more out in all, more not back, one kept since) is `BondPayConfirmAgain`, carrying the contract with that quote, the next confirmation paying. The alerts' total is the collaterals' only, the maker bond not included, and they say so. Nothing limits how many escrows of one order are out at once: N + 1 after N takes, real or pretended, each after the alert. Still refused: the exact agreements, an invoice already presented (`earlier_escrows`, another payment of the trade, or one of another trade, `TradePaymentRepository.holdsPaymentHash`: `BondPayEscrowSeenBefore`, said as such rather than as records not written), a confirmation of another contract. A payment of the same invoice by the wallet's own Send screen is not seen, as one from another wallet.
  The contract confirmed: `escrowContractOf(record, quote)` gives what the confirmation says (`EscrowContract`: the contract, a new one when above 0, each earlier escrow with its case and sats, the sums); `payBond(record, accepted:, contract:)` takes it back and, for a maker seller's escrow, refuses without it or for another contract (`BondPayContractNotConfirmed`), then reads the trail again from the records under the lock (`TradeReader.open`) and compares its `take` and pinned escrow with `WalletPaymentOffered.take` and the invoice, which closes `recordStart`'s acceptance of a new attempt of an earlier contract's escrow (an `UPDATE`, #176); `recordStart` and `recordWalletPayment` take the contract (`take: offered.take`). `followBond` follows, for a maker seller, the payment of the contract the trail stands at; `followEscrows(record)` follows every escrow recorded for the trade, contract by contract (`EscrowPaymentsWatch`), whatever the trail and whether or not the trade is open.
  The screen (#188, next item): before #188 the escrow panel gave no contract and its « Pay » was refused. Texts of ours: `p2pPayEscrowShownBefore` (a collateral invoice already presented), `p2pPayConfirmAgain` (what will leave the wallet changed since the confirmation), and the confirmation's alerts, which no screen shows before #188, composed by `earlierEscrowAlerts` (`escrow_contract_lines.dart`, tested by `escrow_contract_lines_test.dart`): `p2pEscrowEarlierOut`, `p2pEscrowEarlierOutUnknown`, `p2pEscrowEarlierOutAllUnknown`, `p2pEscrowEarlierKept`, `p2pEscrowEarlierKeptUnknown`. Tests: `maker_escrow_from_wallet_test.dart` (the maker bond paid from this wallet first, as in the owner's trial; contract 0, at 7 too, another order of the same Garage Key kept apart, a refusal of the wallet and an app killed before or after the wallet's order, the rule, a range, two taps, a second contract held back and then paid, each state, 40 pretended contracts, an app killed before the wallet, a payment hash seen before, a held escrow found under the lock, every escrow followed after the end and a restart), `maker_escrow_route_test.dart` (the real composition), `wallet_rate_check_test.dart` (the escrow's figures at the threshold).
- **Making a sell order (#188): « Sell » in the create form, a maker seller's escrow panel and confirmation, the escrows of the order's other contracts.** Design note `docs/design/phase-3-making-an-order.md` § 2.3 (« As #188 wrote it »), § 3.3, § 4.3. No schema change: `p2p.sqlite` stays at 8; nothing in `lib/core`, `lib/features/p2p_wallet`, `packages/*`.
  The form: « Sell » is a choice as « Buy » (`MakerFormBlock.sellComingLater` and `p2pMakeSellComingLater` removed), the book's side opens it (« I want to sell » opens « Sell »), the summary says « Sell BTC for … » (`MakerForm.tsx:593-594`), the kept terms of a renewal too (`MakeOrderRequestL10n.summary`). The price check warns a maker of the side that costs it (`MakePriceCheck.warnsFor`): a buyer and a form with no side both ways as before, a seller of a coordinator's price too low only (`p2pMakeRateBelowSell`: a larger bond and more sats locked for the amount), a price too high costing a seller nothing; a press made while the Bull wallet's price is read stops at a line that warns that maker (`MakePriceVerdict.stopsPressFor`). Nothing else of the form depends on the side, as in the reference (the range, the options, « Existing orders match yours! », whose search already takes the other side, the renewal, the pause).
  The confirmation of a maker seller's escrow (`_EscrowConfirmation` of `bond_payment_panel.dart` until #250, drawn when the confirmation carries a contract; since #250 the one lean card of every confirmation, `PayConfirmationCard`, below): `EscrowPaymentCubit` is given `rateOfBond`, `escrowContractOf` and `followEscrows` by the locator (`escrowContractOf` and `followEscrows` give nothing for a taker; `rateOfBond` gives a taker seller's escrow its comparison since #249, drawn in the plain confirmation); `ask` builds the contract from the very quote « Pay » pays (security review of #187, C4), then compares; `confirm` passes that contract with that quote, and only for the confirmation the tapped button was drawn for (`confirm(shown:)`); `BondPayConfirmAgain` shows the confirmation again for the same quote with the contract the refusal carries and `p2pPayConfirmAgain` (« or the state of an earlier collateral », R12), its next « Pay » paying; a read showing the order taken again while it is open says it stale (`p2pPayConfirmStale`; since #250 `p2pPayConfirmStaleTitle` and `p2pPayConfirmStaleLine`) and turns « Pay » off, its payment being refused anyway (`BondPayContractNotConfirmed`, said `p2pPayContractMoved`, R2). Blocks, as #188 drew them (since #250 the lean card's, below): the title, the stale or again line, « New contract … take n » from the second take, the amounts in rows (invoice, swap fees, network fee, total, what stays), within the threshold the comparison with the Bull wallet's price of the contract's amount beside them (`p2pPayRateWalletEscrow`, the gap said to include the coordinator's fee, #187 R1), never the gap from the creation's rate nor its price (security review of #188, C1), the swap's facts, the reference's hold-invoice sentence, then together above the buttons: beyond 10 % the comparison and `p2pPayRateWarningEscrow` as one red alert (no neutral line of the price left beside it), or the warning-colour line of a currency not priced or a price not read (with « Retry », R11), `earlierEscrowAlerts`, and, beside the alert of escrows not back, the order's bond when this wallet paid it (`p2pPayEscrowBondHeldToo`, or `…MaybeHeldToo` when the records cannot tell, C5) (functional review of #188, C2). The amount named and compared is `TradeSeen.contractAmountShown`, the trade screen's own rule of #183, one getter for both: what was asked here (the fixed amount, or a range's amount kept with the contract), never the read's alone; while the order read shows another, or the status shown carries any alert of the state machine (security review, C2), no comparison, the alert speaking alone (functional review of #188, C1); since #249 only a comparison within the threshold is left out there, a red one staying, named with the contract's amount the check carries (security review of #249, C5: an alert never silences another).
  The follow: the cubit follows every escrow first (`start`), again at each status shown and after each payment, since `followEscrows` reads the rows once; at a later contract it also follows the payment of the new contract (a payment of the earlier one no longer keeps « Pay with this wallet » away), also when the order is read taken again while a payment of the earlier contract is being sent: once it ends, the panel looks for the new contract's (functional review of #188, R4; `FakeP2pWalletPort.holdPayments` holds a payment in tests), a refusal of that payment staying said (R10). `followEscrows` gives a row whose recorded wallet id the wallet does not hold as unknown (`EscrowPaymentOfContract.unknownToWallet`, R6). `EarlierEscrowLines` (`ui/trade/earlier_escrow_lines.dart`, `TradeScreen.walletEarlierEscrows`) lists the escrows of the order's other contracts, whatever the view, a wait and the trade's end included (the current contract from the trail `TradeCubit` holds, security review R3): « Take n: … » in the three cases' words, « state unknown » counted not back, and in red an escrow whose refund was pending and is then held or kept again (`BondPaymentState.refundsUndone`, C2).
  The trade after the take, checked status by status for a maker seller (the reference's role-based panels, phase 2's for a taker seller): 6 and 7 the escrow, 8 « The trade collateral is locked! » with a line of ours under the reference's « you will get back the trade collateral and your bond » (`p2pTradePayoutWaitMaker`: for a maker seller whose buyer gives no payout, the collateral comes back, the order goes back to the book with its bond locked, `api/logics.py:445-458`), and the line of a payment through the swap service for the collateral only (`WalletBondState.escrowOnly`); 9 and 10 the chat and « Confirm … received » with the reference's dialog, sent once; 13 and 15 « Trade finished! » with « Renew »; the dispute's, the cancel's and the expiry's as before. `ScriptedMakerCoordinator` plays a maker seller's steps (`lockEscrow` for either seller, `buyerGivesInvoice`, `buyerSentFiat`, the seller's `confirm` at 10 and its 1027 at 9, `sellerConfirmations`); it does not play the chat (`/api/chat/`), so a maker seller's messages, export, dispute and collaborative cancel from the chat's options and the refusal 1028 are not tested.
  Not done: the earlier escrows' sats after a restart (still « at least »): Bull's swap record keeps the payin (`OrderSwapRecord.quotedCounterpartAmountSat`, `OrderSwap.payinAmountSat`) but not the network fee, so the quote's total the confirmation counts cannot be read back; a later ticket. Tests: `maker_sell_route_test.dart` (the real composition by gestures, English and French: « Create », « Sell », the bond and two collaterals paid with this wallet, the alert, a confirmation made again, « Confirm … received » with two taps; a confirmation left open while the order is taken again; the order taken again while a payment is being sent; two taps on « Pay »; two presses of « Confirm … received » within one frame (the panel's guard: a second real tap is absorbed by the navigator until the next frame) and two calls of the cubit (its `actionRunning`); beyond 10 %, the alert above the buttons; a refund pending held again; a payment the wallet does not know; « at least » after the sats are forgotten; the form's price check of a seller; the renewal of a sell order at 13, by gestures), `bond_payment_cubit_test.dart`, `escrow_contract_test.dart` (R11), `maker_form_test.dart`, `create_order_screen_test.dart`, `maker_escrow_route_test.dart` (the contract's amount named: a range's amount taken, a fixed amount and a range's read otherwise while the confirmation is open), `trade_locator_wiring_test.dart`.
- **The order password and the meeting point (#189): « Password » and « Face to Face Location » of the create form.** Design note `docs/design/phase-3-making-an-order.md` § 2.4 (« As #189 wrote it »), § 1.8. The package: `MakeOrderRequest.tryCreate(location:, password:, keptWithPassword:)`, `OrderPasswordHash.of(text)` (the SHA-256 of the UTF-8 bytes in lowercase hexadecimal, as `js-sha256` writes it, `MakerForm.tsx:273`, and as the official client's taker sends the password it types, `OrderDetails/TakeButton.tsx:333`; any text but the empty one, neither trimmed nor bounded, as the reference's field), the body's `latitude`, `longitude` and `password`, the `201` compared with the point sent (texts of 6 decimals); two requests are equal whatever the hash, `hasPassword` compared; `MakeOrderRequest.kept` drops the hash and is not sendable (`isSendable`), which `makeOrder` refuses before sending. **The password is never kept**: its text lives in the form's field only; `MakerTerms.create` keeps `request.kept`, the record `has_password` only (schema 8 unchanged), `MakerTerms.restore` gives a request that says it has a password, not sendable; `MakeOrderUsecase.execute` refuses a request that is not sendable before anything (`MakeTermsRefused`) and sends the request it was given, equal to the terms'; `MakeOrderCubit.make` sends nothing that is not sendable; nothing logs, prints or stores the hash (`toString`s redacted). The state machine compares `has_password` and the meeting point at every read of a maker (`TradeTermsChanged`; `api/views.py:256`, `:320-321`). The form: « Password » in the advanced options, typed hidden with the reference's tooltip, kept and sent while the options are closed (the summary's line of the options sent names « a password »); once typed, one line (out of the book, taken with its link and the password: since #245 this app takes it too, « Search order »), and under « See details » the reference's help, how to share the order (« Copy URL »), who can take it, what stays readable of it by its number (`api/views.py:287-299`), that only the hash leaves and is not kept; `PasswordExposure.of(CoordinatorInfo?)` gives an alert in one short line with the version, in the warning colour, never a lock, for a coordinator whose `/api/info/` version is older than 0.8.7 (`GHSA-r6f6-x59j-8q69`) or could not be read, its explanation under « See details »; under « Password » while the field is in the form, near the summary while a password is sent and the field is not (options closed, fields folded away by #186), never both (independent review of #189, C1). « Face to Face Location »: a button with the reference's tooltip, opening `LocationMapDialog` (« Choose a location », the book's map drawn by `MapChartPainter.paintGround` and `paintPin`, `MapZoomButtons`; « Show tiles » as on the book's map since #246, off in a new form and kept on or off while the form is; no position of the device read), also opened by itself when the last method chosen holds « F2F » (`MakerForm.tsx:213-217`); under the map « Sent to the coordinator; published with the order unless it has a password. » (`api/nostr.py:28-30`); « Save » keeps the place pinned moved by `MeetingPoint.randomized` (`F2fMap.tsx` `onSave`: a draw × 0.1 − 0.05 on each axis, the latitude first, then 6 significant digits, `handleAddLocation`), held within ±90° and ±180° and at most 6 decimals (deviations), with `MeetingPointDraws` (`Random.secure` in the app, the test's in `setUpP2pForTesting(locationDraws:)`), and puts « Cash F2F » first among the methods (once, where the reference can put it twice); the form shows « Point sent: … », the point the order sends, with a cross that removes it, and under « See details » the reference's line on the randomization and what is published; the point goes with the last face-to-face method, as in the reference; the map opens again on the point sent. The renewal of an order that had a password: « Password » the only field open, « Enter a password again » under it in the colour of an error and under « Create order », nothing sent while it is empty, its SHA-256 sent (the reference sends the text typed, `TradeBox/index.tsx:180`, and nothing at 13 to 15, its two defects of § 1.8); the meeting point kept is sent again as it is. Explanations on demand (the owner's rule of 2026-11-20): one short line by a field, the rest behind « See details », an alert in one line. Tests: `make_order_test.dart` (package), `meeting_point_test.dart`, `maker_form_password_test.dart`, `maker_terms_test.dart`, `maker_terms_bounds_test.dart`, `maker_contracts_test.dart`, `make_order_usecase_test.dart`, and `maker_password_location_route_test.dart` (a protected order made, its renewal at 5 and at 14, a meeting point, by real gestures on the real composition, in English and in French) and `password_alert_route_test.dart` (the alert under the field, then near the summary with the options closed, for a version older than 0.8.7 or unreadable, and with the fields folded away).
- **The trade use cases (#87): take, watch, cancel, list.** `lib/src/domain/usecases/trade/`: `TakeOrderUsecase`, `WatchTradeUsecase`, `CancelTradeUsecase`, `ListActiveTradesUsecase`, and `trade_reader.dart` (`TradeReader`, what they share: opening a recorded trade, reading its order, keeping each answer) and `trade_watch_session.dart` (one subscription of the watch); their values and outcomes are in `lib/src/domain/trade/` (`trade_take.dart`, `trade_watch.dart`, `trade_cancel.dart`, `active_trade.dart`, `trade_cadence.dart`). They are registered by `P2pLocator.registerTrades` with the database, under the Garage Key handle; the cubits of the trade screens (#88: `TakeOrderCubit`, `TradeCubit`, `ActiveTradesCubit`) are their only callers. The payout (#90: `SubmitPayoutUsecase`, `HoldTradeKeyUsecase`, `TradeKeyring`) is registered there too, and no screen calls it yet (the payout panel is #91): « The buyer's payout use case and the trade keyring (#90) » below.
  One take of an order at a time and one open trade per order, in three layers (review of #117): the take refuses an order that has an open trade here with `TakeOrderAlreadyTraded(record)` before anything is sent; the takes of one order run in turn in the process (`TradeTakeLocks`, a lazy singleton of the composition, per order so that a slow coordinator holds back no other order); and the trigger `trades_one_open_per_order` of schema 4 refuses a second open trade of an order, whatever the code and across connections, before the take leaves (a trigger rather than a unique index, so that the step from schema 3 cannot fail on a file holding two: it keeps them and refuses a third).
  The take, in this order, each step only once the one before it succeeded: the order read again, from the coordinator's book of its currency and type and `/api/info/` (the taker fee), both anonymous, just before the take (`TradeTerms.take` of that read: never a book left open, and a take refused there costs no robot, in `execute` as in `prepare` of #119); refused with a typed `TakeRefusal` when the order is gone or changed since the order's page (its terms, or its sats at the current price by more than `TradeTolerances.price`: the page is what the user accepted, the reference having no other confirmation), the amount does not fit (`TakeAmount`, rounded to the coordinator's 8 decimals, within the range of that read), or the terms are refused (`TakerFeeTooHigh`, unusable); a buy order is taken as a sell order is, its taker the seller (#96); a fresh robot from `TradeRobots.robotForNewTrade`, put on the coordinator by `PutRobotOnCoordinatorUsecase` (read back, then its first contact), refused when the coordinator already knew it with another pair or with an order, or when the Garage Key no longer gives it (`TradeRobots.robotOfSlot`: the key deleted or replaced meanwhile, whose trade could never be read again; review of #126); the pending take (`recordPendingTake`), its terms (`TradeTrailRepository.recordTerms`) and the first trail of a pending take (`TradeTrail.forPendingTake`, `storeTrail`), in that order, the take refused (and the pending take closed) when any write fails; the take, sent once through the robot's places of `RobotRequestLimiter` (a busy transport, which sends nothing, is tried again as the limiter says); then `OrderTakeStarted` → `TradeStatus.afterTake`, its trail stored and the record confirmed (`TakeStarted`, with `recordProblem` when a write after the answer failed: the trade runs all the same; when its trail could not be stored, the status returned is the stored pending trail's, `StatusUnknown`, with no invoice); `OrderAlreadyTakenByThisRobot` → resumed from the recorded trail (`TakeResumed`); `OrderNotTaken` or a refusal before the action → the pending take closed (`TakeNotMade`); an outcome unknown → the pending take stays (`TakeOutcomeUnknown`) and the watch resolves it. What a kill leaves: before the pending take, a robot bound to the coordinator without a trade, never used again, and no take sent; after it and before its first trail, a pending take that never left, which the watch closes at once with no read (`TradeWatchTakeNeverSent`); after the request left or after its answer, a pending take whose trail the watch resumes, the same robot, never a second take.
  The watch: `execute(record, signals:, onScreen:)` gives a stream of `TradeWatchState`: `TradeWatchStatus` (a `TradeSeen`, the status and the order it was read from, the count of failed reads in a row and the last failure, `recordProblem`, `takePending`), `TradeWatchUnrestorable` (a refused record: `TradeRecordMalformed`, `TradeRecordOfAnotherTrade`, `TradeCoordinatorGone`; shown read-only, nothing sent), `TradeWatchUnavailable` (no records, no key, a binding that refuses the coordinator), `TradeWatchTakeNeverSent` (closed at once), `TradeWatchTakeNotMade` (a take never held: no pre-take, so no invoice, was ever seen; never « bond may have been lost »), `TradeWatchSuperseded` (a newer subscription took the trade over: this stream ends; not an error). Only a status marked `stored` (`TradeSeen.stored`, `TradeWatchStatus.stored`, `TakeStarted.stored`, `TakeResumed.stored`) is the one a read computed and stored with its trail; when the trail of a read cannot be stored, or another write kept it out, the status is the trail on disk's own (`StatusUnknown` or an end, no invoice, no order), unmarked, with `recordProblem` when a write failed. The trade is opened only from its stored terms and trail (a pending take without them never sent its request, a recovered trade without a trail starts on `TradeTrail.recovered`, any other is refused); the network and the coordinator of every read come from the record, checked against the robot's binding (`RobotBinding.admits`). It reads at once, then at the reference's cadence (`TradeCadence`: `GarageContext.tsx:38-60`, ×5 off-screen `:135`, on its tick of 5 s `:136`, `:160-169`; 3 s → 5 s at the pre-take, 8 s → 10 s at 6-8, 10 s at 9, 10, 13, 30 s at 15, 100 s at 11, 300 s at 16; it stops at a terminal position, as the reference stops at 4, 5, 12, 14, 17, 18, `Slot.model.ts:216-223`), at most one read in flight, at once on `TradeWatchSignal.readNow` (or right after the read in flight); nothing runs in the background, and a cancelled subscription leaves no timer and starts no read, a read whose answer comes after it is dropped, and the writes of an answer already being kept finish (the stored trail stays correct). After each read: the answers of a trade are kept in the order the coordinator served them (#141, review of #142). The request limiter lets two requests of a robot be in flight, the coordinator may serve them in either order, the edges allow 10 → 9, and an answer read along no edge is recorded as a breach of trust; so every request that gives a status (the watch's reads; the take's, the cancel's, the bond payment's, the payout's and the chat step's reads and requests) is ranked twice by `TradeReadOrder`, inside the closure handed to the limiter, at each attempt: when it really leaves and when its answer arrives (`TradeReader.request`, `readOrder(times:)`, `timed`, `RequestTimes`; a rank of the process, not the clock). An answer replaces the stored status only if its request left after the stored status's answer arrived: two requests whose flights overlap cannot be ordered, so the later answer of such a pair is superseded, whichever left first; the claim is made in `TradeReader.keep`, through which every status is stored, before anything is awaited. A superseded answer writes nothing and gives the newest status stored in this process, with the furthest record known, the step marked `TradeStep.superseded` (and not `stored`: no use case acts on it as its own read; not a failed write either). The watch sends no read while an action of the trade runs (`TradeReader.duringAction`, around the coordinator requests of the take, the cancel, the bond payment's read, the payout and the chat step, each under the order's lock; checked inside the limiter's closure, when a place is held): a tick that finds one sends nothing and waits for the next, and a read the screen asked for (`readNow`) is sent once the action ends. So the only overlap left is a watch's read that left before an action began. A read of the watch that fails, or says nothing of the trade, keeps the last status, unless a step of the trade (an action's fresh read before sending, its read after) stored one since the watch's own, which the watch never read itself: the watch then stands at that newest status stored in this process, an end and a closed record included (`TradeReader.newestStored`, from `TradeReadOrder.newestStored`, with the furthest record known), goes on from it (its cadence, its next read) and, at an end, ends as after a read of that end; so a refusal the screen shows with that fresh read (« too early », « the trade moved on », to an end too) is not undone by a failed read (reviews of #161). It keeps its own while a newer answer is claimed and not stored, when the status stored is of another trade of the same robot, and when that status's trail was read before the watch's own by this device's clock (for the same reason as the return to the app's check, #152, but a weaker test: it compares the two read times only, where #152 requires the same position and the same read time; a trail on disk newer than the status kept in memory wins; a clock set back may so keep the older status, as before #161). What each does with a superseded answer: the watch says nothing (the next tick reads; an end is said); an action whose fresh read is superseded reads once more, which cannot be superseded again (if it were, it refuses with « could not read the order just now », never « the order moved on »); an action's own answer superseded is judged from itself only, and its read after, which leaves after everything arrived, stores the status. Every answer accepted left after the previous accepted one arrived: accepted answers are in the coordinator's order, so no stale answer replaces a newer status and no race records a breach, in either order of a pair. What it costs: a status can be shown one read later than it could have been. The memory is in this process only (a request in flight lives in it; every request of a run leaves after what an earlier run stored), one entry per trade, never pruned: bounded by the trades of a run. otherwise `TradeStatus.from(previous: last status, …)`, a read that is no `TradeAnswer` keeping the last status; the trail stored by `TradeTrailRepository.storeTrail`, which asks `whyNotReplace` of the stored trail first and writes only over the very trail it compared (`TrailKept` otherwise: the stored trail wins; a read newer than the stored trail is then read again over it and stored, once; at an end the stored trail holds, the status read stays, which says what the end means; otherwise the stream emits from the stored trail); the record confirmed when the answer shows the robot as the taker (a `TradeClosedFailure` is then kept on every status, never dropped), closed at a terminal status whose trail is stored; a pending take never confirmed, with no pre-take ever seen, that a read brings to an end is closed as `TradeWatchTakeNotMade`.
  The cancel: `execute(record, seen, {bondLoss})` with the `TradeSeen` the user saw: free at 3 (the guard sent is 1, the stored status, as `CoordinatorClient.cancelOrder` computes it); at 6 and 7 the bond is lost and the cancel is sent only with `BondLossAcknowledged.fromConfirmationDialog(record, seen)`, bound to that trade and that status seen, which the screen builds from its confirmation dialog alone (`CancelLosesTheBond` without, `CancelAcknowledgementMismatch` for another trade or status); at 8 and 9 `CancelOnlyTogether` (a later slice); `CancelNotPossible` elsewhere, `CancelNeedsARead` when the status comes from no order. Sent once through the limiter, then the order read again and kept: `CancelDone`, `CancelNotDone` (nothing done: 1020 when the bond locked meanwhile, a busy transport), `CancelOutcomeUnknown` (the watch resolves it).
  The list: `ListActiveTradesUsecase.execute(network)`, the active records joined with their stored terms and trail (`ActiveTrade`: `ActiveTradeAt` with the position, deadline, breach and unknown history; `ActiveTradeTakeNeverSent`; `ActiveTradeUnrestorable`), a new list after each write, a `P2pStorageFailure` in place of the list, never an empty one.
  Schema 4: `trade_terms` (every field of `TradeTermsRecord`) and `trade_trails` (every field of `TradeTrailRecord`), keyed as `trades` with a foreign key to it, CHECKs on every column (bounds, enumerated names, real numbers that are finite, lowercase hexadecimal hashes, the pre-take seen no later than the read), written only by `TradeTrailRepositoryImpl`, one statement each; the CHECKs also pin each column's storage class (`typeof`), so that no value reads back as a record this code never wrote; the triggers of `trade_trails.drift` refuse any update or delete of the terms, a second insert of either (`INSERT OR REPLACE` ignored), terms or a trail of no open trade or of another trade (a trail also needs its terms), any delete of a trail, and any update of a trail that changes its trade or moves back its version, its read, its breach, its history, a pinned hash, its pre-take, or leaves an end; an update of the trail of a closed trade is ignored (one that goes back is refused instead, the forward trigger firing first: either way nothing changes). Only `whyNotReplace` says that the position moves along the edges of the trade. Payments are not here: the pull request of « Pay with this wallet » adds `trade_payments`.
  Rules for #88: the trade screen calls `TakeOrderUsecase` from the order's page (with the `PublicOrder` it showed, a `TakeAmount` and an `OrderPassword` it built), and opens the trade of `TakeOrderAlreadyTraded.record` rather than take again; `WatchTradeUsecase` while the trade is shown, one cubit, so one subscription, per shown trade (signals `onScreen`/`offScreen`, `readNow` on the return of the app, the subscription cancelled when the app is hidden or paused; a newer subscription takes the trade over at once, the earlier stream ending with `TradeWatchSuperseded`, which is not an error to show); `CancelTradeUsecase` with the last `TradeSeen` it showed (and a `BondLossAcknowledged` built for that record and that `TradeSeen`, only in the confirmation of a cancel at 6 or 7: `robot_bindings_architecture_test.dart` refuses it anywhere today, and the screen's pull request adds its one file there); and `ListActiveTradesUsecase` for « Active trades »; it shows each state and refusal with a text of its own, never a coordinator's; it shows an invoice, or offers a payment, only from a status marked `stored` (`TradeWatchStatus.stored`, `TakeStarted.stored`, `TakeResumed.stored`, `TradeSeen.stored`): an unmarked status holds no invoice anyway (the trail on disk's own), and `recordProblem` alone does not decide it, since a failed confirmation or close and a `TradeClosedFailure` set it on a status that is stored; the amount shown under the QR is the invoice's own decoded amount, which is what the user pays, the order's page showing an estimate: the take accepts the fresh read's sats within `TradeTolerances.price` (2 % + 1 sat) of the page's, and the state machine accepts the bond within as much of the terms, so a bond can be about 4 % above the page's estimate (the wallet's path is bound by `accepted`, the QR's is not); a take reported « not made » (`TradeWatchTakeNotMade`) can still leave, at the coordinator, a pre-take of a robot that is never used again, whose invoice is never shown: the user may take the order again, with another robot; it never retries a take or a cancel, never calls a coordinator member, a repository or the limiter itself, and pays only as « Rules for the screens (#88) » above say. `robot_bindings_architecture_test.dart` pins that a `TradeWatchRegistry` (a lazy singleton of the composition) lets the newest subscription of a trade take it over, the earlier one superseded once its read in flight has ended, so that a trade never has two reads in flight; only `take_order_usecase.dart` names `TradeStatus.afterTake`, `TradeTrail.forPendingTake` and `TradeTrailRepository.recordTerms`, only it and `trade_reader.dart` name `storeTrail`, only the trade use cases and the composition name `TradeReader` and its members, only `trade_reader.dart` names `TradeTrail.recovered` and `fetchOrder`, only the take `takeOrder` and only the cancel `cancelOrder`, each trade use case that sends a request holds the limiter's requests, and only `TradeTrailRepositoryImpl` writes the two tables, its statements pinned as written and `whyNotReplace` asked before either trail write; `trade_money_calls_architecture_test.dart` keeps the money members in the trade use cases' files.
- **The trade screen (#88): take an order as buyer, lock the bond from another wallet, cancel.** The screens follow the official client (`components/OrderDetails/TakeButton.tsx`, `basic/OrderPage/index.tsx`, `components/TradeBox/` at commit ff1998c) in the wallet's look; the reference's flow, file and line, is in the pull request of #88.
  « Take Order » (`lib/src/ui/take_order_section.dart`, cubit `TakeOrderCubit`) on the order details: for a range order the amount first (« Amount {{currencyCode}} », « Too low » / « Too high » once typed, « Take Order » disabled with « You must specify an amount first » (shown on a tap, as the reference shows it on touch) until it fits, then « You will receive {{satoshis}} Sats (Approx) », computed as the reference's take button does: `TakerEstimate.takeSats`, the taker fee as a positive fee and no routing budget); on press « You do not have a Garage Key » when the vault says there is none, else the take is prepared (#119: the robot that will take the order reads it; the button shows it runs, a bar runs under it with a line for each step and, after 30 s, « Still waiting for … »), the order details then show that read (see « The take in two steps (#119) »), « Continue » opens « Store your garage key », whose « Done » sends the take once; « Go back » of the order details abandons it, while « Go back » of « Store your garage key », or a tap outside it, only closes the dialog, as the reference's does (`TakeButton.tsx:380-382`), the order as read staying on the page (a second step while one runs, or after the take was sent, does nothing); a take sent, or refused because the order already has an open trade here (`TakeOrderAlreadyTraded`), replaces the page by that trade (`pushReplacementNamed`, as the reference's order page becomes the trade at the same address), a take that did not happen says why under the button in words of the app (`TakeProblemL10n`; for a coordinator's error code, the reference's message for that code from the feature's translations). Without the vault's handle the feature holds no robot and a press shows « You do not have a Garage Key ». `TakeOrderSection` is the only caller of `TakeOrderCubit.take`, `confirm` and `back`, on the order details and on the trade screen alike (pinned by `trade_screen_architecture_test.dart`), and the composition the only caller of the use case's steps (`robot_bindings_architecture_test.dart`).
  The trade screen (`lib/src/ui/trade/trade_screen.dart`, route `/p2p/trade` with `P2pTradeRouteArgs`, cubit `TradeCubit`, one per shown trade, closed with its route): the reference's phone order page, the tabs « Order » / « Contract », « Contract » first. `TradeView.of` (`lib/src/presentation/trade_panel.dart`) maps what the watch gives to the screen, the conditions written once there: a spinner before anything is understood; the coordinator's message alone for 1043 (« This order has been cancelled ») and 1044 (« This order is not available », also while the pre-take is unavailable and once taken by another); the order, as to a robot that is no participant, when it is public again (after the pre-take, or after the bond was forfeited), with « Take Order » as the reference offers it (`components/OrderDetails/index.tsx:566-578`): `TradeOrderPageCubit.findPublic` looks for the order in its coordinator's book once per order, and `TakeOrderSection` takes the order as the book lists it with a fresh robot, the take rules of #87 allowing a new take of an order whose earlier trade here is over (tested after a free cancel and after an expired pre-take); once the book lists it, the order shown is the book's (`PublicOrderDetailsCard`, the order details' own card), so that what is shown and what is taken are one order; an order the book no longer lists says « This order is not available », and a book that could not be read « Coordinator offline » with « Retry », which reads it once per press (nothing retries by itself); a take refused because the order's trade here is the very one shown (its close could not be written: `TakeOrderAlreadyTraded` of the shown record, `TakeOrderCubit.shownTrade`) says « The order was not taken. » instead of opening that trade over itself; a line of the app with no action for what the reference has no screen for (a take whose answer is unknown, a take never sent, a take that did not happen, « The order was not taken. », records refused or unreadable, the key missing, a coordinator gone); otherwise a contract: the reference's title with its step (`stepXofY`), the warnings of the state machine (each a text of the app, `TradeWarningL10n`), the prompt, the bond line (`TradeContract.bondLineOf`) and « Copy URL » / « Cancel » at 3, 6 and 7 only (`TradeContract.cancelOf`). When the watch gives the stored trail's own status (`StatusUnknown`: a trade opened again from its stored trail, on the app's return or on a new screen, its first read not in or failing) with no record problem, `TradeCubit` shows instead the newest status this run stored for the trade (`WatchTradeUsecase.lastStored`, #152), only when that status is marked stored and the stored trail is its own (same position, same read time) and no newer answer was accepted without being stored (a write that failed), with what the watch says of its reads (no record problem carried over), so that the screen does not fall back to its wait; otherwise the wait, as before; a `StatusUnknown` at an end with nothing to keep says « This trade is over for this robot. » (1043 and 1044 their own messages). The « Order » tab is the reference's order details for a participant (`trade_order_details.dart`): host and number, the coordinator's fees (`OrderFeeRow`), the details with « You receive … Sats (Approx) » estimated as the reference does (`TakerEstimate.ofTrade`, the taker fee counted against the coordinator and the routing budget deducted) until the coordinator gives `invoice_amount` or `trade_satoshis`, the countdown, then the maker and how recently its coordinator saw it (`OrderMakerRow`); with no order read yet it shows the contract's title, never an endless wait. What it reads besides the trade (`TradeOrderPageCubit`, `presentation/trade_order_page_cubit.dart`, the only caller of `FindPublicOrderUsecase`): `/api/info/` of the trade's coordinator once when the trade's page opens (`FetchCoordinatorInfoUsecase`, as the order details of the book read it), and, only once the trade shows the order public again, that coordinator's `/api/book/` for the order's currency and type, once per order; both anonymous, through the coordinator transport's own Tor connection and its anonymous session, never with the trade's robot, never on a timer, nothing while the page is closed. Above the panel, a line when three reads in a row failed (the last status kept, with the time it was read) and when a write of the records failed (`TradeClosedFailure` has its own).
  Status 3 (`bond_panel.dart`; the amount under the QR code is the invoice's own decoded amount, what the user pays, where the order's page shows an estimate: the two can differ by a few percent, the tolerances of the take and of the state machine adding up to about 4 %): « You are BUYING BTC » (« SWAPPING INTO Lightning » for an order in bitcoin, the reference's text, `LockInvoice.tsx:78`; « SWAP VERS Lightning » in French, the project's), the slot of « Pay with this wallet », then « Pay with another wallet », which holds « See Compatible Wallets », the QR code of the invoice in capitals, the facts read from it (amount, expiry, network, description) and « Copy to clipboard », then the hold-invoice text. Only a status marked `stored` (`TradeSeen.stored`) offers anything payable (`TradeContract.payable`, decided once in `TradeView.of`: otherwise no wallet slot, no QR code, no copy), whatever `recordProblem` says, and the line above the panel says the records could not be written; `TradeCubit` keeps an earlier status only as said above (#152): marked stored, over the stored trail's own status with the same position and read time, no failed write since, no record problem carried over. The QR code and the copy follow `invoiceDisplayOf`: never for an `UnusableInvoice`, behind « Show anyway » only for the amount checks, not at all for the other warnings that hide it; « Show anyway » holds for one payment hash. The screen's clock, which moves every second, hides the QR code, the copy and the wallet's slot as soon as it passes the invoice's `payableUntil` (`invoiceExpiredAt`), with the line « This invoice has expired. », without waiting for a read that may not come (design note § 4.4). The payout prompt of 6 and 8 writes the amount with at most 4 decimals, as the reference's `toFixed(4)`, and an order in bitcoin in sats with « Sats ». The panels of 6 and 8 show the reference's title and prompt and then the payout panel (#91, « The payout panel (#91) » below), given to `TradeScreen(payout:)` by the route; 15 the reference's title and the payout panel's failed payout; 7 the reference's wait; 9 and later (15 aside) the reference's title and « Bull Market cannot show this step yet. »; 5 « The order has expired » with the label of its reason.
  Cancel: at 3 the reference's `CancelOrder` dialog, at 6 and 7 its `ConfirmCancel`; `ConfirmCancelDialog` (`ui/trade/confirm_cancel_dialog.dart`), opened for one record and one `TradeSeen`, is the only place that builds `BondLossAcknowledged.fromConfirmationDialog()`, on « Confirm Cancel » alone; « Go back », a system back or a tap outside send nothing. « Cancel » opens one dialog at a time, and cancels with the `TradeSeen` shown when it was pressed, never the one shown when the dialog closes (an agreement given at 3 is not one for 6). A status kept from no order (an answer the state machine refused as a whole keeps the last status with `order: null`) opens no dialog: « Cancel » asks the cancel, which `CancelTradeUsecase` refuses with `CancelNeedsARead` (« Wait until the order is read again before cancelling. »), nothing sent, as the rules of #87 above say (`CancelNeedsARead` when the status comes from no order); so `ConfirmCancelDialog` is never opened for a status `BondLossAcknowledged` cannot be bound to. In `ConfirmCancel`, « Go back » is the filled button and « Confirm Cancel » the outlined one, in the reference's order (« Go back » first, the reference's focused button). `TradeCubit.cancel` takes the `TradeSeen` the screen showed, runs once at a time, shows what `CancelTradeUsecase` gave (the read after the cancel, and a text for a refusal, a cancel not done or an outcome unknown), then asks the watch to read now.
  The watch: `TradeCubit.start` listens to `WatchTradeUsecase` once (a stream superseded by a newer watch of the trade ends with `TradeWatchSuperseded`, which the cubit does not show: it stops, keeping what it showed); the screen tells it `offScreen` / `onScreen` from the ticker mode of its route (another tab of the shell shown), `readNow` when the app comes back from a short absence (inactive), and stops the subscription when the app is hidden or paused (`suspend`), listening again on its return (`resume`, which reads at once, but not once the status shown is an end, as the reference stops reading at an end: the screen keeps what it shows; the screen's clock is moved on first, so an invoice that expired meanwhile is not drawn); closing the cubit cancels it. A trade opened anew in the same run (on the app's return, or a new screen of it after another tab or « back ») keeps, while its first read is in flight or failing, the newest status this run stored for it (`WatchTradeUsecase.lastStored`, from `TradeReadOrder`, memory only, never for an ended trade or another trade, robot or order), only when that status was marked stored, the stored trail the watch opens is its own (same position and read time) and no newer answer was accepted without being stored (a write that failed): silently, as the reference keeps the order it shows while it reads it again (`OrderPage/index.tsx:76-96`); nothing is sent from it alone: « Cancel » sends that status's guard (`cancel_status`, refused by the coordinator for another status), as the reference cancels from the order it holds (`TradeBox/index.tsx:244-253`), and the other actions read the order first as always (#152). The screen names no use case, client, repository, limiter or wallet member: `trade_screen_architecture_test.dart` pins that, that each trade use case is named by its cubit, the domain and the locator only, that only the confirmation dialog names `fromConfirmationDialog`, that only `TakeOrderSection` calls `TakeOrderCubit.take`, and that the trade's screens open no link and use the clipboard only for the invoice (the bond panel), the order's address (« Copy URL », which says « Copied! » as « Copy to clipboard » does), and the copies the reference makes at the end (the transaction id, the payout's address and the trade summary, #95), besides the chat's. The cubits take the use cases' `execute` (the locator gives them), so that the widget tests play them.
  Reaching a trade: the middle entry of the tab, « Create » (disabled), becomes « Order » while `ActiveTradesCubit` (`ListActiveTradesUsecase`, the device's records only, nothing asked of a coordinator) lists an active trade of the network or cannot read the records, as the middle button of the reference's phone bar becomes the order of the robot that has one; with one trade it opens it, with several (a robot each) a sheet of « Active order #{{orderID}} » lines (`ActiveTradesSheet`), and with unreadable records the sheet says so. Reopening the app, the entry is there at once; nothing is read from a coordinator until a trade is opened.
  The slot of « Pay with this wallet » (`ui/trade/wallet_payment_section.dart`): `TradeScreen(walletPayment:)` takes a `WalletPaymentBuilder`, called at each build of the bond panel with a `WalletPaymentRequest` (the trade's record, the `WaitingForTakerBond` the screen shows, which may be one the `TradeCubit` kept from an earlier read when the watch gave a `StatusUnknown` at the same position, never one the slot keeps, and the bond of the title, `bond_satoshis`), which returns what the wallet offers, a `WalletPaymentSlot` (its widget, and `othersOpen` when « Pay with another wallet » is to open now, after a refusal), or `null`; the request also carries the status as the screen shows it, `seen`, marked stored; with no builder (no wallet's port lent) or `null`, the slot draws nothing, offers no button, and « Pay with another wallet » opens at once; nothing is offered once the screen's clock passed the invoice's `payableUntil`. The payment pull request fills it, under the rules of « Rules for the screens (#88) » above, and one more: the ceiling the wallet pays is the decoded amount of the checked invoice (`CheckedInvoice`, through `walletPaymentFor`), bounded by the take's terms, never `bond_satoshis`, a figure of the coordinator that the confirmation only states.
  Screen capture: not protected, as the design note decides for the invoice panels (§ 4.3: a bond invoice is public, and the user may need a capture of it); « Store your garage key » shows no key, so it needs no protection either.
  Texts: the reference's English and French where they exist, the design note's French for the titles the reference leaves untranslated, the project's otherwise; buttons whose labels wrap (`TradeButton`, `TradeDialogBody`), since `BullButton` cuts a long label at three times the text size (moving such a variant to `bull_ui` is a follow-up). For a screen reader: the contract's title is read out when the status changes, not each line of the panel; the lines that say what an action gave (a cancel's notice, a take's problem, « Still waiting for … ») are read out when they appear, « You will receive … Sats (Approx) » is not read at each keystroke, and the bar of a take in progress is labelled by the button pressed, « Take Order » or « Continue » (#119). The QR code is drawn with `qr_flutter`, the package of the wallet's own `QrDisplayWidget`, declared by the feature (no new package in the lock).
  Deviations from the reference, each with its reason: « Pay with this wallet » above « Pay with another wallet », which holds the reference's QR code and copy (the owner's request of 2026-11-13; « You are BUYING BTC » stays above both, the hold-invoice text under both); the facts under the QR code, no QR code for an unusable invoice, « Show anyway » and the warnings (security, design note § 4.4); « Store your garage key » without the key (security: the key is shown only in the Garage, behind the PIN); the order's details open without a robot (#119, the owner's decisions of 2026-11-14 and, after the review of #133, of 2026-11-15), the reference's order of the take kept (`TakeButton.tsx:137-153`, `:370-387`): « Take Order » → for a robot already used, its dialog (« Robot has an active order » / « Robot has completed a trade », which a fresh robot never needs) → « Order description » with « Go back » / « Continue », when the order has a description → « Store your garage key » with « Go back » / « Done » → the take, at « Done », with no other confirmation; here « Take Order » makes the robot that will take the order read it, so the robot is revealed at « Take Order », before « Store your garage key », where the reference's robot exists before the page; the order details then show that read in place of the reference's dialog: the description as a row « Order description » (the dialog's title; « Description de l'ordre », the project's French, the reference leaving it in English), the read's figures in place of the book's, a row « Taker bond » (the bond's estimate and the most the app takes of its invoice, `TradeTerms.bondCeiling`, the very value « Pay with this wallet » accepts, which the reference shows on its trade page only, `TradeSummary.tsx:262`) and « Read by your robot at … » under them, with « Continue » (a filled button) and « Go back » (an outlined one) where the reference has two text buttons, its focus on « Go back » (dressing); this step shows even with no description (the fresh figures: the owner's decision), so a take of an order without a description is three gestures where the reference's is two; « The order changed. » over « Continue » from the start of the read made again when « Done » found the order changed, the read seen kept shown meanwhile, then the new read with its changed rows marked in colour and bold, as the reference marks the time left, never confirmed by itself; an order found gone keeps « Take Order » disabled; the wait of each step says what it does under its bar (« Checking the order with {coordinator} », « Building your robot! », the reference's words for a robot made, « Your robot is reading the order », then « Your robot is reading the order again » and « Taking the order with {coordinator} »), where the reference shows none, its robot existing before the take (the owner's request of 2026-11-14, who found the wait long and silent), and so does the trade screen before its first read (« Your robot is reading the order from {coordinator} », then « Still waiting for … »), where the reference's trade page opens on an order already read, with above it the alerts of the status it waits on (`TradeWaiting.warnings`: a read refused as a whole for terms changed, on a screen opened anew, #211) in the words they have under a contract and, for an order made here, « To follow this order safely, open it in the official RoboSats app… », and under it what an action of the chat step gave meanwhile (« Nothing was sent: the order read just now carries the alert shown above. » for a confirmation pressed while the read is refused, security review of #219, C2; « Nothing was sent. » alone when that read carries no alert, as an order the coordinator says cancelled, R5); after a restart, a trail marked breached or a contract seen late says so on that wait before the first read (`TradeStatus.beforeFirstRead`); the texts of #119 that are not the reference's: the bond's value, « Read by your robot at … », « The order changed. », « New value: … » said of a changed row, the lines of the steps but « Building your robot! », and the trade screen's line before its first read; « See Compatible Wallets » shows its link for copy and the QR code opens no wallet (privacy, and the feature cannot open a link); the steps from 9 on (15 aside, #91) replaced by a line (a later version); a line for the states of the watch the reference has no screen for (#87); several active trades listed, where the reference follows one robot; no stepper (the reference shows it on a wide screen only); a comma accepted as the decimal separator of the amount, so « 1,000 » is read as one, not a thousand (the estimate under the field shows the figure read, and the take checks the range again); « lose » for the reference's « loose »; while a take runs, a bar under « Take Order » and, after 30 s, « Still waiting for {coordinator}. Tor can take a minute. » (the reference shows its button loading only; a take over Tor can take a minute); the take's failures the reference has no text for said in words of the app (`TakeProblemL10n`, each marked « the project's own » in the feature's English translations: « Could not reach {coordinator}. The order was not taken. », « This order changed since it was shown. It was not taken: open it again from the book. », « {coordinator} asks for a taker fee above 1 %. The order was not taken. », « The terms of this order could not be checked. It was not taken. », « {reason} The order was not taken. » after the text of what failed, « Bull Market could not write its records on this device. The order was not taken. », « The order was not taken. », « The robots of a pasted Garage Key cannot take orders here. Generate a new Garage Key in the Garage. », « The robot chosen for this take already existed on {coordinator}. The order was not taken: try again. »), a coordinator's error code keeping the reference's message for that code; the QR code, the copy and the wallet's slot hidden once the screen's clock passes the invoice's `payableUntil`, with « This invoice has expired. » (the reference leaves the QR code); « Cancel » with no dialog for a status kept from no order, saying « Wait until the order is read again before cancelling. »; « This order is not available » when the book no longer lists an order public again, and « Coordinator offline » with « Retry » when the book could not be read; the contract's title in the « Order » tab while no order has been read; « Order » shown when the records cannot be read, with a sheet that says so (the reference has nothing to read); « Pay with another wallet » a section that opens and closes, open at first while the wallet's slot is empty; « See Compatible Wallets » in that section, under « You are BUYING BTC », where the reference puts it above (the owner's request: it belongs to the other wallets); « Copy URL » and « Cancel » stacked, where the reference puts them side by side, since both do not fit side by side at three times the text size on a phone of 320 points; `TradeButton` a copy of `BullButton.big` whose label wraps, until `bull_ui` gains such a variant (#124); « Generate Garage Key » greyed in « You do not have a Garage Key » reached from « Take Order », where the Garage can make one (#125).
- **The payout panel (#91): where the buyer is paid.** `lib/src/ui/trade/payout_panel.dart` (`PayoutPanel`, and `PayoutTold`, the listener the route puts around the trade screen), cubit `PayoutCubit` (`lib/src/presentation/payout_cubit.dart`, one per shown trade, made by the route (`P2pRouter.tradePage`) and closed with it, the only caller of `SubmitPayoutUsecase` and `HoldTradeKeyUsecase`, which the locator gives it as their members, with the app's clock), texts `lib/src/presentation/payout_l10n.dart`. The trade screen draws it under the payout prompt of 6 and 8 and as the prompt of 15 for the buyer (`TradePrompt.routingFailed`), through `TradeScreen(payout:)`, a `PayoutPanelBuilder` given a `PayoutPanelRequest` (the record, the `TradeSeen` shown, the coordinator's name, the screen's clock, whether a cancel runs), keyed, as the screen's contract is, so that a line added above it keeps it; with none (a feature without robots) the prompt shows no form.
  What the user sees, the official client's (`TradeBox/Prompts/Payout.tsx`, `Forms/LightningPayout.tsx`, `Forms/OnchainPayout.tsx`, `Prompts/RoutingFailed.tsx`, commit ff1998c), step by step: under « Before letting you send … » (#88), the tabs « Lightning » and « Onchain » (`BullSegmented`), « Lightning » first, « Onchain » disabled when the coordinator offers no swap and for an order in bitcoin; « Lightning »: « Advanced options » (`BullSwitch`, named by its text for a screen reader) opening « Routing Budget » with its unit PPM / Sats (typed as the reference takes it: an empty field is 0, a value at 99,999 ppm or more is refused and the field keeps the budget applied; « Too high!
