(That is more than 1%) » in red under it above 10,000; the default budget back when the options are closed), « Submit invoice for {{amountSats}} Sats » (`PayoutAsk.invoiceSatsFor` of the budget) with a copy of the amount, « Payout Lightning Invoice » with « Paste » beside its label (the clipboard read once on the tap, the cursor put at the start of what was pasted as the reference does, the reference's `lightning:` prefix taken off the field itself), the check of what is typed or pasted at each change (`SubmitPayoutUsecase.check`): its refusal under the field, or what « Submit » gives (amount, expiry, network, the payout's warnings), « Submit » (`TradeButton`), what it waits for and gave right under it, and « See Compatible Wallets » (its link shown for copy, as in the bond panel); « Onchain »: « RoboSats coordinator will do a swap and send the Sats to your onchain address. », « Swap fee », « Mining fee » and « Final amount you will receive » (the checked address's own figures once there is one, `CheckedPayoutAddress.swapFeeSats`, `miningFeeSats`, `sentSats`; before, the reference's preview from the form's rate), « Bitcoin Address » with « Paste », « Mining Fee » (the coordinator's suggestion as it sent it, taken once, when the on-chain payout is first offered, and not changed by later reads, as the reference takes it when its form is first shown, `Forms/OnchainPayout.tsx:47-49`; then what the user types; « Invalid » outside 2 to 500 sat/vB; a rate the coordinator later refuses is said as its refusal), the address checked and its network, « Submit ». At 15: while the coordinator retries, « Failure reason: » and the reason, the reference's text and « Next attempt in » with its countdown (« Retrying! » once due); once the invoice expired, the reference's « Your invoice has expired … Submit a new invoice. », the reason, and the Lightning tab alone, with the invoice kept in the form checked again (the coordinator refuses the same invoice, 3002, as with the reference, which keeps its forms above its prompts). A status not stored says « Wait until the order is read again to give your payout info. » and offers no form.
  The form lives in the cubit (`PayoutForm`: the tab, the invoice, the advanced options, the budget, its unit and its field's text, the address, the rate's field), as the reference keeps its forms in `TradeBox` above the prompts: the panel shows it and changes it through the cubit (`selectKind`, `editInvoice`, `setAdvanced`, `editBudget`, `switchBudgetUnit`, `editAddress`, `editFee`), each change checked at once; a panel built again (the « Order » tab and back, a line added above it, a return to the app) shows exactly what is checked (review of #132). « Submit » is on only for a check that is exactly what the fields show (`PayoutPanelState.submittable`: the same text, budget and rate), and gives that `CheckedPayout` to `PayoutCubit.submit(kind, shown:)`, the very one the button's frame was drawn with, sent only if it is still the one offered (a status that changes a figure between the frame and the tap sends nothing, and the panel shows the new figure: second security review of #132), which gives it as it is to `SubmitPayoutUsecase.execute`: one at a time, never again by itself. While it runs, the form cannot change, a status shown changes nothing the panel shows (it is applied once the payout told something), the button turns and each step says what it waits for, from the use case's `onProgress` (`PayoutProgress`: opening the trade from its records; another action of the order to end; reading the robot and unlocking its key, a few seconds the first time; reading the order; sending; reading again), with a bar named by the step and, after 30 s, « Still waiting for {coordinator}. Tor can take a minute. » (the take's own line); then what it gave: received (under « Submit »; the trade's watch is asked to read now, whichever tab is shown, and the status moves on), refused by the coordinator (in red under the field, as the reference's helper text, with its own message from the feature's translations: `api/errors.py`, `api/lightning/lnd.py` with the amount the coordinator expects, `api/utils.py`; « Submit » off for an invoice so refused until it or the budget changes, as the reference does; the refusal goes with the text it was about), not reached (« Your payout info did not reach … »), unknown (« Bull Market does not know yet whether … It keeps checking while this screen is open. », never sent again; a fault of `execute` ends there too, and goes on up), changed since shown (the form checked again against the fresh read, and what changed said: the amount, the invoice's expiry, the status), or nothing sent with its reason. What the panel offers about money is true at each moment: once a payout was received, or may have been, the panel keeps its form, read-only, and what it told, even when the read just after it (which shows the next status before the trade screen does) says nothing is asked any more (`PayoutPanelState.shownAsk`, review of #132); and « Submit » is off until a read sent after the outcome came back (`TradeSeen.requestedAt`, set by the watch and by the payout's own reads; a read with none, such as the cancel's, the bond payment's or a status restored from disk, is never after it) still shows the status asking for one, and a line then says so; the trade screen's « Cancel » is off while a payout is given (`TradeScreen(moneyActionRunning:)`, from the route, with the reason as its tooltip), and « Submit » is off while a cancel runs (`PayoutPanelRequest.cancelling`), as the payout and the take of an order run in turn in the process (`TradeTakeLocks`). The robot's key is held (`PayoutCubit.hold`) while the panel is shown with the app in the foreground and its tab of the shell visible, released when the panel goes, the app is hidden or paused, or another tab is shown, and when the cubit closes.
  The slot of the wallet's invoice and address (#90, the wallet's port): `PayoutPanel(wallet:)` takes a `PayoutWalletBuilder`, called at the top of each tab with a `PayoutWalletRequest` (the record, the `PayoutAsk`, the tab, `use`, which puts a text in the tab's field, where it is checked as a pasted one, the amount the Lightning tab asks with the form's budget, the field's current text, and whether the form may be edited); with `null` the slot draws nothing. The trade route gives `PayoutWalletSection.slotFor` (`ui/trade/payout_wallet_section.dart`, cubit `PayoutWalletCubit`, one per trade page; the use case one for the run) where the wallet's port was lent: on the Lightning tab « Generate Invoice », on the Onchain tab « Generate New Address », the wallet's own words for the same acts (its Receive screen; owner's decision of 2026-11-15). Each is one tap that asks `PayoutFromWalletUsecase` (`lib/src/domain/usecases/trade/payout_from_wallet_usecase.dart`, the only file that names `newLightningInvoice` and `newReceiveAddress`) and puts the answer in the field: the panel's own check, display (the invoice's amount, expiry, network, and the warning when it may expire before the coordinator pays) and « Submit » apply unchanged, and nothing is submitted by the button (the reference, with a WebLN wallet, makes the invoice and submits it by itself on the status change, `TradeBox/index.tsx:336-345`; one tap that also submits is the owner's decision, pending). While the field holds the invoice it put there, the slot says what Instant payments receives once the swap fees are taken, in the wallet's words (« Receive Amount: {amount} sats », « Swap fees: {fee} sats », « This fee will be deducted from the amount sent »). The invoice is asked for one payout key, the trade's, the same at 6, 8 and 15: `network:coordinatorAlias:orderId` (an order of a coordinator on a network has one open trade at a time). At 15 the panel shows the Lightning tab alone; the wallet makes a new invoice once its earlier order for the trade has ended, and while that order still waits it gives its invoice again, which the coordinator refuses (« You must submit a NEW invoice », `api/logics.py:878-880`; the payment hash is the primary key of its payments, `api/models/ln_payment.py:67-69`): the use case remembers, in memory only, the step (status and end) at which it first gave each invoice, and at 15 an invoice first given at another step is not put in the field (`PayoutWalletEarlierInvoiceOpen`) and the slot says « This wallet's earlier invoice for this trade is still open, and the coordinator takes only a new one. Paste an invoice from another wallet, or try again once it has expired. »; after a restart the use case has forgotten, and the coordinator's own refusal says it when the user submits. An answer that comes once the user changed the field during the wait is dropped. The buttons are off while a payout is given, once it told something, and while a cancel of the trade runs. Each wait says what it waits for, with the bar, and « Still waiting for the wallet. » after 30 s; a refusal is said in the wallet's words (the bond panel's swap texts), « This wallet is already waiting for a payment of this amount. Paste an invoice from another wallet, or try again later. » (`P2pWalletAnotherInvoiceWaiting`: the waiting order ends at its deadline, which may be hours away), or « This wallet cannot create an invoice now. » / « This wallet cannot give an address now. ».
  Never printed or logged: the invoice, the address, what was signed; the values print their kind only (tested with the output capture). Not protected from capture: the payout is the user's own invoice or address, shown to the user who gave it, as the bond's invoice panel is not protected (design note § 4.3).
  Deviations from the reference, each with its reason: each wait of « Submit » said in words of the app, with the bar and « Still waiting » after 30 s (the owner's request: a wait that says nothing looks like a fault; the reference turns its button only); what « Submit » gives shown before it (for an invoice the amount, expiry and network read from it, for an address the address checked and its network, and the payout's warnings: security, design note § 4.4); the on-chain figures taken from the check once there is one (review of #132: never two figures for one thing); the check's refusals in words of the app where the reference shows the decoder's error text, and an invoice of no whole number of sats refused (#90); the reason of « Onchain » disabled said in a line (the coordinator's `swap_failure_reason` as a kind, which the reference does not show: design note § 2.2); a pasted text trimmed of the spaces and line breaks around it (the field shows what is checked and signed); the mining fee and the final amount shown only for a rate that may be sent (the reference computes them with at least 2 sat/vB), « Invalid » for a rate of more than 3 decimals (what is sent is what is shown, `MiningFeeRate`); « Submit » of « Onchain » off without a checked address (security: the reference disables it only for an invalid rate); « What the address receives » as the coordinator computes it (#90); a refusal of the coordinator under the field goes when the field changes (the reference keeps an address's until the next submit); the coordinator's `bad_request` refusals (1016, 1017, 1047, 1048) said in the panel, where the reference shows them at the top of the page; « Failure reason » hidden when the coordinator gives none (the reference shows the box empty); no Lnproxy (a third-party server, privacy); « See Compatible Wallets » shows its link for copy and opens nothing (privacy, as in the bond panel); « Copy the amount » as the copy button's spoken name (the reference's icon has none); the countdown of 15 in French « mm min ss s »; « Submit » not offered again after a payout received or that may have been until a later read still asks for one, « Cancel » off while a payout is given and « Submit » while a cancel runs (money: what the screen offers is true at each moment). In the wallet's look, without other reason: the tabs as `BullSegmented` without the reference's icons (Bolt, Link), « Advanced options » without its icon, the labels above the fields rather than floating in them with « Paste » beside the label, « Copied! » as a line under the amount rather than a tooltip, the PPM / Sats unit as a text button beside the field, no frame around the Lightning form.
- **The chat step's actions (#95, phase 2): « Confirm {{amount}} {{currencyCode}} sent ».** The use case `ChatStepActionUsecase` (`lib/src/domain/usecases/trade/chat_step_action_usecase.dart`), the only file that names `CoordinatorClient.confirmFiat` (`robot_bindings_architecture_test.dart`), sends the buyer's « fiat sent » from the status the user saw, in this order: that status offers it (`ChatStepAction.offeredAt`: marked stored, from an order read, the buyer at 9); one action of the order at a time (`TradeTakeLocks`, which the take, the cancel, the payout and the bond's payment share); the order read again and kept, and nothing sent unless that read is marked stored and still offers it (`ChatStepReadFailed`, `ChatStepStatusMoved`), or shows it recorded already (`ChatStepAction.doneAt`: an earlier request that landed late is `ChatStepDone`, nothing sent again); then the action sent once through the robot's places in `RobotRequestLimiter`, and never again; its answer kept, then a read after it kept: `ChatStepDone`, `ChatStepNotDone` (refused before the coordinator acted, or never sent) or `ChatStepOutcomeUnknown`; a read after that shows the action recorded is `ChatStepDone` whatever the answer said, so that no notice contradicts the status shown. « Fiat sent » recorded is a read that shows 10 (or later); a read that still shows 9 after an outcome unknown proves nothing (the request may still land), so the screen never says « not sent »: it says « Bull Market does not know yet whether your confirmation went through. It keeps checking. » and offers the button again only for a new tap of the user, which, if the first request landed meanwhile, finds it recorded or lands as a second `confirm` at 10, which changes nothing at the reference coordinator. Each step is told (`ChatStepProgress`) and said on the screen in the payout's words (`p2pPayoutStep…`, « Sending your confirmation to {coordinator}… » for the request itself), with the bar named by the step and, after 30 s (`TradeCubit.stillWaitingAfter`), « Still waiting for {coordinator}. Tor can take a minute. », as the payout and the take (the owner's rule: every wait says what it waits for). A notice says what an action gave and goes once the watch shows the action recorded or another position than the one it was given at; the outcome unknown in the warning colour (no failure is said), red only where the confirmation was not recorded, in two wordings never used one for the other: « Your confirmation was not sent. » where the request never left (a refusal decided from the read, a busy transport, a coordinator off the network), « {coordinator} did not record your confirmation. » where it left and the coordinator refused it (1029, 1030, 1031, 1047, the empty 400, the authentication's); never « Nothing was sent. », which a buyer who just paid could read as « your payment did not leave ». The button and the dialog are worded from the trade's terms (`Chatting.amount`, `Chatting.currency`, what the user accepted at the take), never from a read; an order read with another amount or currency, or a range order read without its amount, keeps the button offered with the terms' figures and a line in the warning colour gives the coordinator's (« {coordinator} now gives … ; the trade was taken for … »). An `Error` of a use case leaves neither the action nor a cancel running (`TradeCubit.confirmFiatSent` and `cancel`): the outcome is said unknown and the error passed on.
  The screen: the cubit of the trade, `TradeCubit.confirmFiatSent(seen)`, is the only caller; one action of the trade runs at a time, a cancel or an action of the chat step (`TradeScreenState.actionRunning`), and a second one meanwhile does nothing; after the action the watch reads at once. `ChatStepPanel` (`lib/src/ui/trade/chat_step_panel.dart`) is the chat step at 9 and 10 (the seller's lines and « Confirm … received » since #96, below) (`TradeBox/Prompts/Chat.tsx`, commit ff1998c): « Say hi! Ask for payment details and click 'Confirm Sent' as soon as the payment is sent. » at 9, « Wait for the seller to confirm they have received the payment. » at 10, « ⚠️ Beware scams », then the place of the chat (`chat`, `null` until the chat panel of #93 fills it: the chat panel shows only what the reference's chat component shows, and the step's lines are this panel's), then « Confirm {{amount}} {{currencyCode}} sent » at 9 (off, with « Wait until the order is read again. », while the status shown is not marked stored or comes from no order read), which opens `ConfirmFiatSentDialog` (`TradeBox/Dialogs/ConfirmFiatSent.tsx`: the reference's title, its three texts, « ⚠️ This action cannot be undone! » in the warning colour, « Go back » emphasized and « Confirm »), and sends for the status seen when the button was pressed. While it runs the button is busy and a line says each step; another action of the trade running turns it off with « Waiting for another action on this order to end… ». At 13 the buyer sees « Attempting Lightning Payment » with `Prompts/SendingSats.tsx`'s texts and a spinner; at 14 « Trade finished! » between the reference's two bolts, with the end of a successful trade below (fifth pull request, next paragraph).
  « Payment failed? » and the collaborative cancel (#95, third pull request). `ChatStepActionUsecase.undoFiatSent` sends `undo_confirm` (`CoordinatorClient.undoConfirmFiat`, named in that file only) with the same steps and rules as « fiat sent »: offered only to the buyer at 10 from a status marked stored (`ChatStepAction.undoFiatSent.offeredAt`), exactly where the coordinator takes it (1030 if not the buyer, 1031 if not at 10; the backend sets no time limit); a read that shows 9 again is done, nothing sent again. On the screen, « Payment failed? » at 10, the reference's small grey text button (`size='small'`, `text.secondary`), opens `ConfirmUndoFiatSentDialog` (`TradeBox/Dialogs/ConfirmUndoFiatSent.tsx`): « Revert the confirmation of fiat sent? », the reference's « READ. … » in full, « Go back » and « Wait ({{time}}) », then « Confirm »: the reference's counter, kept by the panel for its page as the reference's dialog stays mounted with its page, starts at 60 seconds, runs only while the dialog is open, is not set back when the dialog opens again, and is set to 300 after « Confirm », so that another « Payment failed? » on the same page waits five minutes; a screen reader hears « Wait 42 seconds » and « Confirm » announced as the counter ends. Its notices say « request » where « fiat sent »'s say « confirmation » (« The trade moved on: your request was not sent. », « {coordinator} did not record your request. »). The collaborative cancel is `CancelTradeUsecase` at 9 (the reference offers it from the chat only, and none at 8): guarded with `cancel_status` 9, so that an ask that lands after « fiat sent » is refused (1020, « The order moved on before the cancel: nothing was cancelled. »), never a cancel; the first ask raises this robot's flag, the second side's ends the order (12). « … » (« Order options », `Prompts/Chat.tsx:254-353`) sits left of the action button at 9 and 10, built once, with « Audit Chat » and « Export » first (#157, below), « Open Dispute » (below), and « Collaborative Cancel », off at 10 with « Orders can't be cancelled if fiat has been sent. »; it opens `ConfirmCollabCancelDialog` (`TradeBox/Dialogs/ConfirmCollabCancel.tsx`): « Collaborative cancel the order? », its text, « Your peer has asked for cancellation » when the peer asked, « Go back » and « Ask for Cancel », or « Accept Cancelation » when the peer asked, read live from the trade's cubit while the dialog is open (a peer's ask that arrives meanwhile turns the button before it can be pressed), so that the peer's ask is accepted only by the user's tap. `CancelTradeUsecase` takes it only from a status marked stored at 9 before « fiat sent », as the screen offers it; an ask whose answer was lost and whose read after shows this robot's flag, or the order cancelled, is done. Above the title, at 9, the reference's `CollabCancelAlert`: « You asked for a collaborative cancellation » while this robot's ask waits for the peer, « {{nickname}} is asking for a collaborative cancel » when the peer asked. While an ask is sent, « … », « Confirm … sent » and « Payment failed? » are off, and a line says « Sending your request to {coordinator}… » with the bar and, after 30 s, « Still waiting for {coordinator}. Tor can take a minute. »; what it gave is said under the buttons, an ask whose answer was lost in the request's words (« Bull Market does not know yet whether your request went through. »), never as a cancel that may have happened. A notice of an action of the chat step or of a cancel goes once the status shows that very action recorded (the action's own `doneAt`; for the ask, its flag or the order cancelled) or another position than the one it was given at. « … » is one node for a screen reader, with its tap, read as the reference's `aria-label`, « open options », with no tooltip. `TradeButton`, `TradeDialogBody` and the quiet button wrap their labels and texts at word boundaries only (`WordFitText`): a word wider than its room, at three times the text size on a phone of 320 points, lowers that text's scale just enough to fit, rather than breaking letter by letter. Deviations, each with its reason: the reason of a disabled or enabled « Collaborative Cancel » (« Orders can't be cancelled if fiat has been sent. ») as a line under it, where the reference has a tooltip on a touch, which it shows at 9 as at 10; « Collaborative Cancel » outlined where the reference's is filled, since a filled button in the cancel's colour hides its own label in the wallet's look; `CollabCancelAlert` as a coloured line with an icon above the title where the reference has an alert box, and, for a status kept from no order (no nickname), the reference's « Your peer has asked for cancellation » instead. `TradeDialogBody.onGo` may be `null`, for the reference's wait.
  « Open Dispute » and the dispute's statuses (#95, fourth pull request). `ChatStepActionUsecase.startDispute` sends `dispute` (`CoordinatorClient.openDispute`, named in that file only) with the same steps and rules as « fiat sent »: offered to either side at 9 or 10 from a status marked stored (`ChatStepAction.dispute.offeredAt`), and never before `ChatStepAction.disputeOpensAt`, the chat's end (`expires_at`) less `CoordinatorClient.disputeWindow`, 18 hours (`Logics.open_dispute`, `api/logics.py:485-533`; the reference's prompt computes the same, `Prompts/Chat.tsx:100-104`): checked against the status seen before the order's lock and against the fresh read after it (`ChatStepTooEarly`, with what is left, nothing sent; refused by the fresh read, because a coordinator moved the chat's end later, which the reference coordinator never does at 9 or 10, it carries that read, which the screen shows with the refusal, as for a status that moved, so that the notice is there at once even if the next read fails, #160), and the coordinator's 1054 stays the judge (`DisputeTooEarly`); both are said in the reference coordinator's own words, « Cannot open a dispute yet. You need to wait until 18 hours before expiry. » (`api/errors.py:62`), with no countdown that would stand still; the panel stops showing the fresh read's refusal once the dispute's time has come by the status shown (a timer at that time, armed again at each build while that notice is shown and cancelled otherwise, and each new read, which may move it), when « Open Dispute » turns on; the coordinator's 1054, which came although this device's clock said the time had come, goes as the other notices go; the dispute's other notices name it (« … the dispute was not opened. », « {coordinator} did not open the dispute. », « Bull Market does not know yet whether the dispute was opened. It keeps checking: the dispute shows here once it is open. », ours); a read that shows 11, 16, 17 or 18 is done, nothing sent again. On the screen, « Open Dispute » sits in « Order options » between the chat's « Audit Chat » and « Export » and « Collaborative Cancel », in the warning colour, as deliberate as in the reference and never easier: off until its time, with the reference's « To open a dispute you need to wait {{hours}}h {{minutes}}m » under it, counting down each second on the screen's clock (`OrderOptionsDialog.disputeOpensAt`, `now`), so that it is never offered when the coordinator would refuse it; not drawn when the status shown does not offer it. That time is judged by this device's clock, as the reference's prompt judges it by the browser's: a clock ahead turns the button on too early, and the coordinator then refuses with its 1054, said as above; a clock behind by some time keeps the button off that much longer, and if it is behind by more than the 18 hours left, the button stays off until this device's clock reaches the time, which may be after the chat's end; at the chat's end the coordinator opens the dispute itself (`api/logics.py:460-464`), so the user's recourse is not lost. It opens `ConfirmDisputeDialog` (`TradeBox/Dialogs/ConfirmDispute.tsx`): « Do you want to open a dispute? », the reference's two texts (the staff examines the statements, the winner gets the escrow and the loser loses the bond; « Make sure to EXPORT the chat log. … », in the dialog's text colour as the reference's `DialogContentText`), « Disagree » first and filled, as the button that has the reference's focus (`autoFocus`), and « Agree and open dispute » outlined; while it is sent, « Opening the dispute at {coordinator}… » with the bar and the long wait's line. The dispute's statuses are read-only (`TradePrompt.disputeOpen`, `disputeWaitPeer`, `disputeWaitResolution`, `disputeWon`, `disputeLost`), each with the reference's texts word for word (`Prompts/DisputeWaitPeer.tsx`, `DisputeWaitResolution.tsx`, `DisputeWinner.tsx`, `DisputeLoser.tsx`, the coordinator's email in the texts of 17 and 18, its name when it gives none), the order's number at 11 and 16 (« Order ID: {id} », ours, for the save-information advice), and the coordinator's contacts as its dialog shows them (`CoordinatorContacts`). The dispute's statement (`Prompts/Dispute.tsx`'s form at 11) and the claim of a dispute won from the profile's rewards (17) come in a later phase: in their place, the project's lines say to contact the coordinator (« Bull Market cannot send a dispute statement yet. Contact {coordinator} through one of its contacts below, with the information to identify your order. », « Bull Market cannot claim rewards yet: contact {coordinator} through one of its contacts below. »); when the app's list of coordinators has no contact for the trade's coordinator (a coordinator not in the list, the status kept from no order), no contact is drawn and these lines say nothing of contacts below (« … Contact {coordinator} with the information to identify your order. », « … contact {coordinator}. », #160). Known gap of the reference coordinator, for phase 4 and the trial scripts (`GHSA-9349-m8rp-p7wv`, published on 2026-11-15 and marked patched in `v0.8.8-alpha`, but the code read at babf62e still has the race, #174): « Open Dispute » and the seller's « Confirm … received » are not locked against each other (`Logics.open_dispute`, `api/logics.py:486-509`, and `Logics.confirm_fiat`, `:1686-1735`, each end with `Order.update_status`, an unconditional save, `api/models/order.py:363-366`), so the last write wins. The likelier case is the buyer's: a buyer who opens a dispute while the seller confirms gets 11, which `disputeFollows` takes as done, and is shown « A dispute has been opened » with the bond « settled » (`TradeBondLine.settled`), while the buyer's payout may already be in flight and the bonds returned by the seller's confirmation; the order can then stay at 11 for ever, since `complete_order` moves an order to 14 only from 10, 13 or 15 (`transition_status`, `api/logics.py:1670-1683`): a dispute shown can hide a payout already made. On the seller's side, a confirmation answered at 11 is an outcome unknown, read again and shown as the dispute; on the buyer's, when the seller's confirmation is written last, the dispute answered at 13 is an outcome unknown, read again and shown as the payout. Nothing here asks the user to pay or send anything again; no code changes for it today.
  The end of a successful trade (#95, fifth pull request): `SuccessfulPanel` (`lib/src/ui/trade/successful_panel.dart`) is the prompt at 14, and the seller's at 13 and 15 (#96) (`TradeBox/Prompts/Successful.tsx`, commit ff1998c): « Rate your trade experience » and five stars, « Your TXID » or « Sending coins » with their copy, and `TradeSummaryCard`. A tap on a star sends `rate_platform` once (`ChatStepActionUsecase.rate`, `TradeCubit.rate`, `ChatStepAction.rate`), as the reference sends each change of its stars (`TradeBox/index.tsx:315-317`); the coordinator keeps the robot's latest rating whatever the status (`Logics.rate_platform`, `api/logics.py:1786-1789`). It is offered at 14 from an order read (and to the seller at 13 and 15 too, #96), not necessarily marked stored: a rating moves nothing, and an end the trail holds is never left nor written again (`TradeReader.advance`), so a later read of it is never marked stored. Its own flow, at an end: the trade opened and checked, the order's lock, the order read again just before (sent only if that read still shows 14, with it as the last read), sent once through the robot's places, nothing written and no read after (the order never shows a rating); every outcome carries the status shown. While it runs the stars are off with the request's waits (« Sending your request to {coordinator}… »); recorded, nothing is said, as the reference; refused, the request's words of the chat step; a lost answer is said as such, « Bull Market does not know whether your rating went through: the order does not show it. Tap a star to send it again. » (ours: no read can tell). The stars tapped are the panel's own state, as the reference's `Rating` keeps its own; nothing is stored. On chain (`OnchainPayout`, `api/views.py:511-526`): « Your TXID » with a button that copies the transaction id, which the reference neither writes nor links, and « Sending coins » with a spinner and a button that copies the address while the payment waits in the coordinator's queue; « Copied! » under the line. The « Trade Summary » (`TradeBox/TradeSummary.tsx`), shown when the order carries one: the toggle « Maker » / the coordinator / « Taker », this robot's side first; a side's rows are its role (« Buyer » / « Seller », « User role »), the fiat sent or received with the order's currency, the sats received or sent (« BTC received » / « BTC sent »), the trade fee (`{sats} Sats ({percent}%)`, the percent as `parseFloat((fraction * 100).toPrecision(3))`), for this robot's on-chain payout « Onchain swap fee » and « Mining fee », and the bond (« Maker bond » / « Taker bond ») with « Unlocked »; the coordinator's view gives « Coordinator trade revenue », « Routing budget » and « Contract exchange rate » (`pn(rate.toPrecision(7))`, its trailing zeros kept, then `{currencyCode}/BTC`). « Export trade summary » copies the reference's JSON (`onClickExport`, `utils/saveFile.ts` on a phone: `coordinator`, `order_id`, `currency`, `robot`, `role`, `maker`, `taker`, `platform`, two spaces of indent), written by `TradeSummaryExport` from what the app read, under the coordinator's keys and in its order. `CoordinatorOrder` reads both sides now (`TradeSummary.own`, `peer`, `TradeSideSummary`), `trade_revenue_sats`, the Lightning payout's `payment_hash` and `preimage` (64 hexadecimal characters, or « processing »), and the swap's `address` and `txid`, each bounded; an invalid side of the peer is dropped alone, an invalid own side or contract drops the summary, never the order.
  Deviations and choices of the end, each with its reason: « Rate your host » is built apart (sixth pull request, next paragraph). « Start Again » is not shown; « Renew » is, for an order made here, since #185 (below). The numbers with the separators of the user's language, as the rest of the feature; a figure the coordinator did not give is left out, where the reference writes its unit alone (« Routing budget » of an on-chain payout); the coordinator's view shows the coordinator's avatar, named by the coordinator for a screen reader, where the reference shows RoboSats' logo; no flag before the fiat and no badge on the role's icon; the export writes a date in UTC with milliseconds and leaves out `routing_fee_sats` (always 0, not read) and a `txid` the coordinator did not give; past 10 million per bitcoin the contract's price is written as a number rounded to 7 significant digits (`123,456,800`) where the reference writes `1.234568e+8`; « Sending coins » is shown for a queued payment and the summary only when the coordinator gave a valid one, as the reference means to, where its code as written never shows the first (`txid` starts as `''`, never `null`) and always shows the second, with zeros for what is missing; the stars are off with the project's waits while a rating is sent, and the order is read just before, where the reference's stars never wait; a rating not sent is said in the project's words (« Your rating was not sent. », « The order could not be read again from {coordinator}: your rating was not sent. »). A successful end opened again (a new screen, the app's return, a restart) is read once, as the reference reads an order whenever its page opens and its coordinator sums up a finished trade in every read at 13, 14 and 15 (`api/views.py:490-504`): `TradeWatchSession` emits the end from the records as before, then reads the order once through the robot's own places (never sent while an action of the trade runs, and a read the screen asks for meanwhile sends nothing), and `TradeReader.endShown` takes what it shows only when the trade stands at a successful end, the answer is not superseded (`TradeReadOrder.claim`) and the read still shows 14 with its order; the summary, the payment's lines and the stars then show. Nothing is written, the closed record is neither reopened nor changed, no cadence starts and no other read follows; a read that fails, an order the coordinator no longer knows, or one no longer at that end leave the end shown as before, with no notice. Other ends are not read. The summary's figures wrap at word boundaries only (`WordFitText`), a row's two figures are stacked from a text scale of 1.5, and the toggle is three tiles of the panel's own, so that nothing breaks letter by letter at three times the text size on 320 points. A side of the peer the coordinator gave invalid has no view, and « Export trade summary » is not offered without it.
  « Rate your host » (#95, sixth pull request): the reference rates the trade's coordinator on Nostr only (`Prompts/Successful.tsx:54-219`, commit ff1998c). `RateHostUsecase` (`lib/src/domain/usecases/trade/rate_host_usecase.dart`, the only file that names `CoordinatorClient.fetchReviewToken`, `robot_bindings_architecture_test.dart`) asks the coordinator once, through the robot's places, for the token of the robot's Nostr key (`POST /api/review/`, body `{"pubkey": …}`, the short header, as `Robot.loadReviewToken`, `models/Robot.model.ts:220-245`): the coordinator's BIP-340 signature of `<key><last order id>` (`ReviewView`, `api/views.py:1236-1269`), given only when the robot's last order is at 14, 17 or 18 and the key is the one it holds, otherwise 1052; the package keeps only a token of 128 lowercase hexadecimal characters (`HostReviewToken`), bounded at `CoordinatorReadBounds.reviewBytes`, so that nothing else reaches a public event. Each tap of a star then publishes one `OutgoingHostRating`, signed apart (`RobotKeyComputePort.composeHostRating`): kind 31986, `created_at` the second rounded down, empty content, tags `sig` (the token), `d` (`<shortAlias>:<orderId>`), `p` (the coordinator's Nostr key), `rating` (`String(stars / 5)` of JavaScript: `0.2` … `0.8`, `1`), signed by the robot's Nostr key, its JSON byte for byte what `finalizeEvent` of `nostr-tools` 2.25.2 gives for the reference's template, the signature aside (checked once, outside the repository); to the relays `nostrRelaysFor` gives, through the robot's Tor sessions, by the port of the chat's copies (`NostrRelayTransport`, which now takes a `NostrRelayPublication`). The kind is addressable (30000 to 39999): a relay keeps the robot's latest rating of the order; of two taps within the same second (the same `created_at`), it keeps the event of the lower id, not necessarily the last, exactly as with the reference. It is public: anyone reading the relays learns that this robot's Nostr key rated this coordinator for this order, with these stars; the reference publishes exactly that. On the screen, `HostRatingSection` (`lib/src/ui/trade/host_rating_section.dart`, over `HostRatingCubit`, one per shown trade, which the route provides with the builder `TradeScreen.hostRating`) sits under « Rate your trade experience »: « Rate your host » and the coordinator's name in bold, five stars, and after a tap the reference's thanks, « Thank you! {{shortAlias}} loves you too » with a red heart and « RoboSats gets better with more liquidity and users. Tell a bitcoiner friend about Robosats! » for five stars, « Thank you for using Robosats! » and « Let us know how the platform could improve (SimpleX / Matrix / Github) » otherwise (one translated sentence, its punctuation included, each name a link), whatever the publication gave, as in the reference. Deviations and choices, each with its reason: the reference's setting « api / nostr » does not exist here (the app always publishes the chat's copies), so the stars are offered as with its default, « nostr », and its tooltip « You need to enable nostr to rate your coordinator. » is never shown; the stars are off until the token is in hand, with « Asking {coordinator} for permission to publish your rating… », and stay off without it, with « Your rating cannot be published: {coordinator} could not be asked for permission, or refused it. Open this trade again to try again. » (ours: a refusal and a coordinator that could not be asked are not told apart), where the reference leaves them on and silently drops a tap made without a token; the token is not asked again until the screen is opened again, as the reference asks it again only when its prompt mounts again; each tap publishes once, the same star included (the reference publishes each change, and a second tap on its star clears it), and the publication's outcome is shown nowhere, as in the reference and as the chat's copies (decision D3); the two ratings one under the other for a phone's width; the links of the thanks shown for copy (`CoordinatorLinkSheet`), never opened, as everywhere in the P2P screens, under the names of the reference's code, since its translation puts « Telegram » on the SimpleX link and « Github » on the Matrix one (`en.json:821`). The coordinators' average ratings, which the reference reads from the same events (`Federation.loadRatings`, `RoboPool.subscribeRatings`, `utils/nostr.ts:131-149`) for its federation table and coordinator dialog, are not read here.
  Deviations and choices, each with its reason: « Beware scams » as text in the warning colour, without the reference's link to its documentation or its link colour (the app opens no link from the trade); the emoji the reference writes (« ⚠️ » in `Chat.tsx`, « ✅ » and « ⚠️ » in the keys of `ConfirmFiatSent`) kept, and left out of what a screen reader reads; the dialog closed on « Confirm », where the reference keeps it open with « Confirm » loading until the answer, since the page is where each step of the wait is said, as for the cancel; the amount with the separators of the user's language, where the reference's `pn` always writes « 1,234.5 », and an amount in bitcoin in sats, as the payout's prompt; the waits' lines and the notices of what an action gave, ours (the reference shows a spinner only); the French of the buyer's prompt at 9 (in the reference's code, with no entry in `en.json` nor `fr.json`) and of « Wait for the seller… » (left in English in `fr.json`), ours, declared in the translations' descriptions.
- **Taking an order as seller (#96): the escrow and « Confirm {{amount}} {{currencyCode}} received ».** A buy order is taken as a sell order is (the refusal of #87 is gone), its taker the seller, through the same two steps, robot and bond (status 3 says « You are SELLING BTC », or « SWAPPING OUT of Lightning » for an order in bitcoin, `LockInvoice.tsx:78-79`); the take's estimate says « You will send {{satoshis}} Sats (Approx) » (`TakeButton.tsx:289-292`), and the « Order » tab « You send via Lightning … » and « You receive via … » (`OrderDetails/index.tsx:237-265`, `TakerEstimate.ofTradeAsSeller` until the coordinator gives `trade_satoshis` or `escrow_satoshis`). The statuses follow `statusToContract` (`TradeBox/index.tsx:359-724`, commit ff1998c) for the seller: at 6 and 7 « Lock {{amountSats}} Sats as collateral » in the warning colour (`escrow_satoshis`), with `EscrowPanel` (`ui/trade/bond_panel.dart`, `Prompts/LockInvoice.tsx` with the concept `escrow`): « You risk losing your bond if you do not lock the collateral. Total time available is {{deposit_timer_hours}}h {{deposit_timer_minutes}}m. » from the escrow's time of the trade's terms, the QR code, the facts read from the invoice and « Copy to clipboard », shown as the bond's are (never for an `UnusableInvoice`, nor past `payableUntil`, nor from a status not marked stored; behind « Show anyway » for the amount checks), then the reference's line on a hold invoice for the escrow; at 8 « The trade collateral is locked! » and `Prompts/PayoutWait.tsx`'s two texts, no « Cancel »; at 9 and 10 « Chat with the buyer », the seller's lines of `Prompts/Chat.tsx` (« Say hi! Be helpful and concise. … » at 9, « The buyer has sent the fiat. Click 'Confirm Received' once you receive it. » at 10) and « Confirm {{amount}} {{currencyCode}} received », shown off at 9 as the reference's (`disabled={order.status < 10}`) and on at 10; at 13 and 15, where the buyer is still being paid, and at 14, « Trade finished! » with the stars and the summary, no bond line. « Cancel » at 6 and 7 is the reference's `ConfirmCancel` (the bond lost), as for the buyer; the collaborative cancel and the dispute are the chat's, unchanged. The escrow's checks are the state machine's of #86, unchanged: the invoice decoded on the trade's network, its amount `escrow_satoshis` (`InvoiceAmountMismatch`), `escrow_satoshis` equal to the seller's `trade_satoshis` (`EscrowAmountMismatch`), the fee it implies over the frozen `last_satoshis` between 0 and the announced taker fee plus 1 sat (`TakerFeeAboveAnnounced`), `last_satoshis` within 2 % of the terms (`TradePriceOutsideOrder`), expiry, the pinned payment hash and a payee it states; `WaitingForBoth` and `WaitingForEscrow` carry the terms' currency and escrow time for the panel, never a read's. The escrow is paid from another wallet, or since #97 from this one (« The escrow paid from this wallet (#97) » below).
  « Confirm … received »: `CoordinatorClient.releaseEscrow` of `packages/robosats` (a money member, named in `chat_step_action_usecase.dart` only) sends the reference's `{"action":"confirm"}` as the seller; nothing is sent unless the last read shows this robot as the seller at 10 with `is_fiat_sent`; done only at 13, 14 or 15 (`fiatReceivedFollows`): the reference answers `200` at 10 when it could not settle or check the escrow (`api/logics.py:1716-1735` falls through to `return True, None`), an outcome unknown; 1028 is `EscrowBelowPayout`, said as the coordinator's refusal. `ChatStepActionUsecase.confirmFiatReceived` runs it with the steps of « fiat sent » (`ChatStepAction.confirmFiatReceived.offeredAt`: marked stored, from an order read, the seller at 10 with `is_fiat_sent`; the order's lock; a fresh read that must still offer it, and a read showing 13 to 15 is done with nothing sent, `doneAt`; one send, never again by itself; a read after), `TradeCubit.confirmFiatReceived` is its only caller, and `ConfirmFiatReceivedDialog` (`Dialogs/ConfirmFiatReceived.tsx`: the title, « Confirming will finalize the trade. », « ⚠️ This action cannot be undone! », « The satoshis in the escrow will be released to the buyer: » and its three items, « Go back » emphasized and « Confirm ») is the only way to it; its notices are those of « fiat sent » (« confirmation »), but for an outcome unknown, which says what to do, since the reference coordinator answers `200` at 10 without settling the escrow and does not try again by itself: « Bull Market does not know yet whether your confirmation went through. It keeps reading the order; if the trade does not finish, confirm again: a second confirmation cannot release more than the one escrow. » (ours). After an outcome unknown the button is offered again for a new tap only, as for « fiat sent » and as the reference: a second `confirm` cannot release more than the one escrow nor pay the buyer twice (`Logics.pay_buyer` moves the buyer's single payout), and a fresh read that shows 13 to 15 sends nothing. No PIN: the dialog's « Confirm » is the only tap, as the reference's (an open question for the owner, design note § 7, decision 7). The stars of « Rate your trade experience » are offered where the reference shows them: 14 for both sides, 13 and 15 for the seller (`ChatStepAction.ratedAt`); « Rate your host » is shown with them, its stars off until the order shown is at 14: the coordinator gives its token only at 14, 17 or 18, so `HostRatingCubit` asks it only from a status at 14, and a screen opened at 13 asks it when 14 arrives, once (the reference asks at 13 and silently drops the taps). The order details' figure after « Take Order » (the robot's read, #119) is the seller's estimate for a buy order (`TakerEstimate.ofTradeAsSeller`), the figure of the book above it, as the reference counts the seller's sats before the take and after it.
  Deviations and choices, each with its reason: no « Pay with another wallet » section nor « See Compatible Wallets » around the escrow, which the reference shows for the bond only, and « Pay with this wallet » added by #97 (below); the hold-invoice line shown also while the coordinator has not given the escrow invoice yet, with no QR code (the reference draws a QR code of « Undefined… »); the title's figure the seller's `trade_satoshis` while `escrow_satoshis` is not given yet (the same `Logics.escrow_amount`, `api/views.py:362-365`), where the reference writes `pn(undefined)`; the French of the titles and of the lines `fr.json` misses (« Bloquez {amountSats} sats en garantie », the design note's; « La garantie de l'échange est bloquée ! », « Discutez avec l'acheteur », « Vous VENDEZ des BTC », « SWAP DEPUIS Lightning », the escrow's time warning and « L'acheteur a envoyé la monnaie fiat. … », the project's), each declared in the translations' descriptions; the dialog's three items as lines with a bullet the screen reader does not read; « Confirm » closes the dialog and the page says each step of the wait, as for « fiat sent ».
- **The bond paid from this wallet (#100, W4): « Pay with this wallet » in the trade screen, at status 3.** RoboSats has no such block: it is the owner's request (design note § 2.1), so the rest of status 3 stays the reference's and the block sits in the slot of « Pay with this wallet » above « Pay with another wallet » (`WalletPaymentSection`). Everything happens in the trade screen, through the wallet's port: the wallet's Send screen is never used.
  The use case (`lib/src/domain/usecases/trade/pay_bond_from_wallet_usecase.dart`, `PayBondFromWalletUsecase`), the only file that names `P2pWalletPort.payInvoice`, `watchPayment` and `findPayment` (`wallet_port_architecture_test.dart`): `watchBalance` (the Instant payments balance), `checkBond` (whether the wallet is offered for the status shown: a stored `WaitingForTakerBond` and `walletPaymentFor` with `accepted` the bond the take's terms imply, `TradeTerms.bondCeiling`, the order's bond at the book's price with the state machine's tolerance, 2 % + 1 sat, rounded down; the wallet pays the invoice's own decoded amount, never `bond_satoshis`), `quoteBond` (the swap service's quote, asked on the tap of « Pay with this wallet » only, never when the panel opens), `payBond` (on the tap of the confirmation only, one action of the order at a time: it runs inside `TradeTakeLocks`, which the take and the payout share, so that a take, a payout and a payment of one order never interleave and a second payment waits, reads the order again and finds the first; it reads the order again with the trade's robot through its places in `RobotRequestLimiter`, keeps the read as every read is, and pays only when the status now stored is the bond step and `walletPaymentFor` offers the very invoice quoted, same text and payment hash; then it writes the payment's start, and only once it is written calls `payInvoice`; the wallet's id of the payment is written as soon as the wallet gives one, a payment that already exists, a broadcast unknown and an interrupted payment included), `followBond` (the payment recorded for the trade: `findPayment` by its hash, then `watchPayment`; it pays nothing). Nothing retries a payment or pays by itself: a poll, a reopen or a follow never reaches `payBond`; a refusal that moved nothing may be followed by a new quote and a new confirmation, and the wallet pays an invoice again only while every earlier order for it is provably unspent (« Never twice » above).
  The records: table `trade_payments` of `p2p.sqlite` (schema 5, `StoredTradePayments`, `trade_payments.drift`, `TradePaymentRepositoryImpl`, its only writer), one row per trade and step (`bond`, and `escrow` since schema 7, #97): the invoice's payment hash (public: part of the invoice, and pinned already by the trail as `bond_hash`), when the last attempt started (a new attempt of the same invoice moves it forward and keeps the earlier attempt's id until the wallet gives a new one), and `wallet_payment_id`, the wallet's local id of the swap, and since schema 9 (#233) `confirmed_sats`, the total the user confirmed for it; never the invoice, a preimage, an address or a transaction id. Triggers: never deleted, written once per trade and step, only for an open trade whose trail is recorded, its trade, step and payment hash never changed, its start never moved back. A restart finds the payment by its hash (`findPayment`), whatever was written: killed before the start was written, nothing was asked of the wallet; between the start and the wallet's answer, the wallet's own records say where the payment stands (none, prepared, signed, broadcast unknown, broadcast).
  The screen (`lib/src/ui/trade/bond_payment_panel.dart`, cubit `BondPaymentCubit`, one per trade page, which the trade route provides only when the wallet's port was lent, `P2pLocator.paysBondFromWallet`; without it the trade screen is as before): the balance, « Pay with this wallet », « Getting the swap fees… », the confirmation (« Pay {amount} sats to lock your bond? », « Swap fees », the network fee, the total and what stays in Instant payments, the reference's sentence on a hold invoice word for word, « This is a hold invoice, it will freeze in your wallet. It will be charged only if you cancel or lose a dispute. » (the owner's decision of 2026-11-15, #135: shown in the confirmation, and in « Pay with another wallet » only while this wallet neither quotes, confirms, sends nor paid, so never twice), « Go back » / « Pay {total} sats » (« Go back », the reference's word for stepping back, so that it is never mistaken for the trade's « Cancel », #134)), « Sending the payment… », then where the payment stands (sent and waiting for the coordinator to see the bond locked, interrupted before it was sent, may have been sent, under review), a refusal with its reason (« Pay with another wallet » opens, `WalletPaymentSlot.othersOpen`, and « Pay with this wallet » stays offered while nothing left the wallet); after status 3, a line above the contract for a bond paid by this wallet (`BondPaymentLine`: refund pending, refunded, kept by the coordinator). No PIN, no biometrics and no consent gate: the confirmation's tap is the only one (design note § 3.7). A second tap while a quote or a payment runs does nothing; two screens of one trade pay once (the wallet runs payments one after the other, consumes a quote once and refuses a second payment of an invoice). The screen names neither the port nor its payment: the cubit takes the use case's functions.
  While this wallet sends the payment, and once it may have moved funds, « Pay with another wallet » closes and offers neither the QR code nor the copy of the same invoice (the facts and « See Compatible Wallets » stay), and the payment's line is brought into view; a refusal that moved nothing opens it. A screen opened again while this process pays (its earlier screen closed, the payment going on under the order's lock) says « Sending the payment… » (`BondPaymentsInFlight`, in memory) and follows the payment once it ends; only a preparation left by an earlier run, which the wallet pays again only if provably unspent, offers « Pay with this wallet » again. From the quote to the end of the payment no event of the wallet's records moves the panel (the follow of an earlier payment stops at the tap), and a payment answered sent stays sent when the follow finds nothing yet; an `Error` of the payment is not caught (rule 11): the panel says it cannot tell where the payment stands and follows the records again. « Cancel » waits while the payment is sent (its button disabled, as the payment's own), « Pay with this wallet » waits while a cancel runs, and `CancelTradeUsecase` takes the order's lock too; once sent, « Cancel » stays offered and, at status 3 only and for a payment on its way (sent, held, not yet known, under review; not refunded nor settled), its dialog adds the project's line « A payment of this bond is on its way from this wallet. Cancelling now costs the swap fees, and the refund may take time. » to the reference's texts, `BondLossAcknowledged` and its rules unchanged. The order's lock is held by the fresh read through Tor (up to 132 s) and by `payInvoice` (up to several minutes with `PayTimeouts`): the screen says « Sending the payment… », then « Still sending. This can take a few minutes. ». `trade_payments`' `step IN ('bond')` was widened to `('bond', 'escrow')` by schema 7 (#97), the table rebuilt by hand, one statement at a time and each only when the file needs it, so that a step stopped after any of them runs again (its rows copied aside, the table dropped with its triggers, made again, its rows copied back once each before its triggers are made again, the copy dropped), with no transaction (the feature opens none) and without drift's `TableMigration`, which is experimental and needs an `// ignore`; `robot_bindings_architecture_test.dart` pins its statements, which name the payments' table and its copy only. `wallet_payment_id` is recorded, not read to decide: the payment is found again by its hash. A payment of the invoice made by the wallet's own screens (`P2pWalletPaymentExists` without a handle) is « This wallet already paid this invoice. » with nothing more offered when its funds may have left, « interrupted » with the other wallet otherwise. Past the invoice's end the wallet offers nothing more, but a payment it made or is making is still said. After 30 s of « Sending the payment… », « Still sending. This can take a few minutes. ». After status 3, while the bond is locked, the reference's own bond line says it and nothing of ours is added (#135); elsewhere a payment sent is « Your swap is in progress. ». At status 3 a refund means the swap service could not pay the invoice (the bond was never locked), said so, with the other wallet open. The refund, as the study of #100 observed it: the bond's sats come back to Instant payments, the swap fees and the network fee do not; the texts say it in the wallet's own words (« Swap will be refunded shortly. », « Swap has been refunded. ») and add « The swap fees are not refunded. ». The panel's texts name no swap service and explain no engine (#135): each uses the reference's words, or the wallet's for the same case (`transactionDetailLabelSwapFees`, `coreSwapsLnSend*`, the Send screen's limits, `mempoolErrorServerUnavailable`, `recoverbullErrorRateLimited`), or a short text of ours where neither has one, each source written in the ARB description.
  Deviations and choices, each with its reason: the block itself (the owner's request); the reference's hold-invoice sentence, word for word (the owner's decision of 2026-11-15, #135, which keeps « it will freeze in your wallet » although this wallet's sats leave Instant payments while the bond is locked), shown in the confirmation and in « Pay with another wallet » while this wallet neither quotes, confirms, sends nor paid, so never twice; the panel's other texts in the wallet's words where it has them (#135), in English the wallet's words, in French the wallet's sentence with the reference's word for the swap, « swap » (« SWAP VERS Lightning »; masculine, « le swap », as the wallet's « un swap », `payRequiresSwap`), because the wallet's French word, « échange », is the P2P screens' word for the trade (« Échange terminé ! ») and the wallet's bottom tab; the wallet's title-cased statuses (« Swap Failed », « Swap Expired ») in sentence case inside a line of prose; « Swap server is unavailable. » and « Too many requests. » are ours (which server it is, and the wallet's « Rate limited » being jargon); the refund texts give no amount (no figure of a refund is known before it arrives); texts the study did not list, each marked « not in the study's table » in the feature's translations (may have been sent, ask support with the funding possibly sent or sent, over the maximum, outside the limits, under a minimum the service does not give, busy, order moved, records not written, wallet unavailable, the coordinator not reached before the payment, still sending, the swap service could not pay at status 3, the payment's state unreadable); the confirmation inline in the panel rather than a dialog, so that a status that moves on replaces it at once.
- **The escrow paid from this wallet (#97): « Pay with this wallet » in the seller's escrow panel, at 6 and 7.** The bond's block (#100 W4, above), unchanged, for the escrow: the same use case, cubit and panel, the same checks and the same single confirmation; the study of the issue is the archive's `phase2/sequestre-portefeuille/note-de-travail.md`.
  The use case: a second instance of `PayBondFromWalletUsecase` with `step: InvoiceStep.escrow` (registered under the instance name `escrow`; the methods keep the bond's names): it pays only from a stored `WaitingForBoth` or `WaitingForEscrow` (a buyer's status holds no escrow invoice, so `walletPaymentFor` offers nothing there), within `TradeTerms.escrowCeiling`, the most the state machine lets an escrow be without a warning (the trade's sats at the book's price with `TradeTolerances.price` and `roundingSats`, plus the announced taker fee of them and `feeSats`, rounded down), and records its payment under the step `escrow` of `trade_payments`. `BondPaymentsInFlight` keys each payment by robot and step, so the bond's and the escrow's never show as each other's. The cubit is `EscrowPaymentCubit`, a subtype of `BondPaymentCubit` with no code of its own but its `step`, so that the trade page provides both (`P2pRouter.tradePage`).
  The screen: `EscrowPanel` takes the trade, the status shown and the trade screen's `WalletPaymentBuilder`, and places the slot under the reference's time warning, above the QR code; `WalletPaymentRequest` carries the step and the trade's currency. While this wallet quotes, confirms, sends or paid, the QR code, the copy and the reference's hold-invoice line are not shown (the facts stay); the reference has no « Pay with another wallet » section around the escrow, so none is drawn. The confirmation is the bond's with « Pay {amount} sats to lock the collateral? », the reference's escrow line word for word (« This is a hold invoice, it will freeze in your wallet. It will be released to the buyer once you confirm to have received the {{currencyCode}}. »), and above it one line of ours, informing, never stopping: « Until the trade ends, these sats are with the swap service, not in this wallet. If the trade is cancelled, the swap is refunded to Instant payments without the swap fees and the network fee, and the refund may take time. ». The texts that said « bond » have an escrow's twin (« collateral », « garantie »); « Cancel » at 6 and 7 waits while the escrow's payment is sent and, once it is on its way, its dialog adds « A payment of this collateral is on its way from this wallet. Cancelling does not stop it: if it reaches the coordinator, the coordinator returns it, then the swap service refunds it to Instant payments without the swap fees and the network fee. The refund may take time. » (the reference, read: a taker's cancel at 6 or 7 settles its bond and puts the order back in the book without cancelling the escrow's hold invoice, `api/logics.py` case 4.b, about l. 1108-1125, `publish_order` only detaching it, l. 1205-1222; the invoice follower keeps looking at it while it is `INVGEN`, `follow_invoices.py` l. 68-81, and returns at once a payment that locks it with no order, its « Weird! » branch, l. 271-283; only if that loop fails does the payment stay frozen until the HTLC's expiry, about 344 blocks; with this wallet the returned payment is the swap service's, which then refunds the wallet). After 6 and 7, `BondPaymentLine(escrow: true)` (`TradeScreen.walletEscrowLine`) says a refund, a review or a failure of the escrow's swap in every view but the first read's wait and the escrow's step itself, the bond's line likewise: a contract, a cancelled trade (`TradeMessage`) and an order back in the book (`TradeOrderOnly`), where an escrow returned is said; nothing while the reference's title says the collateral locked, and nothing once the escrow is charged (the trade's end or a dispute's start, which the reference's own texts say). The escrow's line is always prefixed « Collateral: » (« Garantie : »), and the bond's « Bond: » (« Caution : ») only while a payment of the escrow is followed too, so that two lines are never the same and a buyer sees the bond's line as before. While the panel looks for an earlier payment of the escrow (`BondPayLooking`), or cannot read its records, the QR code and the copy wait behind « Pay with another wallet » (`WalletPaymentSlot.othersBehindTap`), as the bond's section stays closed then: the whole trade is not to be paid twice by mistake. The cubit's `step` is the use case's (`PayBondFromWalletUsecase.step`, passed by the locator), and `BondPaymentPanel.slotFor` offers nothing for a request of another step.
  What the seller risks, from Bull's code (the study): the whole trade is paid to the swap service, custodially, and stays with it while the coordinator holds the hold invoice, from the lock until « Confirm … received », up to the rest of the escrow's step and the 24 hours of the chat (the escrow's CLTV is the taker bond's, `api/logics.py:1229-1249`); the service's order has no deadline after its funding (`confirmationDeadline` is the funding's), and what it does with a payment held for hours is not in the code. A returned escrow (collaborative cancel, the buyer's payout never given at 8, a payment that reaches the coordinator after the seller's cancel at 6 or 7) is refunded by the service to an Instant payments address, without its fees and the network fee and with no action of the app, after a delay the code does not give (32 hours once for a bond). A dispute settles the escrow at its opening (`api/logics.py:486-510`): the swap ends paid, and a seller who wins claims the escrow from the coordinator's rewards, as with any wallet. An order the wallet marks `expired` after its funding is no longer polled, so a later refund is seen only in the balance. The service's limits are not in the code (`ERR_ORD_LMT001` with its limit, said by the panel with its figure).
  Deviations and choices, each with its reason: the block itself (the owner's request, as for the bond); the QR code not shown while this wallet pays, where the bond hides it inside « Pay with another wallet » (the escrow has no such section in the reference); the line on the swap service's custody of the escrow, ours, since a whole trade is at stake and the reference's line says « it will freeze in your wallet »; the escrow's texts in the bond's words with « collateral », the reference's word for the escrow.
- **The take in two steps (#119): the order read by the trade's robot before the take.** A deviation from the reference, declared: the order details open from the public book with no robot (browsing reveals nothing to a coordinator), where RoboSats shows an order's page only to an identified robot (`/api/order/`); what the book lacks, the maker's free-text description above all, and figures fresher than its last refresh, is read at « Take Order », by the robot that will take (the owner's decision of 2026-11-14: privacy kept, no information lost).
  `TakeOrderUsecase.prepare(shown, {network, amount})` runs every step and check of `execute` before the take's request, in its order: no open trade of the order here, the book and `/api/info/` read again anonymously and checked against the page, the terms of that read built and checked (`TradeTerms.take`: the taker fee under its ceiling, sats known, the amount in the range; a buy order too since #96, its taker the seller), so that a take refused without a robot costs none; then the robot of the order's preparation, or a fresh one reserved and put on the coordinator; then `GET /api/order/` by that robot through `RobotRequestLimiter`, and gives a `PreparedTake` (`order`, the robot's read; `description`; `terms`, from that read by `TradeTerms.takeRead`; `amount`; `readAt`; `expectedBond`); nothing is written in the trade records and no take is sent. The robot's read must be of a public order without a password that it takes no part in (`TakeOrderGone` otherwise; an order reached by its URL has its own preparation since #245, `prepareUnlisted`, below) and agree with the book's read (type, currency, amount or range, payment methods, maker, bond size, escrow duration, sats of an order priced in sats, premium of an order priced against the market, sats at the current price within `TradeTolerances.price`) and with the page's sats within as much (`TakeOrderChanged` otherwise: a coordinator that answers the robot otherwise than the book is never believed on either side). An order priced in sats takes its premium from the read's `premium_now`, the very one the book gives (`api/views.py:289`, `:809-812`): its own `premium` is `null` when the official client made it. `confirm(prepared)` reads the order again with the same robot and sends the take, on `prepared.terms`, only while that read still agrees with the one the user saw (the same fields, sats within `TradeTolerances.price`, the same description: `TakeOrderChanged`, `TakeOrderGone`), the Garage Key still gives its robot (`TakePreparationGone` when it was deleted or replaced: such a trade could never be read again, its bond paid), and `prepared` is still its order's current preparation once those reads are over (an `abandon` made meanwhile wins: `TakePreparationGone`); then the steps 3 to 5 of `execute`, unchanged (pending take, terms and first trail written, take sent once, answer kept; the lock of an order, the trigger of one open trade per order and the rule « an invoice only from a status marked `stored` » hold as for `execute`); an `abandon` after that point does not stop the take. `abandon(prepared)` sends nothing, and acts only on its order's latest preparation (one replaced by a newer one changes nothing); `abandonOrder(shown, {network})` forgets whatever was prepared of the order, for a screen that leaves after a preparation that failed. A prepared take holds no robot: the robot and its reservation stay in `PreparedTakes` (a lazy singleton of the composition, in memory, reached by `take_order_usecase.dart` alone: `robot_bindings_architecture_test.dart`), one order at a time, which keeps the order's robot for every new preparation of the order (a second tap, a read that failed, figures that changed, another amount) until the take is confirmed or abandoned, the order is refused for good (`TakeOrderGone`, `TakeTermsRefused`, `TakeOrderAlreadyTraded`), or another order is prepared; then it stays bound to that coordinator, without a trade, never used again. So no more than one robot and its token are held, and only while its order's take is on the screen. `TakePreparationGone` for a prepared take confirmed already, abandoned, replaced by a newer preparation, prepared before a restart, or whose key changed. Both steps tell an optional `onStep` each `TakeStep` as it starts, for the screen to say it: `checkingOrder` (the anonymous book and fees), `creatingRobot` (only for a fresh robot: its reservation, its key pair, a Tor session of its own, its read back and first contact), `readingOrder`, then at the confirmation `readingOrderAgain` and `sendingTake`; a step is only what takes real time (requests over Tor, the key pair), never anything of the robot or its token. Every preparation that reserved a robot also put it on the coordinator: the coordinator keeps that robot, without an order, whatever comes next, as for any take not made. The read itself, of a public order by a robot that takes no part in it, leaves nothing at the coordinator but the robot's last activity: no take, no penalty (`api/views.py:226-299`, `robosats/middleware.py:95-96`, commit ff1998c). A robot kept for the order is used again only while the Garage Key still gives it (`robotOfSlot`, second review of #126): one of a key deleted or replaced since is forgotten, and a robot of the key now is reserved. `execute` stays, for the tests of the domain: since #119 no file of `lib/` calls it (`robot_bindings_architecture_test.dart`).
  Rules for the screen, in the reference's order (the owner's decision after the review of #133): « Take Order » (after the amount of a range, and « You do not have a Garage Key » without a key) calls `prepare` and shows a wait whose line says each step; the order details then show the read in the page the user knows: the maker's description when there is one (through `CoordinatorText`, as plain text, under « Order description », no link opened, nothing in it offered for copy or payment), the read's figures (price, premium, sats, the range and the amount chosen with its estimate at the read's rate, the bond estimate with its ceiling, the escrow duration, the order's expiry), the maker's activity as the read gives it, and the time of the read, read out when it arrives; « Continue » opens « Store your garage key », whose « Done » calls `confirm` (its wait says its steps, then the trade's screen opens, the read shown until then and « Take Order » never back meanwhile); « Go back » of « Store your garage key », or a tap outside it, only closes the dialog (`TakeButton.tsx:380-382`): the order as read stays, its take prepared, and a later « Done » sends the take of that read, read again by `confirm` as always; « Go back » of the order details, a system back or leaving the page call `abandon`, or `abandonOrder` when the last preparation failed and the screen holds no `PreparedTake`; on `TakeOrderChanged` it says « The order changed. » at once, keeps the read seen while it prepares again (the same robot), then marks the rows that changed (the taker fee of the fee row too) and waits for « Continue » and « Done » again; on `TakeOrderGone` it says the order is no longer available and keeps « Take Order » disabled; on `TakePreparationGone` it prepares again, and says « The order changed. » with the changed rows marked when the new read shows anything other than the read seen (`PreparedTake.showsTheSameAs`: payment methods, maker, description, price and premium, sats, expiry, escrow, amount, bond and taker fee); it never holds a robot or a reservation, never retries a confirmation by itself, and shows no invoice before the take's own `stored` status. Accessibility: the bar of a step is read as the button pressed, in progress (« Take Order » or « Continue »); the line of each step, « Still waiting for … », « The order changed. » and « Read by your robot at … » are live regions, read out when they change; « Order description » is the label of the maker's words. A row changed since the read seen (`OrderDetailRow.changed`, `OrderDescriptionRow.changed`) is said « New value: … » (« Nouvelle valeur : … »), not by its colour alone, and its label takes the warning colour too, which tells a changed expiry apart from one near its end, whose value only is coloured. Done by `TakeOrderCubit` (states `TakeIdle` with `orderGone`, `TakePreparing` with its `step` and the `previous` read kept, `TakeReady` with its `PreparedTake` and the `previous` read when it changed, `TakeConfirming`, `TakeSent` with the read it was sent on; the use case's steps given as `TakeCalls`), `TakeOrderSection`, `TakeOrderDetailsCard` and `TakeOrderMakerRow`: the cubit's `close`, which the page's route does, abandons a take prepared, or the order otherwise (a preparation that failed, or one that runs, whose result is abandoned when it comes), and lets a confirmation that runs finish, the user having confirmed it; the trade screen before its first read is `TradeFirstReadWait`.
- **Taking an order reached by its URL: a password-protected one, or one the book in memory does not hold (#245).** No book lists a protected order (`api/views.py:788-790`), only an authenticated robot can read an order (`:232-233`), and the book in memory is read again only on the user's gesture, so a public order made after it was read is not in it either: « Search order » with the URL of an order the books received do not hold, a Garage Key stored, opens its page (`UnlistedOrderScreen`, route `/p2p/unlisted` with `P2pUnlistedOrderRouteArgs`, `UnlistedOrderCubit`), where the robot that would take it reads it at once (`TakeOrderUsecase.readUnlisted`: no open trade of the order here, else that trade opens, an order made here included; the robot kept for the order or a fresh one, `TakeStep.creatingRobot`; its read, `TakeStep.readingOrder`), as the reference's order page reads it with the user's robot (`basic/OrderPage/index.tsx:75-96`). The read must be of a public order that the robot takes no part in, with a password or without, read as the book would list it (`PublicOrder.ofRead`, `UnlistedOrder.ofRead`, `UnlistedOrder.hasPassword`); otherwise (taken, paused, cancelled, expired, unknown) « This order is not available », its robot forgotten, and « Retry » only where another read may give it (a coordinator not reached, a robot refused). So the robot is revealed when the page opens, not at « Take Order »: a deviation from #119 that the coordinator forces, the reference doing the same.
  The page is then the order's details (`OrderDetailsScreen` with `TakeOrderCubit.unlisted`), and, for a protected order only, one line that its description and place come with the take (hidden from a robot that takes no part, `:294-298`, `p2pTakeProtectedHidden`) and « Password » above « Take Order » as the reference puts it (`components/OrderDetails/index.tsx:546-571`), typed hidden, its text in the field only; a public order has no such field. « Take Order » prepares the take (`prepareUnlisted`): every step of `prepare`, the page's read standing in place of the book's (a coordinator caches its book for 10 s, `:85`, `:824`, so a book read just after the order was made may not list it): the taker fee read again anonymously, the terms checked (the fee's ceiling, sats known, the amount in the range), the robot kept, its read again, which must still have a password exactly when the page's had one (`TakeOrderGone`) and agree with the page's read as `prepare`'s agrees with the book's (`TakeOrderChanged`). « Continue », « Store your garage key », « Done »: `confirm(prepared, password:)` reads a third time, compares, and sends the take, with the password's hash for a protected order (`OrderPassword` holds only an `OrderPasswordHash`, and `CoordinatorClient.takeOrder` takes that hash, never a text), refused before anything without one (`TakePasswordMissing`); an order without a password never sends a `password` field, whatever is given. What protects a taker here is what protects any taker: the terms read again by its robot and compared with what it saw, the sats within `TradeTolerances.price`, the bond's ceiling of the terms, the trade's alerts; as for any taker, its read on the order page, its bond, its escrow and its payout are compared with the Bull wallet's price since #249 (« The owner's payment rule for an order taken here (#249) » below), the Bull wallet's price read at « Take Order », never when the page opens, though the robot reads the order then.
  « Password » empty: « Take Order » and « Continue » start nothing and say « Enter the order's password. » under the button (the reference would send the take and be refused, 1045). A wrong password: the coordinator's 1045, refused before it does anything (`api/views.py:558-564`), the take not made and its pending take closed, « Wrong password » (`api/errors.py:52`) under the button as the reference shows the coordinator's message there (`TakeButton.tsx:360-366`), the field kept to be corrected; the robot of that take is never used again (one robot per trade: `trades` is keyed by the robot), so the next « Take Order » reserves another. The password is never kept, logged or printed (`toString`s redacted), and the records keep nothing of it, nor whether the order had one: schema 9 unchanged. Once taken the robot is a participant, and the take's own answer gives the description and the place (`:248-252`, `:280`, `:320-321`). The « Order » tab of every trade shows them to the participant as the reference's order details do (`components/OrderDetails/index.tsx:409-433`, `TradeOrderDetails`): the description when it is not empty (`OrderDescriptionRow`, the cleaning and bounds of the order's page), the place for an order whose method holds « Cash F2F », as « F2F location » with the point as numbers and the reference's line that it is approximate (`:286-294`, `:411`), where the reference opens its map. A Garage Key that cannot be read stays « This order is not available »; a pasted key's page says that its robots take no order here.
